## File: IncomingTasksContext

**Import path:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/contexts/IncomingTasksContext.tsx`

**Full content:**

```typescript
import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useCallback, useMemo, useEffect } from 'react';

// Auto-cleanup stale incoming tasks after this many seconds
const STALE_TASK_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 60;

/**
 * Generic incoming task - represents a task that is being prepared/created
 * but hasn't yet appeared in the database. This allows for immediate UI feedback
 * while background operations (like AI prompt generation) complete.
 *
 * Designed to work with any task type, not just image generation.
 */
export interface IncomingTask {
  id: string;
  startedAt: Date;
  taskType: string;       // e.g., 'image_generation', 'travel_video', 'upscale', etc.
  label: string;          // Display text (e.g., "cinematic shot of...", "Travel video")
  expectedCount?: number; // Optional: expected number of tasks to create
  baselineCount?: number; // DB count when placeholder was added (for accurate count calculation)
}

interface IncomingTasksContextValue {
  /** List of all incoming tasks currently being prepared */
  incomingTasks: IncomingTask[];

  /** Add a new incoming task. Returns the generated ID for later removal. */
  addIncomingTask: (task: Omit<IncomingTask, 'id' | 'startedAt'>) => string;

  /** Remove an incoming task by ID (call when real tasks appear or on error) */
  removeIncomingTask: (id: string) => void;

  /**
   * Complete an incoming task: remove it and update remaining placeholders' baselines.
   * Call this after refetching queries to ensure a clean count swap.
   * @param id - The task to remove
   * @param newBaseline - The current DB count (remaining placeholders will use this as their new baseline)
   */
  completeIncomingTask: (id: string, newBaseline: number) => void;

  /** Quick check if there are any incoming tasks */
  hasIncomingTasks: boolean;
}

const IncomingTasksContext = createContext<IncomingTasksContextValue | null>(null);

let idCounter = 0;
const generateId = () => `incoming-${Date.now()}-${++idCounter}`;

export const IncomingTasksProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const [incomingTasks, setIncomingTasks] = useState<IncomingTask[]>([]);

  const addIncomingTask = useCallback((task: Omit<IncomingTask, 'id' | 'startedAt'>): string => {
    const id = generateId();
    const newTask: IncomingTask = {
      ...task,
      id,
      startedAt: new Date(),
    };

    console.log('[IncomingTasks] Adding incoming task:', newTask);
    setIncomingTasks(prev => [newTask, ...prev]);
    return id;
  }, []);

  const removeIncomingTask = useCallback((id: string) => {
    console.log('[IncomingTasks] Removing incoming task:', id);
    setIncomingTasks(prev => prev.filter(task => task.id !== id));
  }, []);

  const completeIncomingTask = useCallback((id: string, newBaseline: number) => {
    console.log('[IncomingTasks] Completing incoming task:', id, 'new baseline for remaining:', newBaseline);
    setIncomingTasks(prev =>
      prev
        .filter(task => task.id !== id)  // Remove completed task
        .map(task => ({ ...task, baselineCount: newBaseline }))  // Update remaining baselines
    );
  }, []);

  const hasIncomingTasks = incomingTasks.length > 0;

  // Auto-cleanup stale incoming tasks that have been sitting too long
  // This prevents stuck placeholders if completeIncomingTask is never called (e.g., due to errors)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (incomingTasks.length === 0) return;

    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      const now = new Date();
      setIncomingTasks(prev => {
        const staleTasks = prev.filter(task => {
          const ageSeconds = (now.getTime() - task.startedAt.getTime()) / 1000;
          return ageSeconds > STALE_TASK_TIMEOUT_SECONDS;
        });

        if (staleTasks.length > 0) {
          console.log('[IncomingTasks] Auto-removing stale tasks:', staleTasks.map(t => t.id));
          return prev.filter(task => {
            const ageSeconds = (now.getTime() - task.startedAt.getTime()) / 1000;
            return ageSeconds <= STALE_TASK_TIMEOUT_SECONDS;
          });
        }
        return prev;
      });
    }, 5000); // Check every 5 seconds

    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [incomingTasks.length]);

  const value = useMemo(() => ({
    incomingTasks,
    addIncomingTask,
    removeIncomingTask,
    completeIncomingTask,
    hasIncomingTasks,
  }), [incomingTasks, addIncomingTask, removeIncomingTask, completeIncomingTask, hasIncomingTasks]);

  return (
    <IncomingTasksContext.Provider value={value}>
      {children}
    </IncomingTasksContext.Provider>
  );
};

export const useIncomingTasks = (): IncomingTasksContextValue => {
  const context = useContext(IncomingTasksContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useIncomingTasks must be used within an IncomingTasksProvider');
  }
  return context;
};

// NOTE: Default export removed - was not used anywhere.
// The context is accessed via useIncomingTasks() hook.
```

## API Summary

**Hook:** `useIncomingTasks()`

**Methods:**
1. **`addIncomingTask(task)`** - Adds a new incoming task placeholder
   - Input: `{ taskType: string; label: string; expectedCount?: number; baselineCount?: number }`
   - Returns: `string` (task ID for later removal)

2. **`removeIncomingTask(id)`** - Removes an incoming task by ID
   - Input: `string` (task ID)

3. **`completeIncomingTask(id, newBaseline)`** - Removes completed task and updates remaining placeholders
   - Input: `id: string`, `newBaseline: number` (current DB count)
   - Purpose: Ensures accurate count calculation across multiple placeholders

4. **`incomingTasks`** - Array of current incoming tasks

5. **`hasIncomingTasks`** - Boolean flag for quick presence check

**Auto-cleanup:** Stale tasks are automatically removed after 60 seconds

Actually, looking more carefully, the `addIncomingTask` call is AFTER all the early returns (validation), so it will always have a value when the finally block runs. Wait, actually since the addIncomingTask is called before the try block, and the finally only runs if execution enters the try block... No. Let me reconsider.

The function structure is:
```
validation checks (early returns)
setIsGeneratingInpaint(true);  // <-- addIncomingTask should be right before this
try {
  ...
} catch {
  ...
} finally {
  setIsGeneratingInpaint(false);  // <-- removeIncomingTask here
}
```

So `addIncomingTask` is called before `try`, and `finally` always runs if we enter `try`. But if `addIncomingTask` itself throws (unlikely), we'd have a problem. The safest pattern:

```ts
    const incomingTaskType = taskType === 'inpaint' ? 'image_inpaint' : 'annotated_image_edit';
    const incomingTaskId = addIncomingTask({
      taskType: incomingTaskType,
      label: inpaintPrompt.trim() || 'Editing...',
    });

    setIsGeneratingInpaint(true);
    try {
      ...
    } catch (error) {
      handleError(error, { context: 'useTaskGeneration', toastTitle: config.taskCreationError });
    } finally {
      removeIncomingTask(incomingTaskId);
      setIsGeneratingInpaint(false);
    }
```

This is clean because addIncomingTask is just a setState call, it won't throw. Make these edits using the Edit tool. Read the file first to see its current state.

1→/**
     2→ * Handles task generation for inpainting and annotated edits.
     3→ * Exports mask from Konva, uploads to storage, and creates tasks.
     4→ */
     5→
     6→import { useCallback } from 'react';
     7→import { toast } from '@/shared/components/ui/sonner';
     8→import { handleError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandler';
     9→import type { GenerationRow } from '@/types/shots';
    10→import { uploadImageToStorage } from '@/shared/lib/imageUploader';
    11→import { createImageInpaintTask } from '@/shared/lib/tasks/imageInpaint';
    12→import { createAnnotatedImageEditTask } from '@/shared/lib/tasks/annotatedImageEdit';
    13→import type { StrokeOverlayHandle } from '../../components/StrokeOverlay';
    14→import type { BrushStroke, EditAdvancedSettings, QwenEditModel } from './types';
    15→
    16→// Import the converter function
    17→import { convertToHiresFixApiParams } from '../useGenerationEditSettings';
    18→import { getGenerationId, getMediaUrl } from '@/shared/lib/mediaTypeHelpers';
    19→import { useIncomingTasks } from '@/shared/contexts/IncomingTasksContext';
    20→
    21→/**
    22→ * Task type configuration - captures the differences between inpaint and annotate modes
    23→ */
    24→type TaskType = 'inpaint' | 'annotate';
    25→
    26→interface TaskTypeConfig {
    27→  logPrefix: string;
    28→  emptyStrokesError: string;
    29→  overlayNotReadyError: string;
    30→  fileNamePrefix: string;
    31→  taskCreationError: string;
    32→  createTask: typeof createImageInpaintTask | typeof createAnnotatedImageEditTask;
    33→}
    34→
    35→const TASK_CONFIGS: Record<TaskType, TaskTypeConfig> = {
    36→  inpaint: {
    37→    logPrefix: '[Inpaint]',
    38→    emptyStrokesError: 'Please paint on the image first',
    39→    overlayNotReadyError: 'Paint overlay not ready',
    40→    fileNamePrefix: 'inpaint_mask',
    41→    taskCreationError: 'Failed to create inpaint task',
    42→    createTask: createImageInpaintTask,
    43→  },
    44→  annotate: {
    45→    logPrefix: '[AnnotateEdit]',
    46→    emptyStrokesError: 'Please draw an annotation rectangle',
    47→    overlayNotReadyError: 'Annotation overlay not ready',
    48→    fileNamePrefix: 'annotated_edit_mask',
    49→    taskCreationError: 'Failed to create annotated edit task',
    50→    createTask: createAnnotatedImageEditTask,
    51→  },
    52→};
    53→
    54→interface UseTaskGenerationProps {
    55→  media: GenerationRow;
    56→  selectedProjectId: string | null;
    57→  shotId?: string;
    58→  toolTypeOverride?: string;
    59→  isVideo: boolean;
    60→  loras?: Array<{ url: string; strength: number }>;
    61→  activeVariantId?: string | null;
    62→  activeVariantLocation?: string | null;
    63→  createAsGeneration?: boolean;
    64→  advancedSettings?: EditAdvancedSettings;
    65→  qwenEditModel?: QwenEditModel;
    66→  // State
    67→  inpaintStrokes: BrushStroke[];
    68→  annotationStrokes: BrushStroke[];
    69→  inpaintPrompt: string;
    70→  inpaintNumGenerations: number;
    71→  // Refs
    72→  strokeOverlayRef: React.RefObject<StrokeOverlayHandle>;
    73→  // Callbacks
    74→  handleExitInpaintMode: () => void;
    75→  setIsGeneratingInpaint: (isGenerating: boolean) => void;
    76→  setInpaintGenerateSuccess: (success: boolean) => void;
    77→}
    78→
    79→export function useTaskGeneration({
    80→  media,
    81→  selectedProjectId,
    82→  shotId,
    83→  toolTypeOverride,
    84→  isVideo,
    85→  loras,
    86→  activeVariantId,
    87→  activeVariantLocation,
    88→  createAsGeneration,
    89→  advancedSettings,
    90→  qwenEditModel,
    91→  inpaintStrokes,
    92→  annotationStrokes,
    93→  inpaintPrompt,
    94→  inpaintNumGenerations,
    95→  strokeOverlayRef,
    96→  handleExitInpaintMode,
    97→  setIsGeneratingInpaint,
    98→  setInpaintGenerateSuccess,
    99→}: UseTaskGenerationProps) {
   100→  // Get actual generation ID (may differ from media.id for shot_generations)
   101→  const actualGenerationId = getGenerationId(media);
   102→
   103→  /**
   104→   * Unified task generation function
   105→   * Handles both inpaint and annotate modes with mode-specific configuration
   106→   */
   107→  const handleGenerateTask = useCallback(async (taskType: TaskType) => {
   108→    const config = TASK_CONFIGS[taskType];
   109→    const strokes = taskType === 'inpaint' ? inpaintStrokes : annotationStrokes;
   110→
   111→    console.log(`${config.logPrefix} handleGenerate called`, {
   112→      selectedProjectId: selectedProjectId?.substring(0, 8),
   113→      strokesLength: strokes.length,
   114→      hasStrokeOverlayRef: !!strokeOverlayRef.current,
   115→    });
   116→
   117→    // Validation
   118→    if (!selectedProjectId || isVideo) {
   119→      toast.error(`Cannot generate ${taskType === 'inpaint' ? 'inpaint' : 'annotated edit'}`);
   120→      return;
   121→    }
   122→
   123→    if (strokes.length === 0) {
   124→      toast.error(config.emptyStrokesError);
   125→      return;
   126→    }
   127→
   128→    if (!inpaintPrompt.trim()) {
   129→      toast.error('Please enter a prompt');
   130→      return;
   131→    }
   132→
   133→    if (!strokeOverlayRef.current) {
   134→      toast.error(config.overlayNotReadyError);
   135→      return;
   136→    }
   137→
   138→    setIsGeneratingInpaint(true);
   139→    try {
   140→      // Export mask directly from Konva
   141→      const maskImageData = strokeOverlayRef.current.exportMask({ pixelRatio: 1.5 });
   142→
   143→      if (!maskImageData) {
   144→        throw new Error('Failed to export mask from overlay');
   145→      }
   146→
   147→      console.log(`${config.logPrefix} Mask exported from Konva`);
   148→
   149→      // Upload mask to storage
   150→      const maskFile = await fetch(maskImageData)
   151→        .then(res => res.blob())
   152→        .then(blob => new File([blob], `${config.fileNamePrefix}_${media.id}_${Date.now()}.png`, { type: 'image/png' }));
   153→
   154→      const maskUrl = await uploadImageToStorage(maskFile);
   155→      console.log(`${config.logPrefix} Mask uploaded:`, maskUrl);
   156→
   157→      // Get source image URL
   158→      const mediaUrl = getMediaUrl(media) || media.imageUrl;
   159→      const sourceUrl = activeVariantLocation || mediaUrl;
   160→
   161→      console.log(`${config.logPrefix} Creating task`, {
   162→        generation_id: actualGenerationId.substring(0, 8),
   163→        prompt: inpaintPrompt.substring(0, 30),
   164→      });
   165→
   166→      await config.createTask({
   167→        project_id: selectedProjectId,
   168→        image_url: sourceUrl,
   169→        mask_url: maskUrl,
   170→        prompt: inpaintPrompt,
   171→        num_generations: inpaintNumGenerations,
   172→        generation_id: actualGenerationId,
   173→        shot_id: shotId,
   174→        tool_type: toolTypeOverride,
   175→        loras: loras,
   176→        create_as_generation: createAsGeneration,
   177→        source_variant_id: activeVariantId || undefined,
   178→        hires_fix: convertToHiresFixApiParams(advancedSettings),
   179→        qwen_edit_model: qwenEditModel,
   180→      });
   181→
   182→      console.log(`${config.logPrefix} Task created successfully`);
   183→
   184→      // Show success state
   185→      setInpaintGenerateSuccess(true);
   186→
   187→      // Wait 1 second to show success, then exit
   188→      setTimeout(() => {
   189→        setInpaintGenerateSuccess(false);
   190→        handleExitInpaintMode();
   191→      }, 1000);
   192→

    43→export function useRepositionTaskCreation({

    68→    }
    69→
    70→    if (!hasTransformChanges) {
    71→      toast.error('Please move, scale, or rotate the image first');
    72→      return;
    73→    }
    74→
    75→    setIsGeneratingReposition(true);
    76→
    77→    try {
    78→      console.log('[Reposition] Starting reposition generation...', {
    79→        mediaId: media.id,
    80→        prompt: inpaintPrompt,
    81→        numGenerations: inpaintNumGenerations,
    82→        transform
    83→      });
    84→
    85→      const transformedCanvas = await createTransformedCanvas();
    86→      const outputWidth = transformedCanvas.width;
    87→      const outputHeight = transformedCanvas.height;
    88→
    89→      // Generate mask from the transparent areas of the transformed image
    90→      const maskCanvas = generateMaskFromCanvas(transformedCanvas, {
    91→        alphaThreshold: 200,
    92→        dilationPixels: 3,
    93→      });
    94→
    95→      console.log('[Reposition] Generated transformed image and mask with dilated edges (alpha threshold: 200, dilation: 3px)');
    96→
    97→      // Create final image with green background to prevent anti-aliased edge artifacts
    98→      // The transparent canvas is needed for mask generation (alpha channel), but for the
    99→      // uploaded image we need a solid background so edge pixels blend properly
   100→      const finalImageCanvas = createCanvasWithBackground(transformedCanvas, '#00FF00');
   101→
   102→      // Convert canvases to blobs and upload
   103→      const transformedBlob = await new Promise<Blob>((resolve, reject) => {
   104→        finalImageCanvas.toBlob(blob => {
   105→          if (blob) resolve(blob);
   106→          else reject(new Error('Failed to create transformed image blob'));
   107→        }, 'image/png');
   108→      });
   109→
   110→      const maskBlob = await new Promise<Blob>((resolve, reject) => {
   111→        maskCanvas.toBlob(blob => {
   112→          if (blob) resolve(blob);
   113→          else reject(new Error('Failed to create mask blob'));
   114→        }, 'image/png');
   115→      });
   116→
   117→      // Upload transformed image and mask
   118→      const transformedFile = new File(
   119→        [transformedBlob],
   120→        `reposition_image_${media.id}_${Date.now()}.png`,
   121→        { type: 'image/png' }
   122→      );
   123→      const maskFile = new File(
   124→        [maskBlob],
   125→        `reposition_mask_${media.id}_${Date.now()}.png`,
   126→        { type: 'image/png' }
   127→      );
   128→
   129→      const [transformedUrl, maskUrl] = await Promise.all([
   130→        uploadImageToStorage(transformedFile),
   131→        uploadImageToStorage(maskFile)
   132→      ]);
   133→
   134→      console.log('[Reposition] Uploaded transformed image:', transformedUrl);
   135→      console.log('[Reposition] Uploaded mask:', maskUrl);
   136→
   137→      // Create inpaint task with transformed image and mask
   138→      const actualGenerationId = getGenerationId(media);
   139→
   140→      // Use a default prompt if none provided - describe filling in the edges
   141→      const effectivePrompt = inpaintPrompt.trim() || 'seamlessly extend and fill the edges matching the existing image style and content';
   142→
   143→      await createImageInpaintTask({
   144→        project_id: selectedProjectId,
   145→        image_url: transformedUrl, // Use the transformed image as base
   146→        mask_url: maskUrl,
   147→        prompt: effectivePrompt,
   148→        num_generations: inpaintNumGenerations,
   149→        generation_id: actualGenerationId,
   150→        shot_id: shotId,
   151→        tool_type: toolTypeOverride,
   152→        loras: loras,
   153→        create_as_generation: createAsGeneration, // If true, create a new generation instead of a variant
   154→        source_variant_id: activeVariantId || undefined, // Track which variant was the source if editing from a variant
   155→        hires_fix: convertToHiresFixApiParams(advancedSettings), // Pass hires fix settings if enabled
   156→        qwen_edit_model: qwenEditModel,
   157→      });
   158→
   159→      console.log('[Reposition] Reposition inpaint tasks created successfully');
   160→
   161→      // Show success state
   162→      setRepositionGenerateSuccess(true);
   163→
   164→      // Wait 1 second to show success, then clear success state (keep transform for further edits)
   165→      setTimeout(() => {
   166→        setRepositionGenerateSuccess(false);
   167→        // Don't reset transform - let user keep their positioning for further edits or regeneration
   168→      }, 1000);
   169→
   170→    } catch (error) {
   171→      handleError(error, { context: 'useRepositionTaskCreation', toastTitle: 'Failed to create reposition task' });
   172→    } finally {
   173→      setIsGeneratingReposition(false);
   174→    }
   175→  }, [
   176→    selectedProjectId,
   177→    imageDimensions,
   178→    hasTransformChanges,
   179→    media,
   180→    inpaintPrompt,
   181→    inpaintNumGenerations,
   182→    transform,
   183→    shotId,
   184→    toolTypeOverride,
   185→    loras,
   186→    createTransformedCanvas,
   187→    createAsGeneration,
   188→    advancedSettings,
   189→    activeVariantId,
   190→    qwenEditModel,
   191→  ]);
   192→
   193→  return {
   194→    isGeneratingReposition,
   195→    repositionGenerateSuccess,
   196→    handleGenerateReposition,
   197→  };
   198→}
   199→

    56→
    57→/**
    58→ * Hook for managing Img2Img mode state and generation
    59→ * Uses Z Image Turbo I2I task type with full LoRA selector support
    60→ * 
    61→ * Strength, enablePromptExpansion, and prompt are persisted via editSettingsPersistence
    62→ * Prompt is persisted per-generation (shared with other edit modes, never inherited)
    63→ */
    64→export const useImg2ImgMode = ({
    65→  media,
    66→  selectedProjectId,
    67→  isVideo,
    68→  sourceUrlForTasks,
    69→  toolTypeOverride,
    70→  createAsGeneration,
    71→  availableLoras = [],
    72→  // Persisted values

    74→  setImg2imgStrength,
    75→  enablePromptExpansion,
    76→  setEnablePromptExpansion,
    77→  // Prompt is persisted per-generation
    78→  img2imgPrompt,

    81→  // Number of generations (shared)
    82→  numGenerations,

    84→  activeVariantLocation,

    86→  activeVariantId,
    87→  // Shot association
    88→  shotId,

    90→  // Local state (not persisted)
    91→  const [isGeneratingImg2Img, setIsGeneratingImg2Img] = useState(false);
    92→  const [img2imgGenerateSuccess, setImg2imgGenerateSuccess] = useState(false);
    93→
    94→  // Derive the base generation prompt from the generation params (best-effort)
    95→  const baseGenerationPrompt = useMemo(() => {
    96→    const params = media.params;

    98→      (typeof params?.base_prompt === 'string' && params.base_prompt) ||
    99→      (typeof params?.prompt === 'string' && params.prompt) ||
   100→      '';
   101→    return raw.trim();
   102→  }, [media]);
   103→
   104→  // Default Img2Img prompt to the prompt that generated the base image (one-time per generation)
   105→  useEffect(() => {
   106→    if (img2imgPromptHasBeenSet) return;
   107→    if (img2imgPrompt && img2imgPrompt.trim().length > 0) return;
   108→    if (!baseGenerationPrompt) return;
   109→
   110→    console.log('[EDIT_DEBUG] 🖼️ Img2Img: defaulting prompt from base generation prompt');
   111→    setImg2imgPrompt(baseGenerationPrompt);
   112→  }, [img2imgPromptHasBeenSet, img2imgPrompt, baseGenerationPrompt, setImg2imgPrompt]);
   113→
   114→  // Use the shared LoRA manager hook
   115→  const loraManager = useLoraManager(availableLoras, {
   116→    projectId: selectedProjectId || undefined,
   117→    persistenceScope: 'none', // Don't persist for img2img - it's a quick tool
   118→    enableProjectPersistence: false,
   119→    disableAutoLoad: true,
   120→  });
   121→
   122→  // Track generation to prevent double-submits
   123→  const isSubmittingRef = useRef(false);
   124→
   125→  const handleGenerateImg2Img = useCallback(async () => {
   126→    // Validate inputs
   127→    if (!selectedProjectId) {
   128→      toast.error('No project selected');
   129→      return;
   130→    }
   131→
   132→    if (isVideo) {
   133→      toast.error('Img2Img is only available for images');
   134→      return;
   135→    }
   136→
   137→    if (!sourceUrlForTasks && !activeVariantLocation) {
   138→      toast.error('No source image URL available');
   139→      return;
   140→    }
   141→
   142→    // Prevent double-submits
   143→    if (isSubmittingRef.current) {
   144→      console.log('[Img2Img] Already submitting, ignoring');
   145→      return;
   146→    }
   147→
   148→    isSubmittingRef.current = true;
   149→    setIsGeneratingImg2Img(true);
   150→
   151→    try {
   152→      // Convert selected LoRAs to the format expected by the task
   153→      const loras: ZImageLoraConfig[] = loraManager.selectedLoras.map((lora: ActiveLora) => ({
   154→        path: lora.path,
   155→        scale: lora.strength,
   156→      }));
   157→
   158→      // Use active variant's location if editing a variant, otherwise use sourceUrlForTasks
   159→      const effectiveImageUrl = activeVariantLocation || sourceUrlForTasks;
   160→
   161→      console.log('[Img2Img] Starting generation...', {
   162→        mediaId: media.id,
   163→        prompt: img2imgPrompt,
   164→        strength: img2imgStrength,
   165→        enablePromptExpansion,
   166→        numGenerations,
   167→        loraCount: loras.length,
   168→        activeVariantLocation: activeVariantLocation?.substring(0, 50),
   169→        sourceUrlForTasks: sourceUrlForTasks?.substring(0, 50),
   170→        effectiveImageUrl: effectiveImageUrl?.substring(0, 50),
   171→        isUsingVariant: !!activeVariantLocation,
   172→      });
   173→
   174→      // Get actual generation ID (handle shot_generations case)
   175→      const actualGenerationId = getGenerationId(media);
   176→
   177→      // Create tasks based on numGenerations
   178→      await createBatchZImageTurboI2ITasks({
   179→        project_id: selectedProjectId,
   180→        image_url: effectiveImageUrl,
   181→        prompt: img2imgPrompt.trim() || undefined,
   182→        strength: img2imgStrength,
   183→        enable_prompt_expansion: enablePromptExpansion,
   184→        numImages: numGenerations,
   185→        loras: loras.length > 0 ? loras : undefined,
   186→        based_on: actualGenerationId,
   187→        source_variant_id: activeVariantId || undefined, // Track source variant if editing from a variant
   188→        create_as_generation: createAsGeneration,
   189→        tool_type: toolTypeOverride,
   190→        shot_id: shotId,
   191→      });
   192→
   193→      console.log('[Img2Img] ✅ Tasks created successfully');
   194→
   195→      // Show success state
   196→      setImg2imgGenerateSuccess(true);
   197→
   198→      // Reset success state after 2 seconds
   199→      setTimeout(() => {
   200→        setImg2imgGenerateSuccess(false);
   201→      }, 2000);
   202→
   203→    } catch (error) {
   204→      handleError(error, { context: 'useImg2ImgMode', toastTitle: 'Failed to create Img2Img tasks' });
   205→    } finally {
   206→      setIsGeneratingImg2Img(false);
   207→      isSubmittingRef.current = false;
   208→    }

1→/**
     2→ * Handles task generation for inpainting and annotated edits.
     3→ * Exports mask from Konva, uploads to storage, and creates tasks.
     4→ */
     5→
     6→import { useCallback } from 'react';
     7→import { toast } from '@/shared/components/ui/sonner';
     8→import { handleError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandler';
     9→import type { GenerationRow } from '@/types/shots';
    10→import { uploadImageToStorage } from '@/shared/lib/imageUploader';
    11→import { createImageInpaintTask } from '@/shared/lib/tasks/imageInpaint';
    12→import { createAnnotatedImageEditTask } from '@/shared/lib/tasks/annotatedImageEdit';
    13→import type { StrokeOverlayHandle } from '../../components/StrokeOverlay';
    14→import type { BrushStroke, EditAdvancedSettings, QwenEditModel } from './types';
    15→
    16→// Import the converter function
    17→import { convertToHiresFixApiParams } from '../useGenerationEditSettings';
    18→import { getGenerationId, getMediaUrl } from '@/shared/lib/mediaTypeHelpers';
    19→import { useIncomingTasks } from '@/shared/contexts/IncomingTasksContext';
    20→
    21→/**
    22→ * Task type configuration - captures the differences between inpaint and annotate modes
    23→ */
    24→type TaskType = 'inpaint' | 'annotate';
    25→
    26→interface TaskTypeConfig {
    27→  logPrefix: string;
    28→  emptyStrokesError: string;
    29→  overlayNotReadyError: string;
    30→  fileNamePrefix: string;
    31→  taskCreationError: string;
    32→  createTask: typeof createImageInpaintTask | typeof createAnnotatedImageEditTask;
    33→}
    34→
    35→const TASK_CONFIGS: Record<TaskType, TaskTypeConfig> = {
    36→  inpaint: {
    37→    logPrefix: '[Inpaint]',
    38→    emptyStrokesError: 'Please paint on the image first',
    39→    overlayNotReadyError: 'Paint overlay not ready',
    40→    fileNamePrefix: 'inpaint_mask',
    41→    taskCreationError: 'Failed to create inpaint task',
    42→    createTask: createImageInpaintTask,
    43→  },
    44→  annotate: {
    45→    logPrefix: '[AnnotateEdit]',
    46→    emptyStrokesError: 'Please draw an annotation rectangle',
    47→    overlayNotReadyError: 'Annotation overlay not ready',
    48→    fileNamePrefix: 'annotated_edit_mask',
    49→    taskCreationError: 'Failed to create annotated edit task',
    50→    createTask: createAnnotatedImageEditTask,
    51→  },
    52→};
    53→
    54→interface UseTaskGenerationProps {
    55→  media: GenerationRow;
    56→  selectedProjectId: string | null;
    57→  shotId?: string;
    58→  toolTypeOverride?: string;
    59→  isVideo: boolean;
    60→  loras?: Array<{ url: string; strength: number }>;
    61→  activeVariantId?: string | null;
    62→  activeVariantLocation?: string | null;
    63→  createAsGeneration?: boolean;
    64→  advancedSettings?: EditAdvancedSettings;
    65→  qwenEditModel?: QwenEditModel;
    66→  // State
    67→  inpaintStrokes: BrushStroke[];
    68→  annotationStrokes: BrushStroke[];
    69→  inpaintPrompt: string;
    70→  inpaintNumGenerations: number;
    71→  // Refs
    72→  strokeOverlayRef: React.RefObject<StrokeOverlayHandle>;
    73→  // Callbacks
    74→  handleExitInpaintMode: () => void;
    75→  setIsGeneratingInpaint: (isGenerating: boolean) => void;
    76→  setInpaintGenerateSuccess: (success: boolean) => void;
    77→}
    78→
    79→export function useTaskGeneration({
    80→  media,
    81→  selectedProjectId,
    82→  shotId,
    83→  toolTypeOverride,
    84→  isVideo,
    85→  loras,
    86→  activeVariantId,
    87→  activeVariantLocation,
    88→  createAsGeneration,
    89→  advancedSettings,
    90→  qwenEditModel,
    91→  inpaintStrokes,
    92→  annotationStrokes,
    93→  inpaintPrompt,
    94→  inpaintNumGenerations,
    95→  strokeOverlayRef,
    96→  handleExitInpaintMode,
    97→  setIsGeneratingInpaint,
    98→  setInpaintGenerateSuccess,
    99→}: UseTaskGenerationProps) {
   100→  // Get actual generation ID (may differ from media.id for shot_generations)
   101→  const actualGenerationId = getGenerationId(media);
   102→  const { addIncomingTask, removeIncomingTask } = useIncomingTasks();
   103→
   104→  /**
   105→   * Unified task generation function
   106→   * Handles both inpaint and annotate modes with mode-specific configuration
   107→   */
   108→  const handleGenerateTask = useCallback(async (taskType: TaskType) => {
   109→    const config = TASK_CONFIGS[taskType];
   110→    const strokes = taskType === 'inpaint' ? inpaintStrokes : annotationStrokes;
   111→
   112→    console.log(`${config.logPrefix} handleGenerate called`, {
   113→      selectedProjectId: selectedProjectId?.substring(0, 8),
   114→      strokesLength: strokes.length,
   115→      hasStrokeOverlayRef: !!strokeOverlayRef.current,
   116→    });
   117→
   118→    // Validation
   119→    if (!selectedProjectId || isVideo) {
   120→      toast.error(`Cannot generate ${taskType === 'inpaint' ? 'inpaint' : 'annotated edit'}`);
   121→      return;
   122→    }
   123→
   124→    if (strokes.length === 0) {
   125→      toast.error(config.emptyStrokesError);
   126→      return;
   127→    }
   128→
   129→    if (!inpaintPrompt.trim()) {
   130→      toast.error('Please enter a prompt');
   131→      return;
   132→    }
   133→
   134→    if (!strokeOverlayRef.current) {
   135→      toast.error(config.overlayNotReadyError);
   136→      return;
   137→    }
   138→
   139→    const incomingTaskType = taskType === 'inpaint' ? 'image_inpaint' : 'annotated_image_edit';
   140→    const incomingTaskId = addIncomingTask({
   141→      taskType: incomingTaskType,
   142→      label: inpaintPrompt.trim() || 'Editing...',
   143→    });
   144→
   145→    setIsGeneratingInpaint(true);
   146→    try {
   147→      // Export mask directly from Konva
   148→      const maskImageData = strokeOverlayRef.current.exportMask({ pixelRatio: 1.5 });
   149→
   150→      if (!maskImageData) {
   151→        throw new Error('Failed to export mask from overlay');
   152→      }
   153→
   154→      console.log(`${config.logPrefix} Mask exported from Konva`);
   155→
   156→      // Upload mask to storage
   157→      const maskFile = await fetch(maskImageData)
   158→        .then(res => res.blob())
   159→        .then(blob => new File([blob], `${config.fileNamePrefix}_${media.id}_${Date.now()}.png`, { type: 'image/png' }));
   160→
   161→      const maskUrl = await uploadImageToStorage(maskFile);
   162→      console.log(`${config.logPrefix} Mask uploaded:`, maskUrl);
   163→
   164→      // Get source image URL
   165→      const mediaUrl = getMediaUrl(media) || media.imageUrl;
   166→      const sourceUrl = activeVariantLocation || mediaUrl;
   167→
   168→      console.log(`${config.logPrefix} Creating task`, {
   169→        generation_id: actualGenerationId.substring(0, 8),
   170→        prompt: inpaintPrompt.substring(0, 30),
   171→      });
   172→
   173→      await config.createTask({
   174→        project_id: selectedProjectId,
   175→        image_url: sourceUrl,
   176→        mask_url: maskUrl,
   177→        prompt: inpaintPrompt,
   178→        num_generations: inpaintNumGenerations,
   179→        generation_id: actualGenerationId,
   180→        shot_id: shotId,
   181→        tool_type: toolTypeOverride,
   182→        loras: loras,
   183→        create_as_generation: createAsGeneration,
   184→        source_variant_id: activeVariantId || undefined,
   185→        hires_fix: convertToHiresFixApiParams(advancedSettings),
   186→        qwen_edit_model: qwenEditModel,
   187→      });
   188→
   189→      console.log(`${config.logPrefix} Task created successfully`);
   190→
   191→      // Show success state
   192→      setInpaintGenerateSuccess(true);
   193→
   194→      // Wait 1 second to show success, then exit
   195→      setTimeout(() => {
   196→        setInpaintGenerateSuccess(false);
   197→        handleExitInpaintMode();
   198→      }, 1000);
   199→
   200→    } catch (error) {
   201→      handleError(error, { context: 'useTaskGeneration', toastTitle: config.taskCreationError });
   202→    } finally {
   203→      removeIncomingTask(incomingTaskId);
   204→      setIsGeneratingInpaint(false);
   205→    }
   206→  }, [
   207→    selectedProjectId, isVideo, inpaintStrokes, annotationStrokes, inpaintPrompt, inpaintNumGenerations,
   208→    media, handleExitInpaintMode, shotId, toolTypeOverride, loras,
   209→    activeVariantLocation, activeVariantId, createAsGeneration, advancedSettings,
   210→    qwenEditModel, strokeOverlayRef, actualGenerationId,
   211→    setIsGeneratingInpaint, setInpaintGenerateSuccess,
   212→    addIncomingTask, removeIncomingTask
   213→  ]);
   214→
   215→  // Stable callbacks that preserve the original API
   216→  const handleGenerateInpaint = useCallback(() => {
   217→    return handleGenerateTask('inpaint');
   218→  }, [handleGenerateTask]);
   219→
   220→  const handleGenerateAnnotatedEdit = useCallback(() => {
   221→    return handleGenerateTask('annotate');
   222→  }, [handleGenerateTask]);
   223→
   224→  return {
   225→    handleGenerateInpaint,
   226→    handleGenerateAnnotatedEdit,
   227→  };
   228→}
   229→

1→import { useState, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
     2→import { handleError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandler';
     3→import { GenerationRow } from '@/types/shots';
     4→import { createImageUpscaleTask } from '@/shared/lib/tasks/imageUpscale';
     5→import { getGenerationId, getMediaUrl } from '@/shared/lib/mediaTypeHelpers';
     6→import type { ImageUpscaleSettings } from '../components/ImageUpscaleForm';
     7→import { useIncomingTasks } from '@/shared/contexts/IncomingTasksContext';
     8→
     9→export interface UseUpscaleProps {
    10→  media: GenerationRow | undefined;
    11→  selectedProjectId: string | null;
    12→  isVideo: boolean;
    13→  shotId?: string;
    14→}
    15→
    16→export interface UseUpscaleReturn {
    17→  isUpscaling: boolean;
    18→  upscaleSuccess: boolean;
    19→  handleUpscale: (settings: ImageUpscaleSettings) => Promise<void>;
    20→  /** Update the active variant info so handleUpscale uses the correct image.
    21→   *  Call this after variant data becomes available (e.g. from useSharedLightboxState). */
    22→  setActiveVariant: (location: string | null | undefined, id: string | null | undefined) => void;
    23→  // Kept for backwards compatibility with other components
    24→  showingUpscaled: boolean;
    25→  handleToggleUpscaled: () => void;
    26→  effectiveImageUrl: string;
    27→  sourceUrlForTasks: string;
    28→  isPendingUpscale: boolean;
    29→  hasUpscaledVersion: boolean;
    30→  upscaledUrl: string | null;
    31→}
    32→
    33→/**
    34→ * Hook for managing image upscaling functionality
    35→ *
    36→ * Simple pattern matching other edit tasks (inpaint, etc.):
    37→ * 1. Click button → isUpscaling = true
    38→ * 2. Task created → upscaleSuccess = true briefly
    39→ * 3. Reset after timeout
    40→ *
    41→ * Variant tracking: call setActiveVariant() after variant data is available
    42→ * so handleUpscale upscales the currently-viewed variant (not the base generation).
    43→ */
    44→export const useUpscale = ({
    45→  media,
    46→  selectedProjectId,
    47→  isVideo,
    48→  shotId,
    49→}: UseUpscaleProps): UseUpscaleReturn => {
    50→  const [isUpscaling, setIsUpscaling] = useState(false);
    51→  const [upscaleSuccess, setUpscaleSuccess] = useState(false);
    52→
    53→  // Get media URL
    54→  const mediaUrl = media ? (getMediaUrl(media) || media.imageUrl || '') : '';
    55→
    56→  // Refs for active variant info — set via setActiveVariant after variant data loads.
    57→  // Using refs so the handleUpscale callback always reads the latest values
    58→  // without needing them in useCallback deps (avoids hook-ordering issues).
    59→  const activeVariantLocationRef = useRef<string | null | undefined>(null);
    60→  const activeVariantIdRef = useRef<string | null | undefined>(null);
    61→
    62→  const setActiveVariant = useCallback((location: string | null | undefined, id: string | null | undefined) => {
    63→    activeVariantLocationRef.current = location;
    64→    activeVariantIdRef.current = id;
    65→  }, []);
    66→
    67→  const handleUpscale = useCallback(async (settings: ImageUpscaleSettings) => {
    68→    if (!media || !selectedProjectId || isVideo) {
    69→      return;
    70→    }
    71→
    72→    // Use active variant's location if viewing a variant, otherwise use base generation URL
    73→    const activeVariantLocation = activeVariantLocationRef.current;
    74→    const activeVariantId = activeVariantIdRef.current;
    75→    const effectiveImageUrl = activeVariantLocation || mediaUrl;
    76→
    77→    setIsUpscaling(true);
    78→    try {
    79→      if (!effectiveImageUrl) {
    80→        throw new Error('No image URL available');
    81→      }
    82→
    83→      const actualGenerationId = getGenerationId(media);
    84→
    85→      console.log('[ImageUpscale] Creating task:', {
    86→        actualGenerationId: actualGenerationId?.substring(0, 8),
    87→        effectiveImageUrl: effectiveImageUrl.substring(0, 50),
    88→        isUsingVariant: !!activeVariantLocation,
    89→        activeVariantId: activeVariantId?.substring(0, 8),
    90→        settings,
    91→      });
    92→
    93→      await createImageUpscaleTask({
    94→        project_id: selectedProjectId,
    95→        image_url: effectiveImageUrl,
    96→        generation_id: actualGenerationId,
    97→        source_variant_id: activeVariantId || undefined,
    98→        scale_factor: settings.scaleFactor,
    99→        noise_scale: settings.noiseScale,
   100→        shot_id: shotId,
   101→      });
   102→
   103→      console.log('[ImageUpscale] Task created successfully');
   104→
   105→      // Show success state briefly
   106→      setUpscaleSuccess(true);
   107→      setTimeout(() => {
   108→        setUpscaleSuccess(false);
   109→      }, 2000);
   110→
   111→    } catch (error) {
   112→      handleError(error, { context: 'useUpscale', toastTitle: 'Failed to create enhance task' });
   113→    } finally {
   114→      setIsUpscaling(false);
   115→    }
   116→  }, [media, selectedProjectId, isVideo, mediaUrl, shotId]);
   117→
   118→  return {
   119→    isUpscaling,
   120→    upscaleSuccess,
   121→    handleUpscale,
   122→    setActiveVariant,
   123→    // Backwards compatibility - these features removed but APIs kept
   124→    showingUpscaled: false,
   125→    handleToggleUpscaled: () => {},
   126→    effectiveImageUrl: mediaUrl,
   127→    sourceUrlForTasks: mediaUrl,
   128→    isPendingUpscale: false,
   129→    hasUpscaledVersion: false,
   130→    upscaledUrl: null,
   131→  };
   132→};
   133→

1→import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
     2→import {
     3→  createVideoEnhanceTask,
     4→  type VideoEnhanceTaskParams,
     5→} from '@/shared/lib/tasks/videoEnhance';
     6→import { handleError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandler';
     7→import type { VideoEnhanceSettings } from './useGenerationEditSettings';
     8→import { useIncomingTasks } from '@/shared/contexts/IncomingTasksContext';
     9→
    10→export interface UseVideoEnhanceProps {
    11→  projectId: string | undefined;
    12→  videoUrl: string | undefined;
    13→  shotId?: string;
    14→  /** Generation ID to create variant on (for based_on relationship) */
    15→  generationId?: string;
    16→  /** Active variant ID - track which variant was enhanced */
    17→  activeVariantId?: string | null;
    18→  /** Settings from persistence system */
    19→  settings: VideoEnhanceSettings;
    20→  /** Setter for updating settings */
    21→  updateSettings: (updates: Partial<VideoEnhanceSettings>) => void;
    22→}
    23→
    24→export interface UseVideoEnhanceReturn {
    25→  // Settings passed through for convenience
    26→  settings: VideoEnhanceSettings;
    27→  updateSetting: <K extends keyof VideoEnhanceSettings>(
    28→    key: K,
    29→    value: VideoEnhanceSettings[K]
    30→  ) => void;
    31→
    32→  // Validation
    33→  isValid: boolean;
    34→  canSubmit: boolean;
    35→
    36→  // Actions
    37→  handleGenerate: () => Promise<void>;
    38→
    39→  // Status
    40→  isGenerating: boolean;
    41→  generateSuccess: boolean;
    42→}
    43→
    44→/**
    45→ * Hook for managing video enhancement actions
    46→ *
    47→ * Settings are managed externally via the persistence system
    48→ * This hook handles validation and task creation
    49→ */
    50→export function useVideoEnhance({
    51→  projectId,
    52→  videoUrl,
    53→  shotId,
    54→  generationId,
    55→  activeVariantId,
    56→  settings,
    57→  updateSettings,
    58→}: UseVideoEnhanceProps): UseVideoEnhanceReturn {
    59→  const [isGenerating, setIsGenerating] = useState(false);
    60→  const [generateSuccess, setGenerateSuccess] = useState(false);
    61→
    62→  // Adapter to match the expected interface
    63→  const updateSetting = useCallback(<K extends keyof VideoEnhanceSettings>(
    64→    key: K,
    65→    value: VideoEnhanceSettings[K]
    66→  ) => {
    67→    updateSettings({ [key]: value });
    68→    // Reset success state when settings change
    69→    setGenerateSuccess(false);
    70→  }, [updateSettings]);
    71→
    72→  // Validation: at least one mode must be enabled
    73→  const isValid = settings.enableInterpolation || settings.enableUpscale;
    74→
    75→  // Can submit: valid settings + required data
    76→  const canSubmit = isValid && !!projectId && !!videoUrl && !isGenerating;
    77→
    78→  // Handle generate action
    79→  const handleGenerate = useCallback(async () => {
    80→    if (!canSubmit || !projectId || !videoUrl) {
    81→      console.warn('[useVideoEnhance] Cannot generate - missing required data');
    82→      return;
    83→    }
    84→
    85→    setIsGenerating(true);
    86→    setGenerateSuccess(false);
    87→
    88→    try {
    89→      const params: VideoEnhanceTaskParams = {
    90→        project_id: projectId,
    91→        video_url: videoUrl,
    92→        enable_interpolation: settings.enableInterpolation,
    93→        enable_upscale: settings.enableUpscale,
    94→        shot_id: shotId,
    95→        based_on: generationId,
    96→        source_variant_id: activeVariantId || undefined,
    97→      };
    98→
    99→      // Add interpolation params if enabled
   100→      if (settings.enableInterpolation) {
   101→        params.interpolation = {
   102→          num_frames: settings.numFrames,
   103→          use_calculated_fps: true, // Always maintain playback speed
   104→        };
   105→      }

1→import { useState, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
     2→import { handleError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandler';
     3→import { GenerationRow } from '@/types/shots';
     4→import { createImageUpscaleTask } from '@/shared/lib/tasks/imageUpscale';
     5→import { getGenerationId, getMediaUrl } from '@/shared/lib/mediaTypeHelpers';
     6→import type { ImageUpscaleSettings } from '../components/ImageUpscaleForm';
     7→import { useIncomingTasks } from '@/shared/contexts/IncomingTasksContext';
     8→
     9→export interface UseUpscaleProps {
    10→  media: GenerationRow | undefined;
    11→  selectedProjectId: string | null;
    12→  isVideo: boolean;
    13→  shotId?: string;
    14→}
    15→
    16→export interface UseUpscaleReturn {
    17→  isUpscaling: boolean;
    18→  upscaleSuccess: boolean;
    19→  handleUpscale: (settings: ImageUpscaleSettings) => Promise<void>;
    20→  /** Update the active variant info so handleUpscale uses the correct image.
    21→   *  Call this after variant data becomes available (e.g. from useSharedLightboxState). */
    22→  setActiveVariant: (location: string | null | undefined, id: string | null | undefined) => void;
    23→  // Kept for backwards compatibility with other components
    24→  showingUpscaled: boolean;
    25→  handleToggleUpscaled: () => void;
    26→  effectiveImageUrl: string;
    27→  sourceUrlForTasks: string;
    28→  isPendingUpscale: boolean;
    29→  hasUpscaledVersion: boolean;
    30→  upscaledUrl: string | null;
    31→}
    32→
    33→/**
    34→ * Hook for managing image upscaling functionality
    35→ *
    36→ * Simple pattern matching other edit tasks (inpaint, etc.):
    37→ * 1. Click button → isUpscaling = true
    38→ * 2. Task created → upscaleSuccess = true briefly
    39→ * 3. Reset after timeout
    40→ *
    41→ * Variant tracking: call setActiveVariant() after variant data is available
    42→ * so handleUpscale upscales the currently-viewed variant (not the base generation).
    43→ */
    44→export const useUpscale = ({
    45→  media,
    46→  selectedProjectId,
    47→  isVideo,
    48→  shotId,
    49→}: UseUpscaleProps): UseUpscaleReturn => {
    50→  const [isUpscaling, setIsUpscaling] = useState(false);
    51→  const [upscaleSuccess, setUpscaleSuccess] = useState(false);
    52→  const { addIncomingTask, removeIncomingTask } = useIncomingTasks();
    53→
    54→  // Get media URL
    55→  const mediaUrl = media ? (getMediaUrl(media) || media.imageUrl || '') : '';
    56→
    57→  // Refs for active variant info — set via setActiveVariant after variant data loads.
    58→  // Using refs so the handleUpscale callback always reads the latest values
    59→  // without needing them in useCallback deps (avoids hook-ordering issues).
    60→  const activeVariantLocationRef = useRef<string | null | undefined>(null);
    61→  const activeVariantIdRef = useRef<string | null | undefined>(null);
    62→
    63→  const setActiveVariant = useCallback((location: string | null | undefined, id: string | null | undefined) => {
    64→    activeVariantLocationRef.current = location;
    65→    activeVariantIdRef.current = id;
    66→  }, []);
    67→
    68→  const handleUpscale = useCallback(async (settings: ImageUpscaleSettings) => {
    69→    if (!media || !selectedProjectId || isVideo) {
    70→      return;
    71→    }
    72→
    73→    // Use active variant's location if viewing a variant, otherwise use base generation URL
    74→    const activeVariantLocation = activeVariantLocationRef.current;
    75→    const activeVariantId = activeVariantIdRef.current;
    76→    const effectiveImageUrl = activeVariantLocation || mediaUrl;
    77→
    78→    setIsUpscaling(true);
    79→    const incomingTaskId = addIncomingTask({
    80→      taskType: 'image-upscale',
    81→      label: `Upscale ${settings.scaleFactor}x`,
    82→    });
    83→    try {
    84→      if (!effectiveImageUrl) {
    85→        throw new Error('No image URL available');
    86→      }
    87→
    88→      const actualGenerationId = getGenerationId(media);
    89→
    90→      console.log('[ImageUpscale] Creating task:', {
    91→        actualGenerationId: actualGenerationId?.substring(0, 8),
    92→        effectiveImageUrl: effectiveImageUrl.substring(0, 50),
    93→        isUsingVariant: !!activeVariantLocation,
    94→        activeVariantId: activeVariantId?.substring(0, 8),
    95→        settings,
    96→      });
    97→
    98→      await createImageUpscaleTask({
    99→        project_id: selectedProjectId,
   100→        image_url: effectiveImageUrl,
   101→        generation_id: actualGenerationId,
   102→        source_variant_id: activeVariantId || undefined,
   103→        scale_factor: settings.scaleFactor,
   104→        noise_scale: settings.noiseScale,
   105→        shot_id: shotId,
   106→      });
   107→
   108→      console.log('[ImageUpscale] Task created successfully');
   109→
   110→      // Show success state briefly
   111→      setUpscaleSuccess(true);
   112→      setTimeout(() => {
   113→        setUpscaleSuccess(false);
   114→      }, 2000);
   115→
   116→    } catch (error) {
   117→      handleError(error, { context: 'useUpscale', toastTitle: 'Failed to create enhance task' });
   118→    } finally {
   119→      removeIncomingTask(incomingTaskId);
   120→      setIsUpscaling(false);
   121→    }
   122→  }, [media, selectedProjectId, isVideo, mediaUrl, shotId, addIncomingTask, removeIncomingTask]);
   123→
   124→  return {
   125→    isUpscaling,
   126→    upscaleSuccess,
   127→    handleUpscale,
   128→    setActiveVariant,
   129→    // Backwards compatibility - these features removed but APIs kept
   130→    showingUpscaled: false,
   131→    handleToggleUpscaled: () => {},
   132→    effectiveImageUrl: mediaUrl,
   133→    sourceUrlForTasks: mediaUrl,
   134→    isPendingUpscale: false,
   135→    hasUpscaledVersion: false,
   136→    upscaledUrl: null,
   137→  };
   138→};
   139→

    23→

    44→/**
    45→ * Hook for managing video enhancement actions
    46→ *
    47→ * Settings are managed externally via the persistence system
    48→ * This hook handles validation and task creation
    49→ */
    50→export function useVideoEnhance({
    51→  projectId,
    52→  videoUrl,
    53→  shotId,
    54→  generationId,
    55→  activeVariantId,
    56→  settings,
    57→  updateSettings,
    58→}: UseVideoEnhanceProps): UseVideoEnhanceReturn {
    59→  const [isGenerating, setIsGenerating] = useState(false);
    60→  const [generateSuccess, setGenerateSuccess] = useState(false);
    61→  const { addIncomingTask, removeIncomingTask } = useIncomingTasks();
    62→
    63→  // Adapter to match the expected interface
    64→  const updateSetting = useCallback(<K extends keyof VideoEnhanceSettings>(
    65→    key: K,
    66→    value: VideoEnhanceSettings[K]
    67→  ) => {
    68→    updateSettings({ [key]: value });
    69→    // Reset success state when settings change
    70→    setGenerateSuccess(false);
    71→  }, [updateSettings]);
    72→
    73→  // Validation: at least one mode must be enabled
    74→  const isValid = settings.enableInterpolation || settings.enableUpscale;
    75→
    76→  // Can submit: valid settings + required data
    77→  const canSubmit = isValid && !!projectId && !!videoUrl && !isGenerating;
    78→
    79→  // Handle generate action
    80→  const handleGenerate = useCallback(async () => {
    81→    if (!canSubmit || !projectId || !videoUrl) {
    82→      console.warn('[useVideoEnhance] Cannot generate - missing required data');
    83→      return;
    84→    }
    85→
    86→    setIsGenerating(true);
    87→    setGenerateSuccess(false);
    88→
    89→    const enhanceLabel = [
    90→      settings.enableInterpolation && 'Interpolation',
    91→      settings.enableUpscale && 'Upscale',
    92→    ].filter(Boolean).join(' + ');
    93→    const incomingTaskId = addIncomingTask({
    94→      taskType: 'video_enhance',
    95→      label: enhanceLabel || 'Video Enhance',
    96→    });
    97→
    98→    try {
    99→      const params: VideoEnhanceTaskParams = {
   100→        project_id: projectId,
   101→        video_url: videoUrl,
   102→        enable_interpolation: settings.enableInterpolation,
   103→        enable_upscale: settings.enableUpscale,
   104→        shot_id: shotId,
   105→        based_on: generationId,
   106→        source_variant_id: activeVariantId || undefined,
   107→      };
   108→
   109→      // Add interpolation params if enabled
   110→      if (settings.enableInterpolation) {
   111→        params.interpolation = {
   112→          num_frames: settings.numFrames,
   113→          use_calculated_fps: true, // Always maintain playback speed
   114→        };
   115→      }
   116→
   117→      // Add upscale params if enabled
   118→      if (settings.enableUpscale) {
   119→        params.upscale = {
   120→          upscale_factor: settings.upscaleFactor,
   121→          color_fix: settings.colorFix,
   122→          output_quality: settings.outputQuality,
   123→        };
   124→      }
   125→
   126→      await createVideoEnhanceTask(params);
   127→      setGenerateSuccess(true);
   128→
   129→      console.log('[useVideoEnhance] Task created successfully');
   130→    } catch (error) {
   131→      console.error('[useVideoEnhance] Error creating task:', error);
   132→      handleError(error, { context: 'useVideoEnhance', toastTitle: 'Failed to create video enhancement task' });
   133→    } finally {
   134→      removeIncomingTask(incomingTaskId);
   135→      setIsGenerating(false);
   136→    }
   137→  }, [
   138→    canSubmit,
   139→    projectId,
   140→    videoUrl,
   141→    shotId,
   142→    generationId,
   143→    activeVariantId,
   144→    settings,
   145→    addIncomingTask,
   146→    removeIncomingTask,
   147→  ]);
   148→
   149→  return {
   150→    settings,
   151→    updateSetting,
   152→    isValid,
   153→    canSubmit,
   154→    handleGenerate,
   155→    isGenerating,
   156→    generateSuccess,
   157→  };
   158→}

   455→  }, [projectImageSettings, selectedProjectId, isLoadingProjectSettings, setGenerationSource, setSelectedTextModel]);
   456→
   457→  // Initialize LORAs from per-category storage (runs after loraManager is created)
   458→  // Categories: 'qwen' (all Qwen models + by-reference) and 'z-image'
   459→  // NOTE: This effect must be AFTER useGenerationSource so generationSource/selectedTextModel are defined
   460→  const hasInitializedLoras = useRef(false);
   461→  useEffect(() => {
   462→    if (isLoadingProjectSettings) return;
   463→    if (hasInitializedLoras.current) return;
   464→
   465→    // Determine current category based on generation source and model
   466→    const textModel = initializedTextModelRef.current || selectedTextModel;
   467→    const currentSource = projectImageSettings?.generationSource || generationSource;
   468→    // by-reference always uses 'qwen' category
   469→    const category: LoraCategory = currentSource === 'by-reference' ? 'qwen' : getLoraCategoryForModel(textModel);
   470→
   471→    // Try new category-based storage first, fall back to old per-model storage for migration
   472→    let categoryLoras: ActiveLora[] = [];
   473→    if (projectImageSettings?.selectedLorasByCategory) {
   474→      categoryLoras = projectImageSettings.selectedLorasByCategory[category] ?? [];
   475→    } else if (projectImageSettings?.selectedLorasByTextModel) {
   476→      // Migration: use old per-model storage
   477→      categoryLoras = projectImageSettings.selectedLorasByTextModel[textModel] ?? [];
   478→    }
   479→
   480→    if (categoryLoras.length > 0) {
   481→      loraManager.setSelectedLoras(categoryLoras);
   482→    }
   483→    hasInitializedLoras.current = true;
   484→  }, [projectImageSettings?.selectedLorasByCategory, projectImageSettings?.selectedLorasByTextModel, projectImageSettings?.generationSource, isLoadingProjectSettings, loraManager, selectedTextModel, generationSource]);
   485→

   632→  // Get current selected reference ID - from shot settings if shot selected, otherwise project settings
   633→  // Note: Unlike other fields, reference ID intentionally falls back to project value while loading
   634→  const effectiveSelectedReferenceId = useMemo<string | null>(() => {
   635→    if (associatedShotId && isShotSettingsReady) {
   636→      // Shot-level reference takes precedence if explicitly set
   637→      const shotRefId = shotPromptSettings.settings.selectedReferenceId;
   638→      if (shotRefId !== undefined) {
   639→        return shotRefId;
   640→      }
   641→    }
   642→    // Fall back to project-level selection

   644→  }, [associatedShotId, isShotSettingsReady, shotPromptSettings.settings.selectedReferenceId, selectedReferenceId]);
   645→
   646→  // Helper to update selected reference ID - routes to shot settings
   647→  const setEffectiveSelectedReferenceId = useCallback((newRefId: string | null) => {
   648→    if (associatedShotId) {
   649→      console.log('[ImageGenerationForm] setSelectedReferenceId for shot:', associatedShotId.substring(0, 8), newRefId);
   650→      shotPromptSettings.updateField('selectedReferenceId', newRefId);
   651→      markAsInteracted();
   652→    }

   656→
   657→  // NOTE: currentBeforePromptText, setCurrentBeforePromptText, currentAfterPromptText,

   659→  // NOTE: prompt count effects and localStorage shot settings now in usePromptManagement hook
   660→
   661→  // Consolidated debug logging (enable via VITE_DEBUG_IMAGE_GEN=true)
   662→  useEffect(() => {
   663→    if (!DEBUG_IMAGE_GEN) return;
   664→    console.log('[ImageGenerationForm] State:', {
   665→      ready,
   666→      isSaving,
   667→      projectId: selectedProjectId,
   668→      shotId: associatedShotId,
   669→      promptsCount: prompts.length,
   670→      shotPromptStatus: shotPromptSettings.status,
   671→    });
   672→  }, [DEBUG_IMAGE_GEN, ready, isSaving, associatedShotId, selectedProjectId, prompts.length, shotPromptSettings.status]);
   673→
   674→  // Apply initialShotId once after hydration (takes precedence over persisted value)
   675→  // If initialShotId is explicitly null, reset to None (opened from outside shot context)
   676→  // IMPORTANT: Do NOT call markAsInteracted() here - initialShotId is a temporary context
   677→  // override (e.g., from modal opened in shot context), not a user preference to persist.
   678→  // The tool page's persisted shot selection should only change when explicitly changed by user.
   679→  const hasAppliedInitialShotId = useRef(false);
   680→  useEffect(() => {
   681→    // Only apply once, after hydration is complete
   682→    // Only override when initialShotId is a specific shot ID (truthy string)
   683→    // null or undefined = keep the persisted value from project settings
   684→    if (ready && !hasAppliedInitialShotId.current && shots) {
   685→      if (initialShotId) {
   686→        // initialShotId was provided as a specific shot - set to that shot if it exists
   687→        const shotExists = shots.some(shot => shot.id === initialShotId);
   688→        if (shotExists && associatedShotId !== initialShotId) {
   689→          setAssociatedShotId(initialShotId);
   690→          // Don't persist - this is a temporary context override
   691→        }
   692→      }
   693→      // If initialShotId is null/undefined, keep the persisted value from project settings
   694→      hasAppliedInitialShotId.current = true;
   695→    }
   696→  }, [ready, initialShotId, shots, associatedShotId]);
   697→
   698→  // Reset associatedShotId if the selected shot no longer exists (e.g., was deleted)
   699→  useEffect(() => {
   700→    if (associatedShotId && shots) {
   701→      const shotExists = shots.some(shot => shot.id === associatedShotId);
   702→      if (!shotExists) {
   703→        console.log('[ImageGenerationForm] Selected shot', associatedShotId, 'no longer exists, resetting to None');
   704→        setAssociatedShotId(null);
   705→        markAsInteracted();
   706→      }
   707→    }
   708→  }, [associatedShotId, shots, markAsInteracted]);
   709→
   710→  // NOTE: initializedEntitiesRef and prompt initialization effect now in usePromptManagement hook
   711→  // NOTE: actionablePromptsCount now comes from usePromptManagement hook
   712→
   713→  const hasApiKey = true; // Always true for wan-local
   714→  
   715→  useImperativeHandle(ref, () => ({
   716→    applySettings: (settings: DisplayableMetadata) => {
   717→      markAsInteracted();
   718→      // Apply settings to the current shot's prompts
   719→      setPrompts([{
   720→        id: generatePromptId(),
   721→        fullPrompt: settings.prompt || '',
   722→        shortPrompt: settings.shortPrompt
   723→      }]);
   724→      setImagesPerPrompt(1);
   725→
   726→      if (settings.activeLoras && settings.activeLoras.length > 0 && availableLoras.length > 0) {
   727→        const newSelectedLoras: ActiveLora[] = [];
   728→        settings.activeLoras.forEach(metaLora => {
   729→          const foundFullLora = availableLoras.find(al => al['Model ID'] === metaLora.id);
   730→          if (foundFullLora) {
   731→            newSelectedLoras.push({
   732→              id: metaLora.id,
   733→              name: metaLora.name,
   734→              path: metaLora.path,
   735→              strength: metaLora.strength,
   736→              previewImageUrl: foundFullLora.Images && foundFullLora.Images.length > 0 ? foundFullLora.Images[0].url : metaLora.previewImageUrl
   737→            });
   738→          }
   739→        });
   740→        loraManager.setSelectedLoras(newSelectedLoras);
   741→      } else {
   742→        loraManager.setSelectedLoras([]);
   743→      }
   744→      // Note: beforeEachPromptText/afterEachPromptText are NOT restored - they reset on page load
   745→    },
   746→    getAssociatedShotId: () => associatedShotId,
   747→  }));
   748→

   771→
   772→  // NOTE: Reference handlers (handleStyleReferenceUpload, handleResourceSelect, handleSelectReference,

   778→
   779→  // NOTE: Prompt handlers (handleAddPrompt, handleUpdatePrompt, handleRemovePrompt, handleDeleteAllPrompts,

   781→
   782→
   783→  const handleOpenMagicPrompt = useCallback(() => {

   787→
   788→  // Handle creating a new shot
   789→  const handleCreateShot = useCallback(async (shotName: string, files: File[]) => {
   790→    // Use unified shot creation - handles inheritance, events, lastAffected automatically
   791→    const result = await createShot({
   792→      name: shotName,
   793→      files: files.length > 0 ? files : undefined,
   794→      dispatchSkeletonEvents: false, // No skeleton needed in form context
   795→      onSuccess: () => {
   796→        // Invalidate and refetch shots to update the list
   797→        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ [REDACTED](selectedProjectId!) });
   798→        queryClient.refetchQueries({ [REDACTED](selectedProjectId!) });
   799→      },
   800→    });
   801→
   802→    if (!result) {
   803→      // Error already shown by useShotCreation
   804→      return;
   805→    }
   806→
   807→    console.log('[ImageGenerationForm] Shot created:', {
   808→      shotId: result.shotId.substring(0, 8),
   809→      shotName: result.shotName,
   810→    });
   811→
   812→    // Note: Settings inheritance is handled automatically by useShotCreation
   813→    
   814→    // Switch to the newly created shot
   815→    markAsInteracted();
   816→    setAssociatedShotId(result.shotId);
   817→    uiActions.setCreateShotModalOpen(false);
   818→  }, [createShot, markAsInteracted, queryClient, selectedProjectId, uiActions]);
   819→
   820→  // Optimize event handlers with useCallback to prevent recreating on each render
   821→  const handleSliderChange = useCallback((setter: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<number>>) => (value: number) => {
   822→    markAsInteracted();
   823→    setter(value);
   824→  }, [markAsInteracted]);
   825→
   826→  const handleTextChange = useCallback((setter: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<string>>) => (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLTextAreaElement>) => {
   827→    markAsInteracted();
   828→    setter(e.target.value);
   829→  }, [markAsInteracted]);
   830→
   831→  // Ensure the `promptIdCounter` is always ahead of any existing numeric IDs.
   832→  // This prevents duplicate IDs which caused multiple prompts to update together.
   833→  useEffect(() => {
   834→    let nextId = prompts.reduce((max, p) => {
   835→      const match = /^prompt-(\d+)$/.exec(p.id || "");
   836→      if (match) {
   837→        const num = parseInt(match[1], 10) + 1;
   838→        return num > max ? num : max;
   839→      }
   840→      return max;
   841→    }, 1);
   842→
   843→    // Resolve any duplicate IDs on the fly by assigning new ones.
   844→    const seen = new Set<string>();
   845→    let hadDuplicates = false;
   846→    const dedupedPrompts = prompts.map(prompt => {
   847→      if (!seen.has(prompt.id)) {
   848→        seen.add(prompt.id);
   849→        return prompt;
   850→      }
   851→      hadDuplicates = true;
   852→      // Duplicate found – give it a fresh ID.
   853→      const newId = `prompt-${nextId++}`;
   854→      seen.add(newId);
   855→      return { ...prompt, id: newId };
   856→    });
   857→
   858→    if (hadDuplicates) {
   859→      setPrompts(dedupedPrompts);
   860→    }
   861→
   862→    if (nextId > promptIdCounter.current) {
   863→      promptIdCounter.current = nextId;
   864→    }
   865→  }, [prompts, setPrompts]);
   866→
   867→  // Handle shot change with proper prompt initialization
   868→  const handleShotChange = (value: string) => {
   869→    markAsInteracted();
   870→    const newShotId = value === "none" ? null : value;
   871→
   872→    // usePersistentToolState auto-syncs associatedShotId to DB
   873→    setAssociatedShotId(newShotId);
   874→
   875→    // Call the parent callback if provided
   876→    if (onShotChange) {
   877→      onShotChange(newShotId);
   878→    }
   879→
   880→    // Note: Prompts for the new shot will be loaded automatically via useAutoSaveSettings
   881→    // and initialized if empty via the initialization effect
   882→    markAsInteracted();
   883→  };
   884→

   887→

   889→  // This allows users to keep their settings when switching references

   913→

   926→
   927→  const referenceState = useMemo<FormReferenceState>(() => ({

   982→
   983→  return (
   984→    <ImageGenerationFormProvider value={contextValue}>
   985→      <form id="image-generation-form" onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-6">
   986→        {/* Main Content Layout */}
   987→        <div className="flex gap-6 flex-col md:flex-row pb-4">
   988→          {/* Left Column - Prompts and Shot Selector */}
   989→          <div className="flex-1 space-y-6">
   990→            <PromptsSection
   991→              onPromptModeChange={(mode) => {

  1002→
  1003→            <ShotSelector
  1004→              shots={shots}
  1005→              associatedShotId={associatedShotId}
  1006→              isGenerating={automatedSubmitButton.isSubmitting}

  1008→              onChangeShot={handleShotChange}
  1009→              onClearShot={() => {
  1010→                markAsInteracted();
  1011→                setAssociatedShotId(null);
  1012→              }}
  1013→              onOpenCreateShot={() => uiActions.setCreateShotModalOpen(true)}
  1014→              onJumpToShot={navigateToShot}
  1015→            />
  1016→          </div>
  1017→          
  1018→          {/* Right Column - Reference Image and Settings */}
  1019→          <ModelSection
  1020→            // Props not in context
  1021→            generationSource={generationSource}
  1022→            onGenerationSourceChange={handleGenerationSourceChange}
  1023→            selectedTextModel={selectedTextModel}
  1024→            onTextModelChange={handleTextModelChange}
  1025→            onOpenLoraModal={() => loraManager.setIsLoraModalOpen(true)}
  1026→            isLoadingReferenceData={isReferenceDataLoading}
  1027→            referenceCount={referenceCount}
  1028→          />
  1029→        </div>
  1030→
  1031→        {/* Generation Settings (dimensions always, phase config for local only) */}
  1032→        <div className="md:col-span-2 mt-2">
  1033→          <GenerationSettingsSection
  1034→            hiresFixConfig={hiresFixConfig}
  1035→            onHiresFixConfigChange={setHiresFixConfig}
  1036→            projectResolution={projectResolution}
  1037→            projectAspectRatio={projectAspectRatio}
  1038→            disabled={automatedSubmitButton.isSubmitting || !hasApiKey}
  1039→            isLocalGeneration={isLocalGenerationEnabled}
  1040→          />
  1041→        </div>
  1042→
  1043→        {/* Spacer to ensure content can scroll above sticky footer (only when not using portal) */}
  1044→        {stickyFooter && !footerPortalId && <div className="h-4" />}
  1045→
  1046→        {/* Footer: portaled when footerPortalId provided, sticky when stickyFooter without portal, inline otherwise */}
  1047→        {(() => {
  1048→          const footerContent = (
  1049→            <div className={
  1050→              stickyFooter
  1051→                ? footerPortalId
  1052→                  ? "px-6 py-3 bg-background border-t border-zinc-700" // Portaled footer
  1053→                  : "sticky bottom-0 z-50 !mt-0 -mx-6 px-6 py-3 bg-background border-t border-zinc-700" // Sticky footer
  1054→                : "border-t border-border pt-6 mt-2" // Inline footer (tool page)
  1055→            }>
  1056→              <GenerateControls
  1057→                imagesPerPrompt={imagesPerPrompt}
  1058→                onChangeImagesPerPrompt={handleSliderChange(setImagesPerPrompt)}
  1059→                actionablePromptsCount={actionablePromptsCount}
  1060→                isGenerating={automatedSubmitButton.isSubmitting}
  1061→                hasApiKey={hasApiKey}
  1062→                justQueued={automatedSubmitButton.isSuccess}
  1063→                promptMode={effectivePromptMode}
  1064→                onUseExistingPrompts={handleUseExistingPrompts}
  1065→                onNewPromptsLikeExisting={handleNewPromptsLikeExisting}
  1066→                promptMultiplier={promptMultiplier}
  1067→                onChangePromptMultiplier={setPromptMultiplier}
  1068→              />
  1069→            </div>
  1070→          );
  1071→
  1072→          // Portal footer outside scroll container when footerPortalId is provided
  1073→          if (stickyFooter && footerPortalId) {
  1074→            const portalTarget = document.getElementById(footerPortalId);
  1075→            return portalTarget ? createPortal(footerContent, portalTarget) : footerContent;
  1076→          }
  1077→          return footerContent;
  1078→        })()}
  1079→      </form>
  1080→
  1081→      <ChunkLoadErrorBoundary>
  1082→        <Suspense fallback={<div className="sr-only">Loading...</div>}>
  1083→          <LazyLoraSelectorModal
  1084→            isOpen={loraManager.isLoraModalOpen}
  1085→            onClose={() => loraManager.setIsLoraModalOpen(false)}
  1086→            loras={availableLoras}
  1087→            onAddLora={handleAddLora}
  1088→            onRemoveLora={handleRemoveLora}
  1089→            onUpdateLoraStrength={handleLoraStrengthChange}
  1090→            selectedLoras={loraManager.selectedLoras.map(lora => {
  1091→              const fullLora = availableLoras.find(loraModel => loraModel['Model ID'] === lora.id);
  1092→              return {
  1093→                ...fullLora,
  1094→                "Model ID": lora.id,
  1095→                Name: lora.name,
  1096→                strength: lora.strength,

  1124→        projectId={selectedProjectId}

  1126→    </ImageGenerationFormProvider>

   407→  const handleGenerateAndQueue = useCallback((updatedPrompts: PromptEntry[]) => {

   453→    }
   454→
   455→    if (generationSourceRef.current === 'by-reference' && !styleReferenceImageGeneration) {
   456→      toast.error("Please upload a style reference image for by-reference mode.");
   457→      return;
   458→    }
   459→
   460→    try {
   461→      isGeneratingAutomatedPromptsRef.current = true;
   462→
   463→      const rawResults = await aiGeneratePrompts({
   464→        overallPromptText: "Make me more prompts like this.",
   465→        numberToGenerate: imagesPerPrompt,
   466→        existingPrompts: activePrompts.map(p => ({ id: p.id, text: p.fullPrompt, shortText: p.shortPrompt })),
   467→        addSummaryForNewPrompts: true,
   468→        replaceCurrentPrompts: true,
   469→        temperature: 0.8,
   470→        rulesToRememberText: '',
   471→      });
   472→
   473→      const newPrompts: PromptEntry[] = rawResults.map(item => ({
   474→        id: item.id,
   475→        fullPrompt: item.text,
   476→        shortPrompt: item.shortText || item.text.substring(0, 30) + (item.text.length > 30 ? "..." : ""),
   477→      }));
   478→
   479→      setPrompts(newPrompts);
   480→
   481→      const taskParams = getTaskParams(newPrompts, { imagesPerPromptOverride: promptMultiplier });
   482→      if (!taskParams) return;
   483→
   484→      onGenerate(taskParams);
   485→    } catch (error) {
   486→      handleError(error, { context: 'useFormSubmission.handleNewPromptsLikeExisting', toastTitle: 'Failed to generate prompts. Please try again.' });
   487→    } finally {
   488→      isGeneratingAutomatedPromptsRef.current = false;
   489→    }
   490→  }, [
   491→    prompts,
   492→    styleReferenceImageGeneration,
   493→    generationSourceRef,
   494→    imagesPerPrompt,
   495→    promptMultiplier,
   496→    getTaskParams,
   497→    onGenerate,
   498→    aiGeneratePrompts,
   499→    setPrompts,
   500→  ]);
   501→
   502→  // ============================================================================
   503→  // Return
   504→  // ============================================================================
   505→
   506→  return {
   507→    getTaskParams,
   508→    handleSubmit,
   509→    handleGenerateAndQueue,
   510→    handleUseExistingPrompts,
   511→    handleNewPromptsLikeExisting,
   512→    isGeneratingAutomatedPrompts: isGeneratingAutomatedPromptsRef.current,
   513→  };
   514→}
   515→

1→import {
     2→  createTask,
     3→  validateRequiredFields,
     4→  TaskValidationError,
     5→  BaseTaskParams,
     6→} from '../taskCreation';
     7→import type { TaskCreationResult } from '../taskCreation';
     8→import { handleError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandler';
     9→
    10→/**
    11→ * Parameters for creating an image upscale task
    12→ */
    13→export interface ImageUpscaleTaskParams {
    14→  project_id: string;
    15→  image_url: string; // The source image to upscale
    16→  generation_id?: string | null; // Optional: the generation ID to update with upscaled URL
    17→  source_variant_id?: string; // Optional: track which variant was the source for lineage
    18→  scale_factor?: number; // Default to 2x upscaling
    19→  noise_scale?: number; // Default to 0.1
    20→  output_format?: string; // Default to "jpeg"
    21→  shot_id?: string; // Optional: associate with shot for navigation from TasksPane
    22→}
    23→
    24→/**
    25→ * Validates image upscale task parameters
    26→ * @param params - Parameters to validate
    27→ * @throws TaskValidationError if validation fails
    28→ */
    29→function validateImageUpscaleParams(params: ImageUpscaleTaskParams): void {
    30→  validateRequiredFields(params, ['project_id', 'image_url']);
    31→
    32→  if (params.image_url.trim() === '') {
    33→    throw new TaskValidationError('Image URL cannot be empty', 'image_url');
    34→  }
    35→
    36→  // Validate URL format
    37→  try {
    38→    new URL(params.image_url);
    39→  } catch {
    40→    throw new TaskValidationError('Image URL must be a valid URL', 'image_url');
    41→  }
    42→
    43→  if (params.scale_factor !== undefined && (params.scale_factor < 1 || params.scale_factor > 8)) {
    44→    throw new TaskValidationError('Scale factor must be between 1 and 8', 'scale_factor');
    45→  }
    46→}
    47→
    48→/**
    49→ * Builds the task params for an image-upscale task
    50→ */
    51→function buildImageUpscaleTaskParams(
    52→  params: ImageUpscaleTaskParams
    53→): Record<string, unknown> {
    54→  const taskParams: Record<string, unknown> = {
    55→    image: params.image_url,
    56→    scale_factor: params.scale_factor ?? 2,
    57→    noise_scale: params.noise_scale ?? 0.1,
    58→    output_format: params.output_format ?? "jpeg",
    59→  };
    60→
    61→  // Add generation_id to task params so the worker knows which generation to update
    62→  // Also add based_on for lineage tracking (same as inpaint and other edit tasks)
    63→  // is_primary=true makes the upscaled version the main display variant
    64→  if (params.generation_id) {
    65→    taskParams.generation_id = params.generation_id;
    66→    taskParams.based_on = params.generation_id;
    67→    taskParams.is_primary = true;
    68→  }
    69→
    70→  // Track source variant if enhancing from a specific variant
    71→  if (params.source_variant_id) {
    72→    taskParams.source_variant_id = params.source_variant_id;
    73→  }
    74→
    75→  if (params.shot_id) {
    76→    taskParams.shot_id = params.shot_id;
    77→  }
    78→
    79→  return taskParams;
    80→}
