   438→  const hasInitializedGenerationSource = useRef(false);
   439→  const initializedTextModelRef = useRef<TextToImageModel | null>(null);
   440→  useEffect(() => {
   441→    if (isLoadingProjectSettings) return;
   442→    if (hasInitializedGenerationSource.current) return;
   443→    if (!projectImageSettings) return;
   444→
   445→    if (projectImageSettings.generationSource) {
   446→      setGenerationSource(projectImageSettings.generationSource);
   447→    }
   448→    const textModel = projectImageSettings.selectedTextModel || 'qwen-image';
   449→    if (projectImageSettings.selectedTextModel) {
   450→      setSelectedTextModel(projectImageSettings.selectedTextModel);
   451→    }
   452→    // Store for LORA initialization (done in separate effect after loraManager is created)
   453→    initializedTextModelRef.current = textModel;
   454→    hasInitializedGenerationSource.current = true;
   455→  }, [projectImageSettings, selectedProjectId, isLoadingProjectSettings, setGenerationSource, setSelectedTextModel]);
   456→
   457→  // Initialize LORAs from per-category storage (runs after loraManager is created)
   458→  // Categories: 'qwen' (all Qwen models + by-reference) and 'z-image'
   459→  // NOTE: This effect must be AFTER useGenerationSource so generationSource/selectedTextModel are defined
   460→  const hasInitializedLoras = useRef(false);
   461→  useEffect(() => {
   462→    if (isLoadingProjectSettings) return;
   463→    if (hasInitializedLoras.current) return;
   464→
   465→    // Determine current category based on generation source and model
   466→    const textModel = initializedTextModelRef.current || selectedTextModel;
   467→    const currentSource = projectImageSettings?.generationSource || generationSource;
   468→    // by-reference always uses 'qwen' category
   469→    const category: LoraCategory = currentSource === 'by-reference' ? 'qwen' : getLoraCategoryForModel(textModel);
   470→
   471→    // Try new category-based storage first, fall back to old per-model storage for migration
   472→    let categoryLoras: ActiveLora[] = [];
   473→    if (projectImageSettings?.selectedLorasByCategory) {
   474→      categoryLoras = projectImageSettings.selectedLorasByCategory[category] ?? [];
   475→    } else if (projectImageSettings?.selectedLorasByTextModel) {
   476→      // Migration: use old per-model storage
   477→      categoryLoras = projectImageSettings.selectedLorasByTextModel[textModel] ?? [];
   478→    }
   479→
   480→    if (categoryLoras.length > 0) {
   481→      loraManager.setSelectedLoras(categoryLoras);
   482→    }
   483→    hasInitializedLoras.current = true;
   484→  }, [projectImageSettings?.selectedLorasByCategory, projectImageSettings?.selectedLorasByTextModel, projectImageSettings?.generationSource, isLoadingProjectSettings, loraManager, selectedTextModel, generationSource]);
   485→

   632→  // Get current selected reference ID - from shot settings if shot selected, otherwise project settings
   633→  // Note: Unlike other fields, reference ID intentionally falls back to project value while loading
   634→  const effectiveSelectedReferenceId = useMemo<string | null>(() => {
   635→    if (associatedShotId && isShotSettingsReady) {
   636→      // Shot-level reference takes precedence if explicitly set
   637→      const shotRefId = shotPromptSettings.settings.selectedReferenceId;
   638→      if (shotRefId !== undefined) {
   639→        return shotRefId;
   640→      }
   641→    }
   642→    // Fall back to project-level selection
   643→    return selectedReferenceId;
   644→  }, [associatedShotId, isShotSettingsReady, shotPromptSettings.settings.selectedReferenceId, selectedReferenceId]);
   645→
   646→  // Helper to update selected reference ID - routes to shot settings
   647→  const setEffectiveSelectedReferenceId = useCallback((newRefId: string | null) => {
   648→    if (associatedShotId) {
   649→      console.log('[ImageGenerationForm] setSelectedReferenceId for shot:', associatedShotId.substring(0, 8), newRefId);
   650→      shotPromptSettings.updateField('selectedReferenceId', newRefId);
   651→      markAsInteracted();
   652→    }
   653→    // Also update the project-level per-shot mapping for backwards compatibility
   654→    // (handled by existing handleReferenceSelection)
   655→  }, [associatedShotId, shotPromptSettings, markAsInteracted]);
   656→
   657→  // NOTE: currentBeforePromptText, setCurrentBeforePromptText, currentAfterPromptText,
   658→  //       setCurrentAfterPromptText now come from usePromptManagement hook
   659→  // NOTE: prompt count effects and localStorage shot settings now in usePromptManagement hook
   660→
   661→  // Consolidated debug logging (enable via VITE_DEBUG_IMAGE_GEN=true)
   662→  useEffect(() => {
   663→    if (!DEBUG_IMAGE_GEN) return;
   664→    console.log('[ImageGenerationForm] State:', {
   665→      ready,
   666→      isSaving,
   667→      projectId: selectedProjectId,
   668→      shotId: associatedShotId,
   669→      promptsCount: prompts.length,
   670→      shotPromptStatus: shotPromptSettings.status,
   671→    });
   672→  }, [DEBUG_IMAGE_GEN, ready, isSaving, associatedShotId, selectedProjectId, prompts.length, shotPromptSettings.status]);
   673→
   674→  // Apply initialShotId once after hydration (takes precedence over persisted value)
   675→  // If initialShotId is explicitly null, reset to None (opened from outside shot context)
   676→  // IMPORTANT: Do NOT call markAsInteracted() here - initialShotId is a temporary context
   677→  // override (e.g., from modal opened in shot context), not a user preference to persist.
   678→  // The tool page's persisted shot selection should only change when explicitly changed by user.
   679→  const hasAppliedInitialShotId = useRef(false);
   680→  useEffect(() => {
   681→    // Only apply once, after hydration is complete
   682→    // Only override when initialShotId is a specific shot ID (truthy string)
   683→    // null or undefined = keep the persisted value from project settings
   684→    if (ready && !hasAppliedInitialShotId.current && shots) {
   685→      if (initialShotId) {
   686→        // initialShotId was provided as a specific shot - set to that shot if it exists
   687→        const shotExists = shots.some(shot => shot.id === initialShotId);
   688→        if (shotExists && associatedShotId !== initialShotId) {
   689→          setAssociatedShotId(initialShotId);
   690→          // Don't persist - this is a temporary context override
   691→        }
   692→      }
   693→      // If initialShotId is null/undefined, keep the persisted value from project settings
   694→      hasAppliedInitialShotId.current = true;
   695→    }
   696→  }, [ready, initialShotId, shots, associatedShotId]);
   697→
   698→  // Reset associatedShotId if the selected shot no longer exists (e.g., was deleted)
   699→  useEffect(() => {
   700→    if (associatedShotId && shots) {
   701→      const shotExists = shots.some(shot => shot.id === associatedShotId);
   702→      if (!shotExists) {
   703→        console.log('[ImageGenerationForm] Selected shot', associatedShotId, 'no longer exists, resetting to None');
   704→        setAssociatedShotId(null);
   705→        markAsInteracted();
   706→      }
   707→    }
   708→  }, [associatedShotId, shots, markAsInteracted]);
   709→
   710→  // NOTE: initializedEntitiesRef and prompt initialization effect now in usePromptManagement hook
   711→  // NOTE: actionablePromptsCount now comes from usePromptManagement hook
   712→
   713→  const hasApiKey = true; // Always true for wan-local
   714→  
   715→  useImperativeHandle(ref, () => ({
   716→    applySettings: (settings: DisplayableMetadata) => {
   717→      markAsInteracted();
   718→      // Apply settings to the current shot's prompts
   719→      setPrompts([{
   720→        id: generatePromptId(),
   721→        fullPrompt: settings.prompt || '',
   722→        shortPrompt: settings.shortPrompt
   723→      }]);
   724→      setImagesPerPrompt(1);
   725→
   726→      if (settings.activeLoras && settings.activeLoras.length > 0 && availableLoras.length > 0) {
   727→        const newSelectedLoras: ActiveLora[] = [];
   728→        settings.activeLoras.forEach(metaLora => {
   729→          const foundFullLora = availableLoras.find(al => al['Model ID'] === metaLora.id);
   730→          if (foundFullLora) {
   731→            newSelectedLoras.push({
   732→              id: metaLora.id,
   733→              name: metaLora.name,
   734→              path: metaLora.path,
   735→              strength: metaLora.strength,
   736→              previewImageUrl: foundFullLora.Images && foundFullLora.Images.length > 0 ? foundFullLora.Images[0].url : metaLora.previewImageUrl
   737→            });
   738→          }
   739→        });
   740→        loraManager.setSelectedLoras(newSelectedLoras);
   741→      } else {
   742→        loraManager.setSelectedLoras([]);
   743→      }
   744→      // Note: beforeEachPromptText/afterEachPromptText are NOT restored - they reset on page load
   745→    },
   746→    getAssociatedShotId: () => associatedShotId,
   747→  }));
   748→

   781→

   788→  // Handle creating a new shot
   789→  const handleCreateShot = useCallback(async (shotName: string, files: File[]) => {
   790→    // Use unified shot creation - handles inheritance, events, lastAffected automatically
   791→    const result = await createShot({
   792→      name: shotName,
   793→      files: files.length > 0 ? files : undefined,
   794→      dispatchSkeletonEvents: false, // No skeleton needed in form context
   795→      onSuccess: () => {
   796→        // Invalidate and refetch shots to update the list
   797→        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ [REDACTED](selectedProjectId!) });
   798→        queryClient.refetchQueries({ [REDACTED](selectedProjectId!) });
   799→      },
   800→    });
   801→
   802→    if (!result) {
   803→      // Error already shown by useShotCreation
   804→      return;
   805→    }
   806→
   807→    console.log('[ImageGenerationForm] Shot created:', {
   808→      shotId: result.shotId.substring(0, 8),
   809→      shotName: result.shotName,
   810→    });
   811→
   812→    // Note: Settings inheritance is handled automatically by useShotCreation
   813→    
   814→    // Switch to the newly created shot
   815→    markAsInteracted();
   816→    setAssociatedShotId(result.shotId);
   817→    uiActions.setCreateShotModalOpen(false);
   818→  }, [createShot, markAsInteracted, queryClient, selectedProjectId, uiActions]);
   819→
   820→  // Optimize event handlers with useCallback to prevent recreating on each render
   821→  const handleSliderChange = useCallback((setter: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<number>>) => (value: number) => {
   822→    markAsInteracted();
   823→    setter(value);
   824→  }, [markAsInteracted]);
   825→
   826→  const handleTextChange = useCallback((setter: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<string>>) => (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLTextAreaElement>) => {
   827→    markAsInteracted();
   828→    setter(e.target.value);
   829→  }, [markAsInteracted]);
   830→
   831→  // Ensure the `promptIdCounter` is always ahead of any existing numeric IDs.
   832→  // This prevents duplicate IDs which caused multiple prompts to update together.
   833→  useEffect(() => {
   834→    let nextId = prompts.reduce((max, p) => {
   835→      const match = /^prompt-(\d+)$/.exec(p.id || "");
   836→      if (match) {
   837→        const num = parseInt(match[1], 10) + 1;
   838→        return num > max ? num : max;
   839→      }
   840→      return max;
   841→    }, 1);
   842→
   843→    // Resolve any duplicate IDs on the fly by assigning new ones.
   844→    const seen = new Set<string>();
   845→    let hadDuplicates = false;
   846→    const dedupedPrompts = prompts.map(prompt => {
   847→      if (!seen.has(prompt.id)) {
   848→        seen.add(prompt.id);
   849→        return prompt;
   850→      }
   851→      hadDuplicates = true;
   852→      // Duplicate found – give it a fresh ID.
   853→      const newId = `prompt-${nextId++}`;
   854→      seen.add(newId);
   855→      return { ...prompt, id: newId };
   856→    });
   857→
   858→    if (hadDuplicates) {
   859→      setPrompts(dedupedPrompts);
   860→    }
   861→
   862→    if (nextId > promptIdCounter.current) {
   863→      promptIdCounter.current = nextId;
   864→    }
   865→  }, [prompts, setPrompts]);
   866→
   867→  // Handle shot change with proper prompt initialization
   868→  const handleShotChange = (value: string) => {
   869→    markAsInteracted();
   870→    const newShotId = value === "none" ? null : value;
   871→
   872→    // usePersistentToolState auto-syncs associatedShotId to DB
   873→    setAssociatedShotId(newShotId);
   874→
   875→    // Call the parent callback if provided
   876→    if (onShotChange) {
   877→      onShotChange(newShotId);
   878→    }
   879→
   880→    // Note: Prompts for the new shot will be loaded automatically via useAutoSaveSettings
   881→    // and initialized if empty via the initialization effect
   882→    markAsInteracted();
   883→  };
   884→

   887→

   926→

   983→  return (
   984→    <ImageGenerationFormProvider value={contextValue}>
   985→      <form id="image-generation-form" onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-6">
   986→        {/* Main Content Layout */}
   987→        <div className="flex gap-6 flex-col md:flex-row pb-4">
   988→          {/* Left Column - Prompts and Shot Selector */}
   989→          <div className="flex-1 space-y-6">
   990→            <PromptsSection
   991→              onPromptModeChange={(mode) => {
   992→                markAsInteracted();
   993→                setEffectivePromptMode(mode);
   994→                // Auto-set imagesPerPrompt based on mode
   995→                if (mode === 'automated') {
   996→                  setImagesPerPrompt(8);
   997→                } else if (mode === 'managed') {
   998→                  setImagesPerPrompt(1);
   999→                }
  1000→              }}
  1001→            />
  1002→
  1003→            <ShotSelector
  1004→              shots={shots}
  1005→              associatedShotId={associatedShotId}
  1006→              isGenerating={automatedSubmitButton.isSubmitting}
  1007→              hasApiKey={hasApiKey}
  1008→              onChangeShot={handleShotChange}
  1009→              onClearShot={() => {
  1010→                markAsInteracted();
  1011→                setAssociatedShotId(null);
  1012→              }}
  1013→              onOpenCreateShot={() => uiActions.setCreateShotModalOpen(true)}
  1014→              onJumpToShot={navigateToShot}
  1015→            />
  1016→          </div>
  1017→          
  1018→          {/* Right Column - Reference Image and Settings */}
  1019→          <ModelSection
  1020→            // Props not in context
  1021→            generationSource={generationSource}
  1022→            onGenerationSourceChange={handleGenerationSourceChange}
  1023→            selectedTextModel={selectedTextModel}
  1024→            onTextModelChange={handleTextModelChange}
  1025→            onOpenLoraModal={() => loraManager.setIsLoraModalOpen(true)}
  1026→            isLoadingReferenceData={isReferenceDataLoading}
  1027→            referenceCount={referenceCount}
  1028→          />
  1029→        </div>
  1030→
  1031→        {/* Generation Settings (dimensions always, phase config for local only) */}
  1032→        <div className="md:col-span-2 mt-2">
  1033→          <GenerationSettingsSection
  1034→            hiresFixConfig={hiresFixConfig}
  1035→            onHiresFixConfigChange={setHiresFixConfig}
  1036→            projectResolution={projectResolution}
  1037→            projectAspectRatio={projectAspectRatio}
  1038→            disabled={automatedSubmitButton.isSubmitting || !hasApiKey}
  1039→            isLocalGeneration={isLocalGenerationEnabled}
  1040→          />
  1041→        </div>
  1042→
  1043→        {/* Spacer to ensure content can scroll above sticky footer (only when not using portal) */}
  1044→        {stickyFooter && !footerPortalId && <div className="h-4" />}
  1045→
  1046→        {/* Footer: portaled when footerPortalId provided, sticky when stickyFooter without portal, inline otherwise */}
  1047→        {(() => {
  1048→          const footerContent = (
  1049→            <div className={
  1050→              stickyFooter
  1051→                ? footerPortalId
  1052→                  ? "px-6 py-3 bg-background border-t border-zinc-700" // Portaled footer
  1053→                  : "sticky bottom-0 z-50 !mt-0 -mx-6 px-6 py-3 bg-background border-t border-zinc-700" // Sticky footer
  1054→                : "border-t border-border pt-6 mt-2" // Inline footer (tool page)
  1055→            }>
  1056→              <GenerateControls
  1057→                imagesPerPrompt={imagesPerPrompt}
  1058→                onChangeImagesPerPrompt={handleSliderChange(setImagesPerPrompt)}
  1059→                actionablePromptsCount={actionablePromptsCount}
  1060→                isGenerating={automatedSubmitButton.isSubmitting}
  1061→                hasApiKey={hasApiKey}
  1062→                justQueued={automatedSubmitButton.isSuccess}
  1063→                promptMode={effectivePromptMode}
  1064→                onUseExistingPrompts={handleUseExistingPrompts}
  1065→                onNewPromptsLikeExisting={handleNewPromptsLikeExisting}
  1066→                promptMultiplier={promptMultiplier}
  1067→                onChangePromptMultiplier={setPromptMultiplier}
  1068→              />
  1069→            </div>
  1070→          );
  1071→
  1072→          // Portal footer outside scroll container when footerPortalId is provided
  1073→          if (stickyFooter && footerPortalId) {
  1074→            const portalTarget = document.getElementById(footerPortalId);
  1075→            return portalTarget ? createPortal(footerContent, portalTarget) : footerContent;
  1076→          }
  1077→          return footerContent;
  1078→        })()}
  1079→      </form>
  1080→
  1081→      <ChunkLoadErrorBoundary>
  1082→        <Suspense fallback={<div className="sr-only">Loading...</div>}>
  1083→          <LazyLoraSelectorModal
  1084→            isOpen={loraManager.isLoraModalOpen}
  1085→            onClose={() => loraManager.setIsLoraModalOpen(false)}
  1086→            loras={availableLoras}
  1087→            onAddLora={handleAddLora}
  1088→            onRemoveLora={handleRemoveLora}
  1089→            onUpdateLoraStrength={handleLoraStrengthChange}
  1090→            selectedLoras={loraManager.selectedLoras.map(lora => {
  1091→              const fullLora = availableLoras.find(loraModel => loraModel['Model ID'] === lora.id);
  1092→              return {
  1093→                ...fullLora,
  1094→                "Model ID": lora.id,
  1095→                Name: lora.name,
  1096→                strength: lora.strength,
  1097→              } as LoraModel & { strength: number };

  1099→            lora_type={generationSource === 'just-text' ? getLoraTypeForModel(selectedTextModel) : BY_REFERENCE_LORA_TYPE}
  1100→          />
  1101→        </Suspense>
  1102→      </ChunkLoadErrorBoundary>
  1103→
  1104→      <ChunkLoadErrorBoundary>
  1105→        <Suspense fallback={<div className="sr-only">Loading...</div>}>

  1114→            onGenerateAndQueue={handleGenerateAndQueue}

  1118→
  1119→      <CreateShotModal

  1121→        onClose={() => uiActions.setCreateShotModalOpen(false)}
  1122→        onSubmit={handleCreateShot}
  1123→        isLoading={isCreatingShot}
  1124→        projectId={selectedProjectId}
  1125→      />
  1126→    </ImageGenerationFormProvider>

   407→  const handleGenerateAndQueue = useCallback((updatedPrompts: PromptEntry[]) => {

   430→
   431→    onGenerate(taskParams);
   432→  }, [setPrompts, getTaskParams, onGenerate]);
   433→
   434→  // ============================================================================
   435→  // Handle Use Existing Prompts
   436→  // ============================================================================
   437→
   438→  const handleUseExistingPrompts = useCallback(async () => {
   439→    const taskParams = getTaskParams(prompts, { imagesPerPromptOverride: promptMultiplier });
   440→    if (!taskParams) return;
   441→    onGenerate(taskParams);
   442→  }, [prompts, promptMultiplier, getTaskParams, onGenerate]);
   443→
   444→  // ============================================================================
   445→  // Handle New Prompts Like Existing
   446→  // ============================================================================
   447→
   448→  const handleNewPromptsLikeExisting = useCallback(async () => {
   449→    const activePrompts = prompts.filter(p => p.fullPrompt.trim() !== "");
   450→    if (activePrompts.length === 0) {
   451→      toast.error("No prompts available. Please add prompts first.");
   452→      return;
   453→    }
   454→
   455→    if (generationSourceRef.current === 'by-reference' && !styleReferenceImageGeneration) {
   456→      toast.error("Please upload a style reference image for by-reference mode.");
   457→      return;
   458→    }
   459→
   460→    try {
   461→      isGeneratingAutomatedPromptsRef.current = true;
   462→
   463→      const rawResults = await aiGeneratePrompts({
   464→        overallPromptText: "Make me more prompts like this.",
   465→        numberToGenerate: imagesPerPrompt,
   466→        existingPrompts: activePrompts.map(p => ({ id: p.id, text: p.fullPrompt, shortText: p.shortPrompt })),
   467→        addSummaryForNewPrompts: true,
   468→        replaceCurrentPrompts: true,
   469→        temperature: 0.8,
   470→        rulesToRememberText: '',
   471→      });
   472→
   473→      const newPrompts: PromptEntry[] = rawResults.map(item => ({
   474→        id: item.id,
   475→        fullPrompt: item.text,
   476→        shortPrompt: item.shortText || item.text.substring(0, 30) + (item.text.length > 30 ? "..." : ""),
   477→      }));
   478→
   479→      setPrompts(newPrompts);
   480→
   481→      const taskParams = getTaskParams(newPrompts, { imagesPerPromptOverride: promptMultiplier });
   482→      if (!taskParams) return;
   483→
   484→      onGenerate(taskParams);
   485→    } catch (error) {
   486→      handleError(error, { context: 'useFormSubmission.handleNewPromptsLikeExisting', toastTitle: 'Failed to generate prompts. Please try again.' });
   487→    } finally {
   488→      isGeneratingAutomatedPromptsRef.current = false;
   489→    }
   490→  }, [
   491→    prompts,
   492→    styleReferenceImageGeneration,
   493→    generationSourceRef,
   494→    imagesPerPrompt,
   495→    promptMultiplier,
   496→    getTaskParams,
   497→    onGenerate,
   498→    aiGeneratePrompts,
   499→    setPrompts,
   500→  ]);
   501→
   502→  // ============================================================================
   503→  // Return
   504→  // ============================================================================
   505→
   506→  return {
   507→    getTaskParams,
   508→    handleSubmit,
   509→    handleGenerateAndQueue,
   510→    handleUseExistingPrompts,
   511→    handleNewPromptsLikeExisting,
   512→    isGeneratingAutomatedPrompts: isGeneratingAutomatedPromptsRef.current,
   513→  };
   514→}
   515→

    77→  joinClipsSuccess: boolean;
    78→  joinValidationData: JoinValidationData;
    79→
    80→  // Handlers
    81→  handleJoinSegments: () => Promise<void>;
    82→  handleRestoreJoinDefaults: () => void;
    83→}
    84→
    85→export function useJoinSegmentsHandler({
    86→  projectId,
    87→  selectedProjectId,
    88→  selectedShotId,
    89→  effectiveAspectRatio,
    90→  audioUrl,
    91→  joinSegmentSlots,
    92→  joinSelectedParent,
    93→  joinLoraManager,
    94→  joinSettings,
    95→}: UseJoinSegmentsHandlerProps): UseJoinSegmentsHandlerReturn {
    96→  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
    97→  const { addIncomingTask, removeIncomingTask } = useIncomingTasks();
    98→  const { data: taskStatusCounts } = useTaskStatusCounts(selectedProjectId);
    99→
   100→  // Local state
   101→  const [isJoiningClips, setIsJoiningClips] = useState(false);
   102→  const [joinClipsSuccess, setJoinClipsSuccess] = useState(false);
   103→
   104→  // Calculate validation data from segment slots
   105→  const joinValidationData = useMemo(() => {
   106→    // Count segments that are in valid slots AND have a location (completed videos)
   107→    const readySlots = joinSegmentSlots.filter(
   108→      slot => slot.type === 'child' && Boolean(slot.child?.location)
   109→    );
   110→
   111→    if (readySlots.length < 2) {
   112→      return { shortestClipFrames: undefined, videoCount: readySlots.length };
   113→    }
   114→
   115→    // Get frame counts from video params or metadata
   116→    const frameCounts = readySlots.map(slot => {
   117→      const child = slot.child;
   118→      const params = child?.params as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
   119→      const metadata = child?.metadata as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
   120→      return (params?.frame_count as number) || (params?.num_frames as number) || (metadata?.frame_count as number) || (metadata?.frameCount as number) || 61;
   121→    });
   122→
   123→    return {
   124→      shortestClipFrames: Math.min(...frameCounts),
   125→      videoCount: readySlots.length,
   126→    };
   127→  }, [joinSegmentSlots]);
   128→
   129→  // Main join segments handler
   130→  const handleJoinSegments = useCallback(async () => {
   131→    if (!projectId || joinValidationData.videoCount < 2) {
   132→      toast.error('Need at least 2 completed video segments to join');
   133→      return;
   134→    }
   135→
   136→    const {
   137→      prompt: joinPrompt,
   138→      negativePrompt: joinNegativePrompt,
   139→      contextFrameCount: joinContextFrames,
   140→      gapFrameCount: joinGapFrames,
   141→      replaceMode: joinReplaceMode,
   142→      keepBridgingImages: joinKeepBridgingImages,
   143→      enhancePrompt: joinEnhancePrompt,
   144→      motionMode: joinMotionMode,
   145→      phaseConfig: joinPhaseConfig,
   146→      selectedPhasePresetId: joinSelectedPhasePresetId,
   147→    } = joinSettings;
   148→
   149→    // Add incoming task immediately for instant TasksPane feedback
   150→    const taskLabel = joinPrompt
   151→      ? (joinPrompt.length > 50 ? joinPrompt.substring(0, 50) + '...' : joinPrompt)
   152→      : `Join ${joinValidationData.videoCount} segments`;
   153→    const currentBaseline = taskStatusCounts?.processing ?? 0;
   154→    const incomingTaskId = addIncomingTask({
   155→      taskType: 'join_clips_orchestrator',
   156→      label: taskLabel,
   157→      baselineCount: currentBaseline,
   158→    });
   159→
   160→    setIsJoiningClips(true);
   161→
   162→    try {
   163→      // Get ordered segments from segmentSlots (already sorted by pair position)
   164→      const orderedSegments = joinSegmentSlots
   165→        .filter((slot): slot is { type: 'child'; child: GenerationRow; index: number } =>
   166→          slot.type === 'child' && Boolean(slot.child?.location)
   167→        )
   168→        .map(slot => slot.child);
   169→
   170→      // Fetch fresh URLs from database
   171→      const videoIds = orderedSegments.map(v => v.id).filter(Boolean);
   172→      const { data: freshVideos, error: fetchError } = await supabase
   173→        .from('generations')
   174→        .select('id, location')
   175→        .in('id', videoIds);
   176→
   177→      if (fetchError) {
   178→        handleError(fetchError, { context: 'JoinSegments', showToast: false });
   179→        throw new Error('Failed to fetch video URLs');
   180→      }
   181→
   182→      const freshUrlMap = new Map(freshVideos?.map(v => [v.id, v.location]) || []);
   183→
   184→      const clips = orderedSegments.map((video, index) => ({
   185→        url: freshUrlMap.get(video.id) || video.location || '',
   186→        name: `Segment ${index + 1}`,
   187→      })).filter(c => c.url);
   188→
   189→      // Convert selected LoRAs
   190→      const lorasForTask = joinLoraManager.selectedLoras.map(lora => ({
   191→        path: lora.path,
   192→        strength: lora.strength,
   193→      }));
   194→
   195→      // Parse resolution from aspect ratio
   196→      let resolutionTuple: [number, number] | undefined;
   197→      if (effectiveAspectRatio && ASPECT_RATIO_TO_RESOLUTION[effectiveAspectRatio]) {
   198→        const res = ASPECT_RATIO_TO_RESOLUTION[effectiveAspectRatio];
   199→        const [w, h] = res.split('x').map(Number);
   200→        if (w && h) {
   201→          resolutionTuple = [w, h];
   202→        }
   203→      }
   204→
   205→      console.log('[JoinSegments] Creating join task:', {
   206→        clipCount: clips.length,
   207→        prompt: joinPrompt,
   208→        contextFrames: joinContextFrames,
   209→        gapFrames: joinGapFrames,
   210→      });
   211→
   212→      await createJoinClipsTask({
   213→        project_id: projectId,
   214→        shot_id: selectedShotId,
   215→        clips,
   216→        prompt: joinPrompt,
   217→        negative_prompt: joinNegativePrompt,
   218→        context_frame_count: joinContextFrames,
   219→        gap_frame_count: joinGapFrames,
   220→        replace_mode: joinReplaceMode,
   221→        keep_bridging_images: joinKeepBridgingImages,
   222→        enhance_prompt: joinEnhancePrompt,
   223→        model: joinSettings.model,
   224→        num_inference_steps: joinSettings.numInferenceSteps,
   225→        guidance_scale: joinSettings.guidanceScale,
   226→        seed: joinSettings.seed,
   227→        parent_generation_id: joinSelectedParent?.id,
   228→        tool_type: 'travel-between-images',
   229→        use_input_video_resolution: false,
   230→        use_input_video_fps: false,
   231→        motion_mode: joinMotionMode,
   232→        selected_phase_preset_id: joinSelectedPhasePresetId ?? null,
   233→        ...(lorasForTask.length > 0 && { loras: lorasForTask }),
   234→        ...(resolutionTuple && { resolution: resolutionTuple }),
   235→        ...(audioUrl && { audio_url: audioUrl }),
   236→        ...(joinPhaseConfig && { phase_config: joinPhaseConfig }),
   237→      });
   238→
   239→      removeIncomingTask(incomingTaskId);
   240→      setJoinClipsSuccess(true);
   241→      setTimeout(() => setJoinClipsSuccess(false), 3000);
   242→      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ [REDACTED] });
   243→    } catch (error: unknown) {
   244→      handleError(error, { context: 'JoinSegments', toastTitle: getErrorMessage(error) || 'Failed to create join task' });
   245→      removeIncomingTask(incomingTaskId);
   246→    } finally {
   247→      setIsJoiningClips(false);
   248→    }
   249→  }, [
   250→    projectId,
   251→    selectedShotId,
   252→    joinValidationData.videoCount,
   253→    joinSegmentSlots,
   254→    joinSettings,
   255→    joinLoraManager.selectedLoras,
   256→    effectiveAspectRatio,
   257→    audioUrl,
   258→    joinSelectedParent,
   259→    queryClient,
   260→    taskStatusCounts,
   261→    addIncomingTask,
   262→    removeIncomingTask,
   263→  ]);
   264→
   265→  // Handler to restore join clips defaults
   266→  const handleRestoreJoinDefaults = useCallback(() => {
   267→    const defaults = joinClipsSettings.defaults;
   268→    let context = defaults.contextFrameCount;
   269→    let gap = defaults.gapFrameCount;
   270→
   271→    // Scale down proportionally if constraint is violated
   272→    const shortestFrames = joinValidationData.shortestClipFrames;
   273→    if (shortestFrames && shortestFrames > 0) {
   274→      const framesNeeded = gap + 2 * context;
   275→      if (framesNeeded > shortestFrames) {
   276→        const scale = shortestFrames / framesNeeded;
   277→        context = Math.max(4, Math.floor(context * scale));
   278→        gap = Math.max(1, Math.floor(gap * scale));
   279→      }
   280→    }
   281→
   282→    joinSettings.updateFields({
   283→      prompt: defaults.prompt,
   284→      negativePrompt: defaults.negativePrompt,
   285→      contextFrameCount: context,
   286→      gapFrameCount: gap,
   287→      replaceMode: defaults.replaceMode,
   288→      keepBridgingImages: defaults.keepBridgingImages,
   289→      enhancePrompt: defaults.enhancePrompt,
   290→      motionMode: defaults.motionMode,
   291→      phaseConfig: DEFAULT_VACE_PHASE_CONFIG,
   292→      selectedPhasePresetId: BUILTIN_VACE_DEFAULT_ID,
   293→      randomSeed: defaults.randomSeed,
   294→      selectedLoras: [],
   295→    });
   296→  }, [joinSettings, joinValidationData.shortestClipFrames]);
   297→
   298→  return {
   299→    isJoiningClips,
   300→    joinClipsSuccess,
   301→    joinValidationData,
   302→    handleJoinSegments,
   303→    handleRestoreJoinDefaults,
   304→  };
   305→}
   306→

   118→/**

   125→function buildJoinClipsPhaseConfig(
   126→  userLoras: Array<{ path: string; strength: number }> = []
   127→): PhaseConfig {

   129→  const defaultLora: PhaseLoraConfig = {

   142→  return {

   154→          defaultLora,

   180→/**
   181→ * Builds the ordered clip sequence from the provided parameters.
   182→ */
   183→function buildClipSequence(params: JoinClipsTaskParams): JoinClipDescriptor[] {
   184→  if (params.clips && params.clips.length > 0) {
   185→    return params.clips.map(clip => ({
   186→      url: clip.url,
   187→      ...(clip.name ? { name: clip.name } : {}),
   188→    }));
   189→  }
   190→
   191→  const legacySequence: JoinClipDescriptor[] = [];
   192→
   193→  if (params.starting_video_path) {
   194→    legacySequence.push({ url: params.starting_video_path });
   195→  }
   196→
   197→  if (params.intermediate_video_paths?.length) {
   198→    legacySequence.push(
   199→      ...params.intermediate_video_paths.map(path => ({ url: path }))
   200→    );
   201→  }
   202→
   203→  if (params.ending_video_path) {
   204→    legacySequence.push({ url: params.ending_video_path });
   205→  }
   206→
   207→  return legacySequence;
   208→}
   209→
   210→/**
   211→ * Validates join clips task parameters and returns the normalized clip sequence
   212→ * 
   213→ * @param params - Parameters to validate
   214→ * @throws TaskValidationError if validation fails
   215→ */
   216→function validateJoinClipsParams(params: JoinClipsTaskParams): JoinClipDescriptor[] {
   217→  validateRequiredFields(params, [
   218→    'project_id'
   219→  ]);
   220→
   221→  const clipSequence = buildClipSequence(params);
   222→
   223→  if (clipSequence.length < 2) {
   224→    throw new TaskValidationError("At least two clips are required to create a join", 'clips');
   225→  }
   226→
   227→  clipSequence.forEach((clip, index) => {
   228→    if (!clip.url || clip.url.trim() === '') {
   229→      throw new TaskValidationError(`Clip at position ${index} is missing a URL`, 'clips');
   230→    }
   231→  });
   232→
   233→  const joinsCount = clipSequence.length - 1;
   234→
   235→  if (params.per_join_settings && params.per_join_settings.length > joinsCount) {
   236→    throw new TaskValidationError(
   237→      "per_join_settings length cannot exceed the number of joins",
   238→      'per_join_settings'
   239→    );
   240→  }
   241→
   242→  return clipSequence;
   243→}
   244→
   245→/**
   246→ * Converts an array of LoRA descriptors into the additional_loras map format.
   247→ */
   248→function mapLorasToRecord(loras: Array<{ path: string; strength: number }>): Record<string, number> {
   249→  return loras.reduce<Record<string, number>>((acc, lora) => {
   250→    if (lora.path) {
   251→      acc[lora.path] = lora.strength;
   252→    }
   253→    return acc;
   254→  }, {});
   255→}

   257→/**
   258→ * Builds the orchestrator payload for join clips
   259→ *

   262→ * @param runId - Generated run ID
   263→ * @param orchestratorTaskId - Generated orchestrator task ID
   264→ * @returns Processed orchestrator payload
   265→ */
   266→function buildJoinClipsPayload(
   267→  params: JoinClipsTaskParams,
   268→  clipSequence: JoinClipDescriptor[],
   269→  runId: string,
   270→  orchestratorTaskId: string
   271→): Record<string, unknown> {
   272→  // Use provided phase config (advanced mode) or build from LoRAs (basic mode)
   273→  const phaseConfig = params.phase_config || buildJoinClipsPhaseConfig(params.loras || []);
   274→  
   275→  const orchestratorDetails: Record<string, unknown> = {
   276→    orchestrator_task_id: orchestratorTaskId,
   277→    clip_list: clipSequence.map(clip => ({
   278→      url: clip.url,
   279→      ...(clip.name ? { name: clip.name } : {}),
   280→    })),
   281→    run_id: runId,
   282→    shot_id: params.shot_id ?? undefined, // For "Visit Shot" button in TasksPane
   283→    prompt: params.prompt ?? TASK_DEFAULTS.prompt,
   284→    gap_frame_count: params.gap_frame_count ?? TASK_DEFAULTS.gap_frame_count,
   285→    context_frame_count: params.context_frame_count ?? TASK_DEFAULTS.context_frame_count,
   286→    replace_mode: params.replace_mode ?? TASK_DEFAULTS.replace_mode,
   287→    keep_bridging_images: params.keep_bridging_images, // Optional, defaults to undefined (backend handles default)
   288→    enhance_prompt: params.enhance_prompt ?? TASK_DEFAULTS.enhance_prompt,
   289→    model: params.model ?? TASK_DEFAULTS.model,
   290→    num_inference_steps: params.num_inference_steps ?? TASK_DEFAULTS.num_inference_steps,
   291→    guidance_scale: params.guidance_scale ?? TASK_DEFAULTS.guidance_scale,
   292→    seed: params.seed ?? TASK_DEFAULTS.seed,
   293→    negative_prompt: params.negative_prompt ?? TASK_DEFAULTS.negative_prompt,
   294→    priority: params.priority ?? TASK_DEFAULTS.priority,
   295→    parent_generation_id: params.parent_generation_id,
   296→    // UNIFIED: Always include phase_config and advanced_mode for consistent backend processing
   297→    phase_config: phaseConfig,
   298→    advanced_mode: true,
   299→    // Motion settings for UI state restoration
   300→    motion_mode: params.motion_mode ?? TASK_DEFAULTS.motion_mode,
   301→    selected_phase_preset_id: params.selected_phase_preset_id ?? TASK_DEFAULTS.selected_phase_preset_id,
   302→  };
   303→
   304→  if (params.resolution) {
   305→    orchestratorDetails.resolution = params.resolution;
   306→  }
   307→
   308→  if (params.fps !== undefined) {
   309→    orchestratorDetails.fps = params.fps;
   310→  }
   311→
   312→  if (params.use_input_video_resolution !== undefined) {
   313→    orchestratorDetails.use_input_video_resolution = params.use_input_video_resolution;
   314→  }
   315→
   316→  if (params.use_input_video_fps !== undefined) {
   317→    orchestratorDetails.use_input_video_fps = params.use_input_video_fps;
   318→  }
   319→
   320→  if (params.vid2vid_init_strength !== undefined && params.vid2vid_init_strength > 0) {
   321→    orchestratorDetails.vid2vid_init_strength = params.vid2vid_init_strength;
   322→  }
   323→
   324→  if (params.loop_first_clip) {
   325→    orchestratorDetails.loop_first_clip = true;
   326→  }
   327→
   328→  // Pass based_on for variant creation when looping a clip from "Add to Join"
   329→  if (params.based_on) {
   330→    orchestratorDetails.based_on = params.based_on;
   331→  }
   332→
   333→  // Keep additional_loras for backward compatibility, but LoRAs are now primarily in phase_config
   334→  if (params.loras && params.loras.length > 0) {
   335→    orchestratorDetails.additional_loras = mapLorasToRecord(params.loras);
   336→  }
   337→  
   338→  // Video edit mode - for regenerating portions of a single video
   339→  if (params.video_edit_mode) {
   340→    orchestratorDetails.video_edit_mode = true;
   341→    orchestratorDetails.source_video_url = params.source_video_url;
   342→    orchestratorDetails.source_video_fps = params.source_video_fps;
   343→    orchestratorDetails.source_video_duration = params.source_video_duration;
   344→    orchestratorDetails.source_video_total_frames = params.source_video_total_frames;
   345→    orchestratorDetails.portions_to_regenerate = params.portions_to_regenerate;
   346→  }
   347→
   348→  // Audio to mix into the final joined video
   349→  if (params.audio_url) {
   350→    orchestratorDetails.audio_url = params.audio_url;
   351→  }
   352→
   353→  const totalJoins = Math.max(clipSequence.length - 1, 0);
   354→  if (totalJoins > 0) {
   355→    const perJoinOverrides: Record<string, unknown>[] = [];
   356→    let hasOverrides = false;
   357→
   358→    for (let index = 0; index < totalJoins; index++) {
   359→      const override = params.per_join_settings?.[index];
   360→      const joinSettings: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   361→
   362→      if (override) {
   363→        if (override.prompt !== undefined) joinSettings.prompt = override.prompt;
   364→        if (override.gap_frame_count !== undefined) joinSettings.gap_frame_count = override.gap_frame_count;
   365→        if (override.context_frame_count !== undefined) joinSettings.context_frame_count = override.context_frame_count;
   366→        if (override.replace_mode !== undefined) joinSettings.replace_mode = override.replace_mode;
   367→        if (override.model !== undefined) joinSettings.model = override.model;
   368→        if (override.num_inference_steps !== undefined) joinSettings.num_inference_steps = override.num_inference_steps;
   369→        if (override.guidance_scale !== undefined) joinSettings.guidance_scale = override.guidance_scale;
   370→        if (override.seed !== undefined) joinSettings.seed = override.seed;
   371→        if (override.negative_prompt !== undefined) joinSettings.negative_prompt = override.negative_prompt;
   372→        if (override.priority !== undefined) joinSettings.priority = override.priority;
   373→        if (override.resolution !== undefined) joinSettings.resolution = override.resolution;
   374→        if (override.fps !== undefined) joinSettings.fps = override.fps;
   375→        if (override.loras && override.loras.length > 0) {
   376→          joinSettings.additional_loras = mapLorasToRecord(override.loras);
   377→        }
   378→      }
   379→
   380→      if (Object.keys(joinSettings).length > 0) {
   381→        hasOverrides = true;
   382→        perJoinOverrides.push(joinSettings);
   383→      } else {
   384→        perJoinOverrides.push({});
   385→      }
   386→    }
   387→
   388→    if (hasOverrides) {
   389→      orchestratorDetails.per_join_settings = perJoinOverrides;
   390→    }
   391→  }
   392→
   393→  return {
   394→    orchestrator_details: orchestratorDetails,
   395→    parent_generation_id: params.parent_generation_id,
   396→    // Only include tool_type if explicitly passed (Join Clips page passes it, ShotEditor doesn't)
   397→    ...(params.tool_type && { tool_type: params.tool_type }),
   398→  };
   399→}
   400→
   401→/**
   402→ * Creates a join clips task using the unified approach
   403→ * 
   404→ * @param params - Join clips task parameters
   405→ * @returns Promise resolving to the created task
   406→ */
   407→export async function createJoinClipsTask(params: JoinClipsTaskParams): Promise<TaskCreationResult> {
   408→  console.log("[createJoinClipsTask] Creating task with params:", params);
   409→
   410→  try {
   411→    // 1. Validate parameters
   412→    const clipSequence = validateJoinClipsParams(params);
   413→
   414→    // 2. Generate IDs for orchestrator payload
   415→    const orchestratorTaskId = generateTaskId("join_clips_orchestrator");
   416→    const runId = params.run_id ?? generateRunId();
   417→
   418→    // 3. Build orchestrator payload
   419→    const orchestratorPayload = buildJoinClipsPayload(
   420→      params, 
   421→      clipSequence,
   422→      runId,
   423→      orchestratorTaskId
   424→    );
   425→
   426→    // 4. Create task using unified create-task function
   427→    const result = await createTask({
   428→      project_id: params.project_id,
   429→      task_type: 'join_clips_orchestrator',
   430→      params: orchestratorPayload
   431→    });
   432→
   433→    console.log("[createJoinClipsTask] Task created successfully:", result);
   434→    return result;
   435→
   436→  } catch (error) {
   437→    handleError(error, { context: 'JoinClips', showToast: false });
   438→    throw error;
   439→  }
   440→}
   441→
   442→// TaskValidationError is used internally - import from taskCreation.ts if needed externally
   443→
   444→

    48→// Convert time selections to frame ranges with per-segment settings
    49→function selectionsToFrameRanges(
    50→  selections: PortionSelection[], 
    51→  fps: number, 
    52→  totalDuration: number,
    53→  globalGapFrameCount: number,
    54→  globalPrompt: string
    55→): FrameRangeSelection[] {
    56→  const totalFrames = Math.round(totalDuration * fps);
    57→  
    58→  return selections.map(selection => {
    59→    const startFrame = Math.max(0, Math.round(selection.start * fps));
    60→    const endFrame = Math.min(totalFrames, Math.round(selection.end * fps));
    61→    return {
    62→      start_frame: startFrame,
    63→      end_frame: endFrame,
    64→      start_time: selection.start,
    65→      end_time: selection.end,
    66→      frame_count: endFrame - startFrame,
    67→      gap_frame_count: selection.gapFrameCount ?? globalGapFrameCount,
    68→      prompt: selection.prompt || globalPrompt,
    69→    };
    70→  });
    71→}
    72→
    73→interface InlineEditVideoViewProps {
    74→  media: GenerationRow;
    75→  onClose: () => void;
    76→  onVideoSaved?: (newVideoUrl: string) => Promise<void>;

    78→  initialSegments?: PortionSelection[];
    79→  onSegmentsChange?: (segments: PortionSelection[]) => void;
    80→}

   312→  const handleAddSelection = useCallback(() => {
   313→    const selectionWidth = 0.1; // 10% of video duration
   314→    const gap = 0.1; // 10% gap after last selection
   315→    
   316→    // Find the last selection's end position
   317→    const sortedSelections = [...selections].sort((a, b) => a.end - b.end);
   318→    const lastSelection = sortedSelections[sortedSelections.length - 1];
   319→    
   320→    let newStart: number;
   321→    let newEnd: number;
   322→    
   323→    if (lastSelection) {
   324→      // Try to place 10% after the last selection
   325→      const afterStart = lastSelection.end + (videoDuration * gap);
   326→      const afterEnd = afterStart + (videoDuration * selectionWidth);
   327→      
   328→      if (afterEnd <= videoDuration) {
   329→        // There's space after
   330→        newStart = afterStart;
   331→        newEnd = afterEnd;
   332→      } else {
   333→        // No space after, try 10% before the first selection
   334→        const firstSelection = sortedSelections[0];
   335→        const beforeEnd = firstSelection.start - (videoDuration * gap);
   336→        const beforeStart = beforeEnd - (videoDuration * selectionWidth);

   339→          newStart = beforeStart;
   340→          newEnd = beforeEnd;
   341→        } else {
   342→          // No space before either, just overlap with default

   344→          newEnd = videoDuration * 0.5;
   345→        }
   346→      }
   347→    } else {
   348→      // No existing selections, use default 10%-20%

   350→      newEnd = videoDuration * 0.2;

   353→    // Calculate gap frames based on actual frame difference

   366→      end: newEnd,

   368→      prompt: '',

    10→import type { VideoClip, TransitionPrompt } from '../types';
    11→import type { useJoinClipsSettings } from './useJoinClipsSettings';

    23→}
    24→
    25→export function useJoinClipsGenerate({
    26→  selectedProjectId,
    27→  clips,
    28→  transitionPrompts,
    29→  joinSettings,
    30→  loraManager,
    31→  projectAspectRatio,
    32→  validationResult,
    33→}: UseJoinClipsGenerateParams) {
    34→  const { toast } = useToast();
    35→  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
    36→
    37→  const [showSuccessState, setShowSuccessState] = useState(false);
    38→
    39→  const {
    40→    prompt: globalPrompt,
    41→    negativePrompt,
    42→    contextFrameCount,
    43→    gapFrameCount,
    44→    replaceMode,
    45→    keepBridgingImages,
    46→    useIndividualPrompts,
    47→    enhancePrompt,
    48→    useInputVideoResolution,
    49→    useInputVideoFps,
    50→    noisedInputVideo,
    51→    loopFirstClip,
    52→    motionMode,
    53→    phaseConfig,
    54→  } = joinSettings.settings;
    55→
    56→  const generateJoinClipsMutation = useMutation({
    57→    mutationFn: async () => {
    58→      if (!selectedProjectId) throw new Error('No project selected');
    59→
    60→      const validClips = clips.filter(c => c.url);
    61→
    62→      const isLooping = loopFirstClip && validClips.length === 1;
    63→      if (!isLooping && validClips.length < 2) {
    64→        throw new Error('At least 2 clips with videos required');
    65→      }
    66→
    67→      const clipsForTask = isLooping
    68→        ? [{ url: validClips[0].url }, { url: validClips[0].url }]
    69→        : validClips.map(clip => ({ url: clip.url }));
    70→
    71→      const perJoinSettings = validClips.slice(1).map((clip) => {
    72→        let finalPrompt = '';
    73→
    74→        if (useIndividualPrompts) {
    75→          const individualPrompt = transitionPrompts.find(p => p.id === clip.id)?.prompt || '';
    76→          if (individualPrompt && globalPrompt) {
    77→            finalPrompt = `${individualPrompt}. ${globalPrompt}`;
    78→          } else if (individualPrompt) {
    79→            finalPrompt = individualPrompt;
    80→          } else {
    81→            finalPrompt = globalPrompt;
    82→          }
    83→        } else {
    84→          finalPrompt = globalPrompt;
    85→        }
    86→
    87→        return { prompt: finalPrompt };
    88→      });
    89→
    90→      const lorasForTask = loraManager.selectedLoras.map(lora => ({
    91→        path: lora.path,
    92→        strength: lora.strength,
    93→      }));
    94→
    95→      let resolutionTuple: [number, number] | undefined;
    96→      if (projectAspectRatio) {
    97→        const resolutionStr = ASPECT_RATIO_TO_RESOLUTION[projectAspectRatio];
    98→        if (resolutionStr) {
    99→          const [width, height] = resolutionStr.split('x').map(Number);
   100→          if (width && height) {
   101→            resolutionTuple = [width, height];
   102→          }
   103→        }
   104→      }
   105→
   106→      const taskParams: import('@/shared/lib/tasks/joinClips').JoinClipsTaskParams = {
   107→        project_id: selectedProjectId,
   108→        clips: clipsForTask,
   109→        per_join_settings: perJoinSettings,
   110→        context_frame_count: contextFrameCount,
   111→        gap_frame_count: gapFrameCount,
   112→        replace_mode: replaceMode,
   113→        keep_bridging_images: keepBridgingImages ?? false,
   114→        enhance_prompt: enhancePrompt,
   115→        model: (joinSettings.settings.model?.startsWith('wan_2_2_')
   116→          ? joinSettings.settings.model
   117→          : VACE_GENERATION_DEFAULTS.model),
   118→        num_inference_steps: joinSettings.settings.numInferenceSteps,
   119→        guidance_scale: joinSettings.settings.guidanceScale,
   120→        seed: joinSettings.settings.seed,
   121→        negative_prompt: negativePrompt,
   122→        priority: joinSettings.settings.priority,
   123→        use_input_video_resolution: useInputVideoResolution,
   124→        use_input_video_fps: useInputVideoFps,
   125→        ...(motionMode === 'advanced' && phaseConfig
   126→          ? { phase_config: phaseConfig }
   127→          : lorasForTask.length > 0 && { loras: lorasForTask }
   128→        ),
   129→        ...(resolutionTuple && { resolution: resolutionTuple }),
   130→        ...(noisedInputVideo > 0 && { vid2vid_init_strength: noisedInputVideo }),
   131→        ...(isLooping && { loop_first_clip: true }),
   132→        ...(isLooping && validClips[0].generationId && { based_on: validClips[0].generationId }),
   133→        motion_mode: motionMode,
   134→        selected_phase_preset_id: joinSettings.settings.selectedPhasePresetId,
   135→        tool_type: 'join-clips',
   136→      };
   137→
   138→      const result = await createJoinClipsTask(taskParams);
   139→      return result;
   140→    },
   141→    onSuccess: () => {
   142→      toast({
   143→        title: 'Task created',
   144→        description: 'Your join clips task has been queued',
   145→      });
   146→
   147→      setShowSuccessState(true);
   148→      setTimeout(() => setShowSuccessState(false), 3000);
   149→
   150→      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ [REDACTED] });

    18→export interface UseUpscaleReturn {
    19→  isUpscaling: boolean;
    20→  upscaleSuccess: boolean;
    21→  handleUpscale: (settings: ImageUpscaleSettings) => Promise<void>;
    22→  /** Update the active variant info so handleUpscale uses the correct image.
    23→   *  Call this after variant data becomes available (e.g. from useSharedLightboxState). */
    24→  setActiveVariant: (location: string | null | undefined, id: string | null | undefined) => void;
    25→  // Kept for backwards compatibility with other components
    26→  showingUpscaled: boolean;
    27→  handleToggleUpscaled: () => void;
    28→  effectiveImageUrl: string;
    29→  sourceUrlForTasks: string;
    30→  isPendingUpscale: boolean;
    31→  hasUpscaledVersion: boolean;
    32→  upscaledUrl: string | null;
    33→}
    34→
    35→/**
    36→ * Hook for managing image upscaling functionality
    37→ *
    38→ * Simple pattern matching other edit tasks (inpaint, etc.):
    39→ * 1. Click button → isUpscaling = true
    40→ * 2. Task created → upscaleSuccess = true briefly
    41→ * 3. Reset after timeout
    42→ *
    43→ * Variant tracking: call setActiveVariant() after variant data is available
    44→ * so handleUpscale upscales the currently-viewed variant (not the base generation).
    45→ */
    46→export const useUpscale = ({
    47→  media,
    48→  selectedProjectId,
    49→  isVideo,
    50→  shotId,
    51→}: UseUpscaleProps): UseUpscaleReturn => {
    52→  const [isUpscaling, setIsUpscaling] = useState(false);
    53→  const [upscaleSuccess, setUpscaleSuccess] = useState(false);
    54→  const { addIncomingTask, removeIncomingTask } = useIncomingTasks();
    55→  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
    56→
    57→  // Get media URL
    58→  const mediaUrl = media ? (getMediaUrl(media) || media.imageUrl || '') : '';
    59→
    60→  // Refs for active variant info — set via setActiveVariant after variant data loads.
    61→  // Using refs so the handleUpscale callback always reads the latest values
    62→  // without needing them in useCallback deps (avoids hook-ordering issues).
    63→  const activeVariantLocationRef = useRef<string | null | undefined>(null);
    64→  const activeVariantIdRef = useRef<string | null | undefined>(null);
    65→
    66→  const setActiveVariant = useCallback((location: string | null | undefined, id: string | null | undefined) => {
    67→    activeVariantLocationRef.current = location;
    68→    activeVariantIdRef.current = id;
    69→  }, []);
    70→
    71→  const handleUpscale = useCallback(async (settings: ImageUpscaleSettings) => {
    72→    if (!media || !selectedProjectId || isVideo) {
    73→      return;
    74→    }
    75→
    76→    // Use active variant's location if viewing a variant, otherwise use base generation URL
    77→    const activeVariantLocation = activeVariantLocationRef.current;
    78→    const activeVariantId = activeVariantIdRef.current;
    79→    const effectiveImageUrl = activeVariantLocation || mediaUrl;
    80→
    81→    setIsUpscaling(true);
    82→    const incomingTaskId = addIncomingTask({
    83→      taskType: 'image-upscale',
    84→      label: `Upscale ${settings.scaleFactor}x`,
    85→    });
    86→    try {
    87→      if (!effectiveImageUrl) {
    88→        throw new Error('No image URL available');
    89→      }
    90→
    91→      const actualGenerationId = getGenerationId(media);
    92→
    93→      console.log('[ImageUpscale] Creating task:', {
    94→        actualGenerationId: actualGenerationId?.substring(0, 8),
    95→        effectiveImageUrl: effectiveImageUrl.substring(0, 50),
    96→        isUsingVariant: !!activeVariantLocation,
    97→        activeVariantId: activeVariantId?.substring(0, 8),
    98→        settings,
    99→      });
   100→
   101→      await createImageUpscaleTask({
   102→        project_id: selectedProjectId,
   103→        image_url: effectiveImageUrl,
   104→        generation_id: actualGenerationId,
   105→        source_variant_id: activeVariantId || undefined,
   106→        scale_factor: settings.scaleFactor,
   107→        noise_scale: settings.noiseScale,
   108→        shot_id: shotId,
   109→      });
   110→
   111→      console.log('[ImageUpscale] Task created successfully');
   112→
   113→      // Show success state briefly
   114→      setUpscaleSuccess(true);
   115→      setTimeout(() => {
   116→        setUpscaleSuccess(false);
   117→      }, 2000);
   118→
   119→    } catch (error) {
   120→      handleError(error, { context: 'useUpscale', toastTitle: 'Failed to create enhance task' });
   121→    } finally {
   122→      await queryClient.refetchQueries({ [REDACTED] });
   123→      await queryClient.refetchQueries({ [REDACTED] });
   124→      removeIncomingTask(incomingTaskId);
   125→      setIsUpscaling(false);
   126→    }
   127→  }, [media, selectedProjectId, isVideo, mediaUrl, shotId, addIncomingTask, removeIncomingTask, queryClient]);
   128→
   129→  return {
   130→    isUpscaling,
   131→    upscaleSuccess,
   132→    handleUpscale,
   133→    setActiveVariant,
   134→    // Backwards compatibility - these features removed but APIs kept
   135→    showingUpscaled: false,
   136→    handleToggleUpscaled: () => {},
   137→    effectiveImageUrl: mediaUrl,
   138→    sourceUrlForTasks: mediaUrl,
   139→    isPendingUpscale: false,
   140→    hasUpscaledVersion: false,
   141→    upscaledUrl: null,
   142→  };
   143→};
   144→

    42→ * Hook for generating inpaint tasks from repositioned images.
    43→ * Creates transformed image with mask for the dead space and submits to inpaint API.
    44→ */
    45→export function useRepositionTaskCreation({
    46→  media,
    47→  selectedProjectId,
    48→  shotId,
    49→  toolTypeOverride,
    50→  imageDimensions,
    51→  loras,
    52→  inpaintPrompt,
    53→  inpaintNumGenerations,
    54→  transform,
    55→  hasTransformChanges,
    56→  createAsGeneration,
    57→  advancedSettings,
    58→  activeVariantId,
    59→  qwenEditModel,
    60→  createTransformedCanvas,
    61→}: UseRepositionTaskCreationProps): UseRepositionTaskCreationReturn {
    62→  const [isGeneratingReposition, setIsGeneratingReposition] = useState(false);
    63→  const [repositionGenerateSuccess, setRepositionGenerateSuccess] = useState(false);
    64→  const { addIncomingTask, removeIncomingTask } = useIncomingTasks();
    65→  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
    66→
    67→  // Generate transformed image and mask, then create inpaint task
    68→  const handleGenerateReposition = useCallback(async () => {
    69→    if (!selectedProjectId || !imageDimensions) {
    70→      toast.error('Missing project or image dimensions');
    71→      return;
    72→    }
    73→
    74→    if (!hasTransformChanges) {
    75→      toast.error('Please move, scale, or rotate the image first');
    76→      return;
    77→    }
    78→
    79→    const incomingTaskId = addIncomingTask({
    80→      taskType: 'image_inpaint',
    81→      label: inpaintPrompt.trim() || 'Fill edges...',
    82→    });
    83→
    84→    setIsGeneratingReposition(true);
    85→
    86→    try {
    87→      console.log('[Reposition] Starting reposition generation...', {
    88→        mediaId: media.id,
    89→        prompt: inpaintPrompt,
    90→        numGenerations: inpaintNumGenerations,
    91→        transform
    92→      });
    93→
    94→      const transformedCanvas = await createTransformedCanvas();
    95→      const outputWidth = transformedCanvas.width;
    96→      const outputHeight = transformedCanvas.height;
    97→
    98→      // Generate mask from the transparent areas of the transformed image
    99→      const maskCanvas = generateMaskFromCanvas(transformedCanvas, {
   100→        alphaThreshold: 200,
   101→        dilationPixels: 3,
   102→      });
   103→
   104→      console.log('[Reposition] Generated transformed image and mask with dilated edges (alpha threshold: 200, dilation: 3px)');
   105→
   106→      // Create final image with green background to prevent anti-aliased edge artifacts
   107→      // The transparent canvas is needed for mask generation (alpha channel), but for the
   108→      // uploaded image we need a solid background so edge pixels blend properly
   109→      const finalImageCanvas = createCanvasWithBackground(transformedCanvas, '#00FF00');
   110→
   111→      // Convert canvases to blobs and upload
   112→      const transformedBlob = await new Promise<Blob>((resolve, reject) => {
   113→        finalImageCanvas.toBlob(blob => {
   114→          if (blob) resolve(blob);
   115→          else reject(new Error('Failed to create transformed image blob'));
   116→        }, 'image/png');
   117→      });
   118→
   119→      const maskBlob = await new Promise<Blob>((resolve, reject) => {
   120→        maskCanvas.toBlob(blob => {
   121→          if (blob) resolve(blob);
   122→          else reject(new Error('Failed to create mask blob'));
   123→        }, 'image/png');
   124→      });
   125→
   126→      // Upload transformed image and mask
   127→      const transformedFile = new File(
   128→        [transformedBlob],
   129→        `reposition_image_${media.id}_${Date.now()}.png`,
   130→        { type: 'image/png' }
   131→      );
   132→      const maskFile = new File(
   133→        [maskBlob],
   134→        `reposition_mask_${media.id}_${Date.now()}.png`,
   135→        { type: 'image/png' }
   136→      );
   137→
   138→      const [transformedUrl, maskUrl] = await Promise.all([
   139→        uploadImageToStorage(transformedFile),
   140→        uploadImageToStorage(maskFile)
   141→      ]);
   142→
   143→      console.log('[Reposition] Uploaded transformed image:', transformedUrl);
   144→      console.log('[Reposition] Uploaded mask:', maskUrl);
   145→
   146→      // Create inpaint task with transformed image and mask
   147→      const actualGenerationId = getGenerationId(media);
   148→
   149→      // Use a default prompt if none provided - describe filling in the edges
   150→      const effectivePrompt = inpaintPrompt.trim() || 'seamlessly extend and fill the edges matching the existing image style and content';
