   226→      }],
   227→    });
   228→  }, [effectiveLoras, onChange]);
   229→
   230→  const handleRemoveLora = useCallback((loraId: string) => {
   231→    const currentLoras = effectiveLoras;
   232→    onChange({
   233→      loras: currentLoras.filter(l => l.id !== loraId && l.path !== loraId),
   234→    });
   235→  }, [effectiveLoras, onChange]);
   236→
   237→  const handleLoraStrengthChange = useCallback((loraId: string, strength: number) => {
   238→    const currentLoras = effectiveLoras;
   239→    onChange({
   240→      loras: currentLoras.map(l =>
   241→        (l.id === loraId || l.path === loraId) ? { ...l, strength } : l
   242→      ),
   243→    });
   244→  }, [effectiveLoras, onChange]);
   245→
   246→  const handleFrameCountChange = useCallback((value: number) => {
   247→    const quantized = quantizeFrameCount(value, 9);
   248→    onChange({ numFrames: quantized });
   249→    onFrameCountChange?.(quantized);
   250→  }, [onChange, onFrameCountChange]);
   251→
   252→  const handleSaveAsShotDefaults = useCallback(async () => {
   253→    if (!onSaveAsShotDefaults) return;
   254→    setIsSavingDefaults(true);
   255→    setSaveDefaultsSuccess(false);
   256→    try {
   257→      const success = await onSaveAsShotDefaults();
   258→      if (success) {
   259→        setSaveDefaultsSuccess(true);
   260→        setTimeout(() => setSaveDefaultsSuccess(false), 2000);
   261→      }
   262→    } finally {
   263→      setIsSavingDefaults(false);
   264→    }
   265→  }, [onSaveAsShotDefaults]);
   266→
   267→  const handleSaveFieldAsDefault = useCallback(async (field: keyof SegmentSettings, value: SegmentSettings[keyof SegmentSettings]) => {
   268→    if (!onSaveFieldAsDefault) return;
   269→    setSavingField(field);
   270→    try {
   271→      const success = await onSaveFieldAsDefault(field, value);
   272→      if (success) {
   273→        await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 0));
   274→        onChange({ [field]: undefined } as Partial<SegmentSettings>);
   275→      }
   276→    } finally {
   277→      setSavingField(null);
   278→    }
   279→  }, [onSaveFieldAsDefault, onChange]);
   280→
   281→  // Drag and drop handlers for video upload
   282→  const handleDragOver = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
   283→    e.preventDefault();
   284→    e.stopPropagation();
   285→  }, []);
   286→
   287→  const handleDragEnter = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
   288→    e.preventDefault();
   289→    e.stopPropagation();
   290→    // Check if dragging files (not text or other content)
   291→    if (e.dataTransfer.types.includes('Files')) {
   292→      setIsDraggingVideo(true);
   293→    }
   294→  }, []);
   295→
   296→  const handleDragLeave = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
   297→    e.preventDefault();
   298→    e.stopPropagation();
   299→    // Only set to false if leaving the drop zone (not entering a child)
   300→    if (e.currentTarget === e.target || !e.currentTarget.contains(e.relatedTarget as Node)) {
   301→      setIsDraggingVideo(false);
   302→    }
   303→  }, []);
   304→
   305→  const handleDrop = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
   306→    e.preventDefault();
   307→    e.stopPropagation();
   308→    setIsDraggingVideo(false);
   309→
   310→    const files = e.dataTransfer.files;
   311→    if (files.length > 0) {
   312→      const file = files[0];
   313→      // Check if it's a video file
   314→      if (file.type.startsWith('video/')) {
   315→        // Trigger the file select handler with a synthetic event
   316→        videoUpload.handleFileSelect({ target: { files } } as unknown as React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>);
   317→      }
   318→    }
   319→  }, [videoUpload]);
   320→
   321→  // ==========================================================================
   322→  // RENDER
   323→  // ==========================================================================
   324→
   325→  return (
   326→    <div className="space-y-4">
   327→      {/* Header */}
   328→      {showHeader && (
   329→        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   330→          <h3 className="text-lg font-medium flex items-center gap-2">
   331→            <RotateCcw className="w-5 h-5 text-primary" />
   332→            {headerTitle}
   333→          </h3>
   334→        </div>
   335→      )}
   336→
   337→      {/* Input Images with Frames Slider */}
   338→      {(startImageUrl || endImageUrl) && (
   339→        <div className="@container overflow-hidden">
   340→          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 @[280px]:grid-cols-3">
   341→            {/* Start Image */}
   342→            <div className="relative aspect-video">
   343→              {startImageUrl && (
   344→                <button
   345→                  type="button"
   346→                  onClick={() => startImageShotGenerationId && onNavigateToImage?.(startImageShotGenerationId)}
   347→                  disabled={!onNavigateToImage || !startImageShotGenerationId}
   348→                  className="w-full h-full relative bg-muted/30 rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-border/50 transition-all hover:ring-2 hover:ring-primary/50 hover:scale-[1.02] disabled:hover:ring-0 disabled:hover:scale-100 disabled:cursor-default"
   349→                  title={onNavigateToImage && startImageShotGenerationId ? "View start image" : undefined}
   350→                >
   351→                  <img
   352→                    src={startImageUrl}
   353→                    alt="Start frame"
   354→                    className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full object-cover"
   355→                  />
   356→                  <span className="absolute bottom-0.5 left-0.5 text-[10px] bg-black/60 text-white px-1 rounded">Start</span>
   357→                </button>
   358→              )}
   359→            </div>
   360→
   361→            {/* Frames Slider */}
   362→            <div className="order-last col-span-2 @[280px]:order-none @[280px]:col-span-1 flex items-center gap-2">
   363→              <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col justify-center space-y-1">
   364→                <div className="flex flex-col items-center text-center">
   365→                  <Label className="text-xs font-medium">Frames</Label>
   366→                  <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
   367→                    {settings.numFrames} ({framesToSeconds(settings.numFrames)})
   368→                  </span>
   369→                </div>
   370→                <Slider
   371→                  value={quantizeFrameCount(settings.numFrames, 9)}
   372→                  onValueChange={(value) => handleFrameCountChange(value)}
   373→                  min={9}
   374→                  max={maxFrames}
   375→                  step={4}
   376→                  className="w-full"
   377→                />
   378→              </div>
   379→            </div>
   380→
   381→            {/* End Image */}
   382→            <div className="relative aspect-video">
   383→              {endImageUrl && (
   384→                <button
   385→                  type="button"
   386→                  onClick={() => endImageShotGenerationId && onNavigateToImage?.(endImageShotGenerationId)}
   387→                  disabled={!onNavigateToImage || !endImageShotGenerationId}
   388→                  className="w-full h-full relative bg-muted/30 rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-border/50 transition-all hover:ring-2 hover:ring-primary/50 hover:scale-[1.02] disabled:hover:ring-0 disabled:hover:scale-100 disabled:cursor-default"
   389→                  title={onNavigateToImage && endImageShotGenerationId ? "View end image" : undefined}
   390→                >
   391→                  <img
   392→                    src={endImageUrl}
   393→                    alt="End frame"
   394→                    className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full object-cover"
   395→                  />
   396→                  <span className="absolute bottom-0.5 right-0.5 text-[10px] bg-black/60 text-white px-1 rounded">End</span>
   397→                </button>
   398→              )}
   399→            </div>
   400→          </div>
   401→        </div>
   402→      )}
   403→
   404→      {/* Prompt */}
   405→      <div className="space-y-2">
   406→        <div className="space-y-1">
   407→          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   408→            <Label className="text-xs font-medium">Prompt:</Label>
   409→            {promptField.badgeType === 'enhanced' && (
   410→              <EnhancedPromptBadge
   411→                onClear={promptField.handleClearEnhanced}
   412→                onSetAsDefault={onSaveFieldAsDefault ? () => handleSaveFieldAsDefault('prompt', promptField.displayValue) : undefined}
   413→                isSaving={savingField === 'prompt'}
   414→                basePrompt={basePromptForEnhancement}
   415→              />
   416→            )}
   417→            {promptField.badgeType === 'default' && (
   418→              <span className="text-[10px] bg-primary/15 text-primary px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">
   419→                Default
   420→              </span>
   421→            )}
   422→            {promptField.badgeType === null && promptField.userHasSetPrompt && (
   423→              <FieldDefaultControls
   424→                isUsingDefault={false}
   425→                onUseDefault={promptField.handleClearAll}
   426→                onSetAsDefault={onSaveFieldAsDefault ? () => handleSaveFieldAsDefault('prompt', promptField.displayValue) : undefined}
   427→                isSaving={savingField === 'prompt'}
   428→              />
   429→            )}
   430→          </div>
   431→          <Textarea
   432→            value={promptField.displayValue}
   433→            onChange={(e) => promptField.handleChange(e.target.value)}
   434→            className="h-20 text-sm resize-none"
   435→            placeholder="Describe this segment..."
   436→            clearable
   437→            onClear={promptField.handleClearAll}
   438→            voiceInput
   439→            voiceContext="This is a prompt for a video segment. Describe the motion, action, or visual content you want in this part of the video."
   440→            onVoiceResult={promptField.handleVoiceResult}
   441→          />
   442→        </div>
   443→
   444→        {/* Enhance Prompt Toggle & Make Primary Variant */}
   445→        <div className="flex gap-2">
   446→          {onEnhancePromptChange && (
   447→            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 p-2 bg-muted/30 rounded-lg border flex-1">
   448→              <Switch
   449→                id="enhance-prompt-segment"
   450→                checked={enhancePromptEnabled ?? false}
   451→                onCheckedChange={onEnhancePromptChange}
   452→              />
   453→              <Label htmlFor="enhance-prompt-segment" className="text-sm font-medium cursor-pointer flex-1">
   454→                Enhance Prompt
   455→              </Label>
   456→            </div>
   457→          )}
   458→          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 p-2 bg-muted/30 rounded-lg border flex-1">
   459→            <Switch
   460→              id="make-primary-segment"
   461→              checked={isRegeneration ? settings.makePrimaryVariant : true}
   462→              onCheckedChange={isRegeneration ? (value) => onChange({ makePrimaryVariant: value }) : undefined}
   463→              disabled={!isRegeneration}
   464→            />
   465→            <Label htmlFor="make-primary-segment" className="text-sm font-medium cursor-pointer flex-1">
   466→              Make Primary
   467→            </Label>
   468→          </div>
   469→        </div>
   470→      </div>
   471→
   472→      {/* Advanced Settings */}
   473→      <Collapsible open={showAdvanced} onOpenChange={setShowAdvanced}>
   474→        <CollapsibleTrigger asChild>
   475→          <Button
   476→            variant="ghost"
   477→            size="sm"
   478→            className={`w-full justify-between h-9 text-xs font-medium ${
   479→              showAdvanced
   480→                ? 'bg-muted text-foreground hover:bg-muted rounded-b-none'
   481→                : 'bg-primary/10 text-primary hover:bg-primary/20 hover:text-primary'
   482→            }`}
   483→          >
   484→            <span>Advanced Settings</span>
   485→            <ChevronLeft className={`w-3 h-3 transition-transform ${showAdvanced ? '-rotate-90' : ''}`} />
   486→          </Button>
   487→        </CollapsibleTrigger>
   488→        <CollapsibleContent className={edgeExtendAmount === 6 ? '-mx-6' : '-mx-4'}>
   489→          <div className={`space-y-3 bg-muted/30 border-y border-border/50 ${edgeExtendAmount === 6 ? 'px-6 py-3' : 'px-4 py-3'}`}>
   490→            {/* Before/After Each Prompt */}
   491→            {(shotDefaults?.textBeforePrompts !== undefined || shotDefaults?.textAfterPrompts !== undefined) && (
   492→              <div className="space-y-2">
   493→                <DefaultableTextarea
   494→                  label="Before:"
   495→                  value={settings.textBeforePrompts}
   496→                  defaultValue={shotDefaults?.textBeforePrompts}
   497→                  hasDbOverride={hasOverride?.textBeforePrompts}
   498→                  onChange={(value) => onChange({ textBeforePrompts: value })}
   499→                  onClear={() => onChange({ textBeforePrompts: '' })}
   500→                  onUseDefault={() => onChange({ textBeforePrompts: undefined })}
   501→                  onSetAsDefault={onSaveFieldAsDefault ? (displayValue) => handleSaveFieldAsDefault('textBeforePrompts', displayValue) : undefined}
   502→                  isSavingDefault={savingField === 'textBeforePrompts'}
   503→                  className="min-h-0 h-8 text-xs resize-none py-1.5 overflow-hidden"
   504→                  placeholder="Text to prepend..."
   505→                  voiceInput
   506→                  voiceContext="This is text to prepend before video prompts. Keep it brief - style keywords, quality tags, or consistent elements."
   507→                  onVoiceResult={(result) => {
   508→                    onChange({ textBeforePrompts: result.prompt || result.transcription });
   509→                  }}
   510→                />
   511→                <DefaultableTextarea
   512→                  label="After:"
   513→                  value={settings.textAfterPrompts}
   514→                  defaultValue={shotDefaults?.textAfterPrompts}
   515→                  hasDbOverride={hasOverride?.textAfterPrompts}
   516→                  onChange={(value) => onChange({ textAfterPrompts: value })}
   517→                  onClear={() => onChange({ textAfterPrompts: '' })}
   518→                  onUseDefault={() => onChange({ textAfterPrompts: undefined })}
   519→                  onSetAsDefault={onSaveFieldAsDefault ? (displayValue) => handleSaveFieldAsDefault('textAfterPrompts', displayValue) : undefined}
   520→                  isSavingDefault={savingField === 'textAfterPrompts'}
   521→                  className="min-h-0 h-8 text-xs resize-none py-1.5 overflow-hidden"
   522→                  placeholder="Text to append..."
   523→                  voiceInput
   524→                  voiceContext="This is text to append after video prompts. Keep it brief - style keywords, quality tags, or consistent elements."
   525→                  onVoiceResult={(result) => {
   526→                    onChange({ textAfterPrompts: result.prompt || result.transcription });
   527→                  }}
   528→                />
   529→              </div>
   530→            )}
   531→
   532→            {/* Negative Prompt */}
   533→            <DefaultableTextarea
   534→              label="Negative Prompt:"
   535→              value={settings.negativePrompt}
   536→              defaultValue={shotDefaults?.negativePrompt}
   537→              hasDbOverride={hasOverride?.negativePrompt}
   538→              onChange={(value) => onChange({ negativePrompt: value })}
   539→              onClear={() => onChange({ negativePrompt: '' })}
   540→              onUseDefault={() => onChange({ negativePrompt: undefined })}
   541→              onSetAsDefault={onSaveFieldAsDefault ? (displayValue) => handleSaveFieldAsDefault('negativePrompt', displayValue) : undefined}
   542→              isSavingDefault={savingField === 'negativePrompt'}
   543→              className="h-16 text-xs resize-none"
   544→              placeholder="Things to avoid..."
   545→              voiceInput
   546→              voiceContext="This is a negative prompt - things to AVOID in video generation. List unwanted qualities as a comma-separated list."
   547→              onVoiceResult={(result) => {
   548→                onChange({ negativePrompt: result.prompt || result.transcription });
   549→              }}
   550→              containerClassName="space-y-1.5"
   551→            />
   552→
   553→            {/* Motion Controls */}
   554→            {(() => {
   555→              const isUsingMotionModeDefault = settings.motionMode === undefined && !!shotDefaults?.motionMode;
   556→              const isUsingPhaseConfigDefault = settings.phaseConfig === undefined && !!shotDefaults?.phaseConfig;
   557→              const isUsingLorasDefault = settings.loras === undefined && (shotDefaults?.loras?.length ?? 0) > 0;
   558→              const isUsingMotionDefaults = isUsingMotionModeDefault && isUsingPhaseConfigDefault;
   559→
   560→              return (
   561→                <MotionPresetSelector
   562→                  builtinPreset={builtinPreset}
   563→                  featuredPresetIds={featuredPresetIds}
   564→                  generationTypeMode={generationMode}
   565→                  selectedPhasePresetId={settings.selectedPhasePresetId ?? shotDefaults?.selectedPhasePresetId ?? null}
   566→                  phaseConfig={settings.phaseConfig ?? shotDefaults?.phaseConfig ?? builtinPreset.metadata.phaseConfig}
   567→                  motionMode={settings.motionMode ?? shotDefaults?.motionMode ?? 'basic'}
   568→                  onPresetSelect={handlePhasePresetSelect}
   569→                  onPresetRemove={handlePhasePresetRemove}
   570→                  onModeChange={handleMotionModeChange}
   571→                  onPhaseConfigChange={handlePhaseConfigChange}
   572→                  availableLoras={availableLoras}
   573→                  randomSeed={settings.randomSeed}
   574→                  onRandomSeedChange={handleRandomSeedChange}
   575→                  queryKeyPrefix={queryKeyPrefix}
   576→                  labelSuffix={
   577→                    <FieldDefaultControls
   578→                      isUsingDefault={isUsingMotionDefaults}
   579→                      onUseDefault={() => onChange({ motionMode: undefined, phaseConfig: undefined, selectedPhasePresetId: undefined })}
   580→                      onSetAsDefault={onSaveFieldAsDefault ? async () => {
   581→                        await handleSaveFieldAsDefault('motionMode', settings.motionMode ?? shotDefaults?.motionMode ?? 'basic');
   582→                        await handleSaveFieldAsDefault('phaseConfig', settings.phaseConfig ?? shotDefaults?.phaseConfig);
   583→                        await handleSaveFieldAsDefault('selectedPhasePresetId', settings.selectedPhasePresetId ?? shotDefaults?.selectedPhasePresetId ?? null);
   584→                      } : undefined}
   585→                      isSaving={savingField === 'motionMode' || savingField === 'phaseConfig' || savingField === 'selectedPhasePresetId'}
   586→                    />
   587→                  }
   588→                  renderBasicModeContent={() => (
   589→                    <div className="space-y-3">
   590→                      <div className="relative">
   591→                        <ActiveLoRAsDisplay
   592→                          selectedLoras={effectiveLoras}
   593→                          onRemoveLora={handleRemoveLora}
   594→                          onLoraStrengthChange={handleLoraStrengthChange}
   595→                          availableLoras={availableLoras}
   596→                        />
   597→                        <div className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 z-10">
   598→                          <FieldDefaultControls
   599→                            isUsingDefault={isUsingLorasDefault}
   600→                            onUseDefault={() => onChange({
   601→                              loras: undefined,
   602→                              motionMode: undefined,
   603→                              phaseConfig: undefined,
   604→                              selectedPhasePresetId: undefined,
   605→                            })}
   606→                            onSetAsDefault={onSaveFieldAsDefault ? async () => {
   607→                              await handleSaveFieldAsDefault('loras', effectiveLoras);
   608→                              await handleSaveFieldAsDefault('motionMode', 'basic');
   609→                            } : undefined}
   610→                            isSaving={savingField === 'loras' || savingField === 'motionMode'}
   611→                          />
   612→                        </div>
   613→                      </div>
   614→                      <button
   615→                        onClick={handleAddLoraClick}
   616→                        className="w-full text-sm text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground border border-dashed border-muted-foreground/30 hover:border-muted-foreground/50 rounded-lg py-2 transition-colors"
   617→                      >
   618→                        Add or manage LoRAs
   619→                      </button>
   620→                    </div>
   621→                  )}
   622→                />
   623→              );
   624→            })()}
   625→
   626→            {/* Structure Video Section */}
   627→            {/* Timeline Mode: Loading state when uploading or waiting for props (no existing video) */}
   628→            {isTimelineMode && videoUpload.isVideoLoading && !structureVideoType && (
   629→              <div className="space-y-3 pt-3 border-t border-border/50">
   630→                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
   631→                  <Video className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
   632→                  <span>Structure Video</span>
   633→                  <Loader2 className="w-3 h-3 animate-spin text-primary" />
   634→                </div>
   635→                <VideoPreviewSkeleton message="Loading video..." />
   636→              </div>
   637→            )}
   638→
   639→            {/* Timeline Mode: Add Structure Video (when no video exists and not loading) */}
   640→            {isTimelineMode && !structureVideoType && !videoUpload.isVideoLoading && onAddSegmentStructureVideo && (
   641→              <div
   642→                className="relative space-y-3 pt-3 border-t border-border/50"
   643→                onDragOver={handleDragOver}
   644→                onDragEnter={handleDragEnter}
   645→                onDragLeave={handleDragLeave}
   646→                onDrop={handleDrop}
   647→              >
   648→                {/* Drop overlay - extends slightly beyond form edges */}
   649→                {isDraggingVideo && (
   650→                  <div
   651→                    className="absolute z-20 flex items-center justify-center bg-primary/10 rounded-lg ring-2 ring-primary ring-dashed"
   652→                    style={{
   653→                      top: '-4px',
   654→                      bottom: '-4px',
   655→                      left: '-8px',
   656→                      right: '-8px',
   657→                    }}
   658→                  >
   659→                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-primary font-medium">
   660→                      <Video className="w-4 h-4" />
   661→                      Drop video here
   662→                    </div>
   663→                  </div>
   664→                )}
   665→                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
   666→                  <Video className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
   667→                  <span>Structure Video</span>
   668→                </div>
   669→                <div className="space-y-2">
   670→                  <input
   671→                    ref={videoUpload.addFileInputRef}
   672→                    type="file"
   673→                    accept="video/mp4,video/webm,video/quicktime"
   674→                    onChange={videoUpload.handleFileSelect}
   675→                    disabled={videoUpload.isUploadingVideo}
   676→                    className="hidden"
   677→                    id="segment-structure-video-upload"
   678→                  />
   679→                  <div className="flex gap-2">
   680→                    <Label htmlFor="segment-structure-video-upload" className="m-0 cursor-pointer flex-1">
   681→                      <Button
   682→                        variant="outline"
   683→                        size="sm"
   684→                        disabled={videoUpload.isUploadingVideo}
   685→                        className="w-full"
   686→                        asChild
   687→                      >
   688→                        <span>
   689→                          {videoUpload.isUploadingVideo ? (
   690→                            <>
   691→                              <Loader2 className="w-3 h-3 mr-2 animate-spin" />
   692→                              {Math.round(videoUpload.uploadProgress)}%
   693→                            </>
   694→                          ) : (
   695→                            <>
   696→                              <Video className="w-3 h-3 mr-2" />
   697→                              Upload
   698→                            </>
   699→                          )}
   700→                        </span>
   701→                      </Button>
   702→                    </Label>
   703→                    <Button
   704→                      variant="outline"
   705→                      size="sm"
   706→                      disabled={videoUpload.isUploadingVideo}
   707→                      onClick={() => videoUpload.setShowVideoBrowser(true)}
   708→                      className="flex-1"
   709→                    >
   710→                      <Images className="w-3 h-3 mr-2" />
   711→                      Browse
   712→                    </Button>
   713→                  </div>
   714→                  <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">
   715→                    Drop a video here or click to upload
   716→                  </p>
   717→                </div>
   718→              </div>
   719→            )}
   720→
   721→            {/* Structure Video Overrides - shown when segment has structure video */}
   722→            {structureVideoType && (
   723→              <div
   724→                className="space-y-3 pt-3 border-t border-border/50"
   725→                onDragOver={isTimelineMode ? handleDragOver : undefined}
   726→                onDragEnter={isTimelineMode ? handleDragEnter : undefined}
   727→                onDragLeave={isTimelineMode ? handleDragLeave : undefined}
   728→                onDrop={isTimelineMode ? handleDrop : undefined}
   729→              >
   730→                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
   731→                  <Video className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
   732→                  <span>Structure Video {isTimelineMode ? '' : 'Overrides'}</span>
   733→                  <span className="text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-muted text-muted-foreground/80">
   734→                    {structureVideoType === 'uni3c' ? 'Uni3C' : structureVideoType === 'flow' ? 'Optical Flow' : structureVideoType === 'canny' ? 'Canny' : structureVideoType === 'depth' ? 'Depth' : structureVideoType}
   735→                  </span>
   736→                </div>
   737→
   738→                {/* 3-Frame Preview with Remove button overlay */}
   739→                {structureVideoUrl && structureVideoFrameRange && (
   740→                  <div className="relative">
   741→                    {/* Drop overlay when dragging - extends slightly beyond preview */}
   742→                    {isDraggingVideo && isTimelineMode && (
   743→                      <div

    12→/**
    13→ * Interface for individual travel segment regeneration task parameters
    14→ * This accepts the original segment params and rebuilds them for a standalone task
    15→ */
    16→export interface IndividualTravelSegmentParams {
    17→  project_id: string;
    18→  
    19→  // Parent generation to create variant for (optional - will be created if not provided)
    20→  parent_generation_id?: string;

    37→  start_image_url: string;
    38→  end_image_url?: string;
    39→

    47→

    51→  
    52→  // Overrides - these can override values from originalParams
    53→  base_prompt?: string;
    54→  // AI-enhanced prompt (kept separate from base_prompt, worker prefers this when present)
    55→  enhanced_prompt?: string;
    56→  negative_prompt?: string;
    57→  num_frames?: number;

    82→
    83→// Maximum frames allowed per segment (81-frame limit)
    84→const MAX_SEGMENT_FRAMES = 81;
    85→
    86→/**
    87→ * Validates individual travel segment parameters
    88→ */
    89→function validateIndividualTravelSegmentParams(params: IndividualTravelSegmentParams): void {
    90→  const errors: string[] = [];
    91→
    92→  if (!params.project_id) {
    93→    errors.push("project_id is required");
    94→  }
    95→
    96→  // Either parent_generation_id OR shot_id must be provided
    97→  // (shot_id is used to create a new parent if none exists)
    98→  if (!params.parent_generation_id && !params.shot_id) {
    99→    errors.push("Either parent_generation_id or shot_id is required");
   100→  }
   101→
   102→  if (typeof params.segment_index !== 'number' || params.segment_index < 0) {
   103→    errors.push("segment_index must be a non-negative number");
   104→  }
   105→
   106→  if (!params.start_image_url) {
   107→    errors.push("start_image_url is required");
   108→  }
   109→
   110→  // end_image_url is optional for trailing segments (single-image-to-video mode)
   111→  // When not provided, the GPU worker will generate video from just the start image
   112→
   113→  // Enforce 81-frame limit per segment
   114→  const numFrames = params.num_frames ?? params.originalParams?.num_frames ?? 49;
   115→  if (numFrames > MAX_SEGMENT_FRAMES) {
   116→    errors.push(`num_frames (${numFrames}) exceeds maximum of ${MAX_SEGMENT_FRAMES} frames per segment`);
   117→  }
   118→
   119→  if (errors.length > 0) {
   120→    throw new TaskValidationError(errors.join(", "));
   121→  }
   122→}
   123→
   124→/**
   125→ * Builds the task params for an individual travel segment
   126→ * Matches the exact structure of travel_segment tasks
   127→ */
   128→function buildIndividualTravelSegmentParams(
   129→  params: IndividualTravelSegmentParams,
   130→  finalResolution: string
   131→): Record<string, unknown> {
   132→  const orig = params.originalParams || {};
   133→  const orchDetails = orig.orchestrator_details || {};
   134→  
   135→  // Handle random seed generation
   136→  const baseSeed = orig.seed_to_use || orig.seed || orchDetails.seed_base || 789;
   137→  const finalSeed = params.random_seed 
   138→    ? Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000000) 
   139→    : (params.seed ?? baseSeed);
   140→  
   141→  if (params.random_seed) {
   142→    console.log(`[IndividualTravelSegment] Generated random seed: ${finalSeed}`);
   143→  }
   144→
   145→  // Build additional_loras from params.loras or original
   146→  // If params.loras is explicitly provided (even as empty array), use it - user may have removed all LoRAs
   147→  // Only fall back to original if params.loras is undefined
   148→  const additionalLoras: Record<string, number> = {};
   149→  if (params.loras !== undefined) {
   150→    // User explicitly provided loras (could be empty array to clear them)
   151→    params.loras.forEach(lora => {
   152→      additionalLoras[lora.path] = lora.strength;
   153→    });
   154→    console.log('[IndividualTravelSegment] Using provided loras:', params.loras.length > 0 ? params.loras : '(none - cleared by user)');
   155→  } else if (orig.additional_loras) {
   156→    Object.assign(additionalLoras, orig.additional_loras);
   157→    console.log('[IndividualTravelSegment] Using original additional_loras');
   158→  } else if (orchDetails.additional_loras) {
   159→    Object.assign(additionalLoras, orchDetails.additional_loras);
   160→    console.log('[IndividualTravelSegment] Using orchestrator_details additional_loras');
   161→  }
   162→
   163→  // Determine model settings FIRST (needed for basic mode phase config)
   164→  // Get structure_guidance from new unified format (preferred)
   165→  // Also check params.structure_videos (passed directly from buildTaskParams)
   166→  const structureGuidance = orig.structure_guidance || orchDetails.structure_guidance;
   167→
   168→  // Check for structure videos - look at multiple sources:
   169→  // 1. params.structure_videos - passed directly from UI (new flow via buildTaskParams)
   170→  // 2. structureGuidance.videos - unified format
   171→  // 3. orig/orchDetails.structure_videos - legacy from originalParams
   172→  const hasStructureVideos = !!(
   173→    (params.structure_videos as unknown[] | undefined)?.length > 0 ||
   174→    (structureGuidance?.videos as unknown[] | undefined)?.length > 0 ||
   175→    orchDetails.structure_videos?.length > 0 ||
   176→    orig.structure_videos?.length > 0 ||
   177→    orchDetails.structure_video_path ||
   178→    orig.structure_video_path
   179→  );
   180→
   181→  // Determine if using uni3c mode
   182→  let isUni3c = false;
   183→  if (structureGuidance?.target === 'uni3c') {
   184→    isUni3c = hasStructureVideos;
   185→  } else if (hasStructureVideos && !structureGuidance) {
   186→    // Check params.structure_videos first (from UI), then legacy sources
   187→    const structureType = ((params as Record<string, unknown>).structure_videos as Array<Record<string, unknown>> | undefined)?.[0]?.structure_type ||
   188→      orig.structure_type || orchDetails.structure_type ||
   189→      orchDetails.structure_videos?.[0]?.structure_type || orig.structure_videos?.[0]?.structure_type ||
   190→      orchDetails.structure_video_type || orig.structure_video_type;
   191→    isUni3c = structureType === 'uni3c';
   192→  }
   193→
   194→  // VACE model is no longer supported - always use I2V
   195→  // Structure video type is hardcoded to uni3c, which uses I2V
   196→  const useVaceModel = false;
   197→
   198→  // Determine motion settings
   199→  const motionMode = params.motion_mode ?? orig.motion_mode ?? orchDetails.motion_mode ?? 'basic';
   200→  const isBasicMode = motionMode === 'basic';
   201→  const amountOfMotion = params.amount_of_motion ?? orig.amount_of_motion ?? orchDetails.amount_of_motion ?? 0.5;
   202→  const userLoras = params.loras ?? [];
   203→
   204→  // Phase config is ALWAYS built and sent (matches batch mode behavior)
   205→  // - Basic mode: build from defaults with user LoRAs
   206→  // - Advanced mode: use explicit if provided, otherwise build from defaults
   207→  const explicitPhaseConfig = params.phase_config || orig.phase_config || orchDetails.phase_config;
   208→  const phaseConfig: PhaseConfig = explicitPhaseConfig || buildBasicModePhaseConfig(useVaceModel, amountOfMotion, userLoras);
   209→
   210→  // advanced_mode indicates whether user provided explicit config (true) or we computed it (false)
   211→  const advancedMode = isBasicMode ? false : (params.advanced_mode ?? orig.advanced_mode ?? orchDetails.advanced_mode ?? !!explicitPhaseConfig);
   212→
   213→  // Build lora_multipliers from phase_config (always available now)
   214→  const loraMultipliers = phaseConfig.phases.flatMap((phase) =>
   215→    (phase.loras || []).map((lora) => ({ url: lora.url, multiplier: lora.multiplier }))
   216→  );
   217→  const defaultModelName = useVaceModel
   218→    ? VACE_GENERATION_DEFAULTS.model
   219→    : "wan_2_2_i2v_lightning_baseline_2_2_2";
   220→  const modelName = orig.model_name || orchDetails.model_name || defaultModelName;
   221→  
   222→  // Log model selection for debugging
   223→  console.log('[IndividualTravelSegment] Model selection:', {
   224→    origModelName: orig.model_name,
   225→    orchModelName: orchDetails.model_name,
   226→    hasStructureVideos,
   227→    hasStructureGuidance: !!structureGuidance,
   228→    structureGuidanceTarget: structureGuidance?.target,
   229→    isUni3c,
   230→    useVaceModel,
   231→    defaultModelName,
   232→    finalModelName: modelName,
   233→  });
   234→  // Extract flat fields from phaseConfig (when available) or fallback to orig/orchDetails
   235→  const flowShift = phaseConfig?.flow_shift ?? orig.flow_shift ?? orchDetails.flow_shift ?? 5;
   236→  const sampleSolver = phaseConfig?.sample_solver ?? orig.sample_solver ?? orchDetails.sample_solver ?? "euler";
   237→  const guidanceScale = phaseConfig?.phases?.[0]?.guidance_scale ?? orig.guidance_scale ?? orchDetails.guidance_scale ?? 1;
   238→  const guidance2Scale = phaseConfig?.phases?.[1]?.guidance_scale ?? orig.guidance2_scale ?? orchDetails.guidance2_scale ?? 1;
   239→  const guidancePhases = phaseConfig?.num_phases ?? orig.guidance_phases ?? orchDetails.guidance_phases ?? 2;
   240→  const numInferenceSteps = phaseConfig?.steps_per_phase
   241→    ? phaseConfig.steps_per_phase.reduce((sum: number, steps: number) => sum + steps, 0)
   242→    : (orig.num_inference_steps ?? orchDetails.num_inference_steps ?? 6);
   243→  const modelSwitchPhase = phaseConfig?.model_switch_phase ?? orig.model_switch_phase ?? orchDetails.model_switch_phase ?? 1;
   244→  const switchThreshold = orig.switch_threshold ?? orchDetails.switch_threshold;
   245→  
   246→  // Segment-specific settings (UI overrides take precedence)
   247→  // Clamp to MAX_SEGMENT_FRAMES (81) as a safety measure even if validation passed
   248→  const rawNumFrames = params.num_frames ?? orig.num_frames ?? 49;
   249→  const numFrames = Math.min(rawNumFrames, MAX_SEGMENT_FRAMES);
   250→  
   251→  // CRITICAL: User-input prompts from UI MUST take precedence
   252→  // params.base_prompt is the explicit override from the SegmentCard UI
   253→  const basePrompt = params.base_prompt ?? orig.base_prompt ?? orig.prompt ?? "";
   254→  const negativePrompt = params.negative_prompt ?? orig.negative_prompt ?? "";
   255→  // AI-enhanced prompt (kept separate from base_prompt)
   256→  // When present, worker should prefer this over base_prompt
   257→  const enhancedPrompt = params.enhanced_prompt ?? orig.enhanced_prompt;
   258→
   259→  // [SegmentPromptDebug] Log prompt resolution to verify UI values are being used
   260→  console.log('[IndividualTravelSegment] [SegmentPromptDebug] Prompt resolution:', {
   261→    hasParamsBasePrompt: params.base_prompt !== undefined && params.base_prompt !== null,
   262→    paramsBasePrompt: params.base_prompt?.substring(0, 50),
   263→    hasEnhancedPrompt: !!enhancedPrompt,
   264→    enhancedPromptPreview: enhancedPrompt?.substring(0, 50),
   265→    origBasePrompt: orig.base_prompt?.substring(0, 50),
   266→    origPrompt: orig.prompt?.substring(0, 50),
   267→    finalBasePrompt: basePrompt?.substring(0, 50),
   268→    hasParamsNegativePrompt: params.negative_prompt !== undefined && params.negative_prompt !== null,
   269→    finalNegativePrompt: negativePrompt?.substring(0, 50),
   270→    source: params.base_prompt !== undefined ? 'UI override (user-input)' : 'original params',
   271→  });
   272→  
   273→  // Build input_image_paths_resolved for orchestrator_details
   274→  // Include all input images from the original orchestrator if available
   275→  // For trailing segments (single-image-to-video), end_image_url is undefined
   276→  const inputImages = params.end_image_url
   277→    ? [params.start_image_url, params.end_image_url]
   278→    : [params.start_image_url];
   279→  const allInputImages = orchDetails.input_image_paths_resolved || inputImages;
   280→  
   281→  // Build orchestrator_details to match travel_segment structure exactly
   282→  // IMPORTANT: Remove orchestrator references so this task is billed as a standalone task
   283→  // (otherwise complete_task thinks it's a sub-task and skips billing)
   284→  const { 
   285→    orchestrator_task_id: _removedOrchTaskId, 
   286→    orchestrator_task_id_ref: _removedOrchTaskIdRef,
   287→    run_id: _removedRunId,
   288→    orchestrator_run_id: _removedOrchRunId,
   289→    ...orchDetailsWithoutOrchestratorRefs 
   290→  } = orchDetails;
   291→  
   292→  // Explicitly preserve critical orchestrator fields
   293→  // These are REQUIRED for the GPU worker to handle individual segment generation correctly
   294→  const fpsHelpers = orig.fps_helpers ?? orchDetails.fps_helpers ?? 16;
   295→  const segmentFramesExpanded = orchDetails.segment_frames_expanded;
   296→  const frameOverlapExpanded = orchDetails.frame_overlap_expanded;
   297→  
   298→  // UNIFIED STRUCTURE GUIDANCE FORMAT: Ensure we use the new format
   299→  // Priority:
   300→  // 1. params.structure_videos - passed directly from UI via buildTaskParams (new flow)
   301→  // 2. structure_guidance (new unified format from originalParams)
   302→  // 3. legacy structure_videos from originalParams/orchDetails
   303→  let finalStructureGuidance = structureGuidance;
   304→
   305→  // Check for structure_videos passed directly from UI (highest priority)
   306→  const directStructureVideos = (params as Record<string, unknown>).structure_videos as Array<Record<string, unknown>> | undefined;
   307→  if (!finalStructureGuidance && directStructureVideos?.length > 0) {
   308→    // Build unified format from UI-provided structure_videos
   309→    const firstVideo = directStructureVideos[0];
   310→    const isUni3cDirect = firstVideo.structure_type === 'uni3c';
   311→
   312→    const cleanedVideos = directStructureVideos.map((v: Record<string, unknown>) => ({
   313→      path: v.path,
   314→      start_frame: v.start_frame ?? 0,
   315→      end_frame: v.end_frame ?? null,
   316→      treatment: v.treatment ?? 'adjust',
   317→    }));
   318→
   319→    finalStructureGuidance = {
   320→      target: isUni3cDirect ? 'uni3c' : 'vace',
   321→      videos: cleanedVideos,
   322→      strength: firstVideo.motion_strength ?? 1.0,
   323→    } as Record<string, unknown>;
   324→
   325→    if (isUni3cDirect) {
   326→      finalStructureGuidance.step_window = [
   327→        firstVideo.uni3c_start_percent ?? 0,
   328→        firstVideo.uni3c_end_percent ?? 0.1,
   329→      ];
   330→      finalStructureGuidance.frame_policy = 'fit';
   331→      finalStructureGuidance.zero_empty_frames = true;
   332→    } else {
   333→      const preprocessingMap: Record<string, string> = {
   334→        'flow': 'flow', 'canny': 'canny', 'depth': 'depth', 'raw': 'none',
   335→      };
   336→      finalStructureGuidance.preprocessing = preprocessingMap[firstVideo.structure_type ?? 'flow'] ?? 'flow';
   337→    }
   338→
   339→    console.log('[IndividualTravelSegment] Built structure_guidance from UI params.structure_videos:', {
   340→      target: finalStructureGuidance.target,
   341→      videosCount: cleanedVideos.length,
   342→      firstVideoPath: firstVideo.path?.substring(0, 50),
   343→    });
   344→  }
   345→
   346→  if (!finalStructureGuidance) {
   347→    // Check for legacy separate structure_videos array from originalParams
   348→    const legacyStructureVideos = orchDetails.structure_videos ?? orig.structure_videos;
   349→    if (legacyStructureVideos?.length > 0) {
   350→      // Build unified format from legacy structure_videos
   351→      const firstVideo = legacyStructureVideos[0];
   352→      const isUni3cLegacy = firstVideo.structure_type === 'uni3c';
   353→
   354→      const cleanedVideos = legacyStructureVideos.map((v: Record<string, unknown>) => ({
   355→        path: v.path,
   356→        start_frame: v.start_frame ?? 0,
   357→        end_frame: v.end_frame ?? null,
   358→        treatment: v.treatment ?? 'adjust',
   359→      }));
   360→
   361→      finalStructureGuidance = {
   362→        target: isUni3cLegacy ? 'uni3c' : 'vace',
   363→        videos: cleanedVideos,
   364→        strength: firstVideo.motion_strength ?? 1.0,
   365→      } as Record<string, unknown>;
   366→
   367→      if (isUni3cLegacy) {
   368→        finalStructureGuidance.step_window = [
   369→          firstVideo.uni3c_start_percent ?? 0,
   370→          firstVideo.uni3c_end_percent ?? 1.0,
   371→        ];
   372→        finalStructureGuidance.frame_policy = 'fit';
   373→        finalStructureGuidance.zero_empty_frames = true;
   374→      } else {
   375→        const preprocessingMap: Record<string, string> = {
   376→          'flow': 'flow', 'canny': 'canny', 'depth': 'depth', 'raw': 'none',
   377→        };
   378→        finalStructureGuidance.preprocessing = preprocessingMap[firstVideo.structure_type ?? 'flow'] ?? 'flow';
   379→      }
   380→
   381→      console.log('[IndividualTravelSegment] Converted legacy structure_videos to unified format:', {
   382→        target: finalStructureGuidance.target,
   383→        videosCount: cleanedVideos.length,
   384→      });
   385→    }
   386→  }
   387→  
   388→  // Log the structure guidance data being preserved
   389→  console.log('[IndividualTravelSegment] Preserving orchestrator data:', {
   390→    hasStructureGuidance: !!finalStructureGuidance,
   391→    structureGuidanceTarget: finalStructureGuidance?.target,
   392→    structureGuidanceVideosCount: (finalStructureGuidance?.videos as unknown[] | undefined)?.length ?? 0,
   393→    hasSegmentFramesExpanded: !!segmentFramesExpanded,
   394→    segmentFramesExpandedLength: segmentFramesExpanded?.length ?? 0,
   395→    hasFrameOverlapExpanded: !!frameOverlapExpanded,
   396→    frameOverlapExpandedLength: frameOverlapExpanded?.length ?? 0,
   397→    fpsHelpers,
   398→    segmentIndex: params.segment_index,
   399→  });
   400→
   401→  const orchestratorDetails: Record<string, unknown> = {
   402→    ...orchDetailsWithoutOrchestratorRefs,
   403→    // Common identifier for comparing batch vs individual segment generation
   404→    generation_source: 'individual_segment',
   405→    // Ensure key fields are set
   406→    parsed_resolution_wh: finalResolution,
   407→    input_image_paths_resolved: allInputImages,
   408→    seed_base: finalSeed,
   409→    model_name: modelName,
   410→    flow_shift: flowShift,
   411→    sample_solver: sampleSolver,
   412→    guidance_scale: guidanceScale,
   413→    guidance2_scale: guidance2Scale,
   414→    guidance_phases: guidancePhases,
   415→    num_inference_steps: numInferenceSteps,
   416→    model_switch_phase: modelSwitchPhase,
   417→    additional_loras: additionalLoras,
   418→    advanced_mode: advancedMode,
   419→    motion_mode: params.motion_mode ?? orchDetails.motion_mode ?? 'basic',
   420→    amount_of_motion: amountOfMotion,
   421→    // Add parent_generation_id for variant creation
   422→    parent_generation_id: params.parent_generation_id,
   423→    
   424→    // Explicitly preserve critical fields for position calculation
   425→    fps_helpers: fpsHelpers,
   426→    // Preserve segment frame counts and overlaps for position calculation
   427→    ...(segmentFramesExpanded ? { segment_frames_expanded: segmentFramesExpanded } : {}),
   428→    ...(frameOverlapExpanded ? { frame_overlap_expanded: frameOverlapExpanded } : {}),
   429→    // UNIFIED FORMAT: Only inject structure_guidance (contains videos inside)
   430→    // NO separate structure_videos array - everything is in structure_guidance.videos
   431→    ...(finalStructureGuidance ? { structure_guidance: finalStructureGuidance } : {}),
   432→  };
   433→
   434→  // HARDCODED: SVI (smooth continuations) feature has been removed from UX
   435→  // Always disable SVI - override any inherited value from original orchestrator_details
   436→  orchestratorDetails.use_svi = false;
   437→  delete orchestratorDetails.svi_predecessor_video_url;
   438→  delete orchestratorDetails.svi_strength_1;
   439→  delete orchestratorDetails.svi_strength_2;
   440→  
   441→  // CLEANUP: Remove legacy structure video params that are now replaced by unified structure_guidance
   442→  // These may be inherited from parent orchestrator_details and are no longer needed
   443→  const legacyStructureParams = [
   444→    'structure_type',
   445→    'structure_videos',
   446→    'structure_video_path',
   447→    'structure_video_treatment',
   448→    'structure_video_motion_strength',
   449→    'structure_video_type',
   450→    'structure_canny_intensity',
   451→    'structure_depth_contrast',
   452→    'structure_guidance_video_url',
   453→    'structure_guidance_frame_offset',
   454→    'use_uni3c',
   455→    'uni3c_guide_video',
   456→    'uni3c_strength',
   457→    'uni3c_start_percent',
   458→    'uni3c_end_percent',
   459→    'uni3c_guidance_frame_offset',
   460→  ];
   461→  for (const param of legacyStructureParams) {
   462→    delete orchestratorDetails[param];
   463→  }
   464→
   465→  if (phaseConfig) {
   466→    orchestratorDetails.phase_config = phaseConfig;
   467→  }
   468→  if (switchThreshold !== undefined) {
   469→    orchestratorDetails.switch_threshold = switchThreshold;
   470→  }
   471→  if (loraMultipliers.length > 0) {
   472→    orchestratorDetails.lora_multipliers = loraMultipliers;
   473→  }
   474→
   475→  // Build task params matching travel_segment structure exactly
   476→  const taskParams: Record<string, unknown> = {
   477→    // Core settings matching travel_segment
   478→    flow_shift: flowShift,
   479→    lora_names: orig.lora_names || orchDetails.lora_names || [],
   480→    model_name: modelName,
   481→    project_id: params.project_id,
   482→    shot_id: params.shot_id, // CRITICAL: Include shot_id for "Open Shot" button in TasksPane
   483→    base_prompt: basePrompt,
   484→    // AI-enhanced prompt (kept separate from base_prompt, worker should prefer this when present)
   485→    ...(enhancedPrompt ? { enhanced_prompt: enhancedPrompt } : {}),
   486→    fps_helpers: orig.fps_helpers ?? orchDetails.fps_helpers ?? 16,
   487→    seed_to_use: finalSeed,
   488→    
   489→    // Solver and guidance settings
   490→    cfg_zero_step: orig.cfg_zero_step ?? -1,
   491→    sample_solver: sampleSolver,
   492→    segment_index: params.segment_index,
   493→    guidance_scale: guidanceScale,
   494→    // UNIFIED FORMAT: Include structure_guidance at top level (contains videos inside)
   495→    // NO separate structure_type - worker reads from structure_guidance.target + preprocessing
   496→    ...(finalStructureGuidance ? { structure_guidance: finalStructureGuidance } : {}),
   497→    cfg_star_switch: orig.cfg_star_switch ?? 0,
   498→    guidance2_scale: guidance2Scale,
   499→    guidance_phases: guidancePhases,
   500→    
   501→    // Segment position flags
   502→    // Check params.is_last_segment first (from buildTaskParams trailing segment detection),
   503→    // then fall back to orig.is_last_segment (from originalParams)
   504→    is_last_segment: params.is_last_segment ?? orig.is_last_segment ?? false,
   505→    negative_prompt: negativePrompt,
   506→    is_first_segment: orig.is_first_segment ?? (params.segment_index === 0),
   507→    
   508→    // LoRA settings
   509→    additional_loras: additionalLoras,
   510→    lora_multipliers: loraMultipliers,
   511→    
   512→    // Model settings
   513→    switch_threshold: switchThreshold,
   514→    debug_mode_enabled: orig.debug_mode_enabled ?? orchDetails.debug_mode_enabled ?? false,
   515→    model_switch_phase: modelSwitchPhase,
   516→    num_inference_steps: numInferenceSteps,
   517→    
   518→    // Resolution
   519→    parsed_resolution_wh: finalResolution,
   520→    
   521→    // Frame settings
   522→    num_frames: numFrames,
   523→    
   524→    // Motion settings (UI override)
   525→    amount_of_motion: amountOfMotion,
   526→    
   527→    // The full orchestrator_details object
   528→    orchestrator_details: orchestratorDetails,
   529→    
   530→    // For individual_travel_segment, include parent_generation_id at top level too
   531→    parent_generation_id: params.parent_generation_id,
   532→    child_generation_id: params.child_generation_id,
   533→    // Track lineage for UI - new variant is based on the child generation being regenerated
   534→    // (only set when creating a variant on an existing child)
   535→    ...(params.child_generation_id ? { based_on: params.child_generation_id } : {}),
   536→    
   537→    // Input images at top level for TaskItem image display
   538→    // For trailing segments (single-image-to-video), end_image_url is undefined
   539→    input_image_paths_resolved: inputImages,
   540→    
   541→    // Image tethering IDs - stored at top level so they're on the generation params
   542→    ...(params.start_image_generation_id && { start_image_generation_id: params.start_image_generation_id }),
   543→    ...(params.end_image_generation_id && { end_image_generation_id: params.end_image_generation_id }),
   544→    ...(params.pair_shot_generation_id && { pair_shot_generation_id: params.pair_shot_generation_id }),
   545→    
   546→    // Post-processing
   547→    after_first_post_generation_saturation: orig.after_first_post_generation_saturation ?? 
   548→      orchDetails.after_first_post_generation_saturation ?? 1,
   549→    after_first_post_generation_brightness: orig.after_first_post_generation_brightness ?? 
   550→      orchDetails.after_first_post_generation_brightness ?? 0,
   551→  };
   552→
   553→  // Don't add phase_config at top level - it goes in individual_segment_params (per-segment override)
   554→  // The worker checks individual_segment_params first, then falls back to orchestrator_details
   555→
   556→  // Add generation name if provided
   557→  if (params.generation_name) {
   558→    taskParams.generation_name = params.generation_name;
   559→  }
   560→
   561→  // Add make_primary_variant flag (defaults to true for backward compatibility)
   562→  taskParams.make_primary_variant = params.make_primary_variant ?? true;
   563→
   564→  // Build individual_segment_params - all UI overrides in one place
   565→  // GPU worker should check these first before falling back to top-level values
   566→  const individualSegmentParams: Record<string, unknown> = {
   567→    // Input images for this segment
   568→    // For trailing segments (single-image-to-video), end_image_url is undefined
   569→    input_image_paths_resolved: inputImages,
   570→    start_image_url: params.start_image_url,
   571→    ...(params.end_image_url && { end_image_url: params.end_image_url }),
   572→    
   573→    // Shot generation IDs for image tethering (video follows its source image on timeline)
   574→    ...(params.start_image_generation_id && { start_image_generation_id: params.start_image_generation_id }),
   575→    ...(params.end_image_generation_id && { end_image_generation_id: params.end_image_generation_id }),
   576→    ...(params.pair_shot_generation_id && { pair_shot_generation_id: params.pair_shot_generation_id }),
   577→
   578→    // Primary variant IDs (for "Load Images" feature to restore source images)
   579→    ...(params.start_image_variant_id && { start_image_variant_id: params.start_image_variant_id }),
   580→    ...(params.end_image_variant_id && { end_image_variant_id: params.end_image_variant_id }),
   581→    
   582→    // Prompts
   583→    base_prompt: basePrompt,
   584→    // AI-enhanced prompt (kept separate from base_prompt, worker should prefer this when present)
   585→    ...(enhancedPrompt ? { enhanced_prompt: enhancedPrompt } : {}),
   586→    negative_prompt: negativePrompt,
   587→
   588→    // Frame settings
   589→    num_frames: numFrames,
   590→    
   591→    // Seed settings
   592→    seed_to_use: finalSeed,
   593→    random_seed: params.random_seed ?? false,
   594→    
   595→    // Motion settings
   596→    amount_of_motion: amountOfMotion,
   597→    motion_mode: params.motion_mode ?? orchDetails.motion_mode ?? 'basic',
   598→    advanced_mode: advancedMode,
   599→    
   600→    // LoRA settings
   601→    additional_loras: additionalLoras,
   602→    
   603→    // Post-processing
   604→    after_first_post_generation_saturation: orig.after_first_post_generation_saturation ?? 
   605→      orchDetails.after_first_post_generation_saturation ?? 1,
   606→    after_first_post_generation_brightness: orig.after_first_post_generation_brightness ?? 
   607→      orchDetails.after_first_post_generation_brightness ?? 0,
   608→  };
   609→
   610→  // Always add phase_config
   611→  individualSegmentParams.phase_config = phaseConfig;
   612→
   613→  // Preserve selected_phase_preset_id for UI state restoration
   614→  if (params.selected_phase_preset_id) {
   615→    individualSegmentParams.selected_phase_preset_id = params.selected_phase_preset_id;
   616→  }
   617→
   618→  // Add the individual_segment_params to task params
   619→  taskParams.individual_segment_params = individualSegmentParams;
   620→
   621→  return taskParams;
   622→}
   623→
   624→/**
   625→ * Creates an individual travel segment regeneration task
   626→ * 
   627→ * This creates a standalone task (visible in TasksPane) that regenerates
   628→ * a single segment and creates a variant on the parent generation when complete.
   629→ * The task params match the exact structure of travel_segment for GPU worker compatibility.
   630→ * 
   631→ * If no parent_generation_id is provided but shot_id is, a placeholder parent
   632→ * generation will be created and linked to the shot.
   633→ * 
   634→ * @param params - Individual travel segment parameters
   635→ * @returns Promise resolving to the created task
   636→ */
   637→export async function createIndividualTravelSegmentTask(params: IndividualTravelSegmentParams): Promise<TaskCreationResult> {
   638→  // Detect trailing segment mode (single-image-to-video, no end image)
   639→  const isTrailingSegment = !params.end_image_url;
   640→
   641→  console.log("[TrailingGen] createTask called:", {
   642→    project_id: params.project_id?.substring(0, 8),
   643→    parent_generation_id: params.parent_generation_id?.substring(0, 8),
   644→    shot_id: params.shot_id?.substring(0, 8),
   645→    segment_index: params.segment_index,
   646→    isTrailingSegment,
   647→    hasStartImageUrl: !!params.start_image_url,
   648→    hasEndImageUrl: !!params.end_image_url,
   649→    hasOriginalParams: !!params.originalParams,
   650→  });
   651→
   652→  try {
   653→    // 1. Validate parameters
   654→    validateIndividualTravelSegmentParams(params);
   655→
   656→    // 2. Ensure we have a parent_generation_id (create placeholder if needed)
   657→    let effectiveParentGenerationId = params.parent_generation_id;
   658→    
   659→    if (!effectiveParentGenerationId && params.shot_id) {
   660→      console.log("[IndividualTravelSegment] No parent_generation_id provided, creating placeholder parent");
   661→      
   662→      // Create a placeholder parent generation
   663→      const newParentId = crypto.randomUUID();
   664→      // Build input images array (end_image_url is optional for trailing segments)
   665→      const placeholderInputImages = params.end_image_url
   666→        ? [params.start_image_url, params.end_image_url]
   667→        : [params.start_image_url];
   668→
   669→      const placeholderParams = {
   670→        tool_type: 'travel-between-images',
   671→        created_from: 'individual_segment_first_generation',
   672→        // Include basic orchestrator_details structure so it shows in segment outputs
   673→        orchestrator_details: {
   674→          num_new_segments_to_generate: 1, // Will be updated as more segments are generated
   675→          input_image_paths_resolved: placeholderInputImages,
   676→        },
   677→      };
   678→      
   679→      const { data: newParent, error: parentError } = await supabase
   680→        .from('generations')
   681→        .insert({
   682→          id: newParentId,
   683→          project_id: params.project_id,
   684→          type: 'video',
   685→          is_child: false,
   686→          location: null, // Placeholder - no video yet
   687→          params: placeholderParams,
   688→          created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   689→        })
   690→        .select()
   691→        .single();
   692→      
   693→      if (parentError) {
   694→        console.error("[IndividualTravelSegment] Error creating placeholder parent:", parentError);
   695→        throw new Error(`Failed to create placeholder parent generation: ${parentError.message}`);
   696→      }
   697→      
   698→      console.log("[IndividualTravelSegment] Created placeholder parent:", newParentId);
   699→      effectiveParentGenerationId = newParentId;
   700→      
   701→      // Link the parent to the shot using the RPC
   702→      const { error: linkError } = await supabase.rpc('add_generation_to_shot', {
   703→        p_shot_id: params.shot_id,
   704→        p_generation_id: newParentId,
   705→        p_with_position: false, // Don't add to timeline position
   706→      });
   707→      
   708→      if (linkError) {
   709→        console.error("[IndividualTravelSegment] Error linking parent to shot:", linkError);
   710→        // Don't fail - the generation was created, just not linked
   711→      } else {
   712→        console.log("[IndividualTravelSegment] Linked parent to shot:", params.shot_id);
   713→      }
   714→    }
   715→    
   716→    if (!effectiveParentGenerationId) {
   717→      throw new Error("Could not determine or create parent_generation_id");
   718→    }
   719→
   720→    // 3. Look up existing child to create variant on
   721→    // Priority: pair_shot_generation_id match > child_order match
   722→    // This ensures we regenerate the correct video even if timeline was reordered
   723→    let effectiveChildGenerationId = params.child_generation_id;
   724→    
   725→    console.log("[IndividualTravelSegment] 🔍 CHILD LOOKUP START:", {
   726→      providedChildGenId: params.child_generation_id?.substring(0, 8) || null,
   727→      parentGenId: effectiveParentGenerationId?.substring(0, 8) || null,
   728→      pairShotGenId: params.pair_shot_generation_id?.substring(0, 8) || null,
   729→      segmentIndex: params.segment_index,
   730→      willSkipLookup: !!effectiveChildGenerationId,
   731→    });
   732→    
   733→    if (!effectiveChildGenerationId && effectiveParentGenerationId) {
   734→      // Strategy 1: Look for child with matching pair_shot_generation_id (most accurate)
   735→      // Query the FK column (source of truth with referential integrity)
   736→      if (params.pair_shot_generation_id) {
   737→        console.log("[IndividualTravelSegment] 🔍 Strategy 1: Looking for child by pair_shot_generation_id column...");
   738→        const { data: childByPairId, error: pairIdError } = await supabase
   739→          .from('generations')
   740→          .select('id')
   741→          .eq('parent_generation_id', effectiveParentGenerationId)
   742→          .eq('pair_shot_generation_id', params.pair_shot_generation_id)
   743→          .limit(1)
   744→          .maybeSingle();
   745→
   746→        if (pairIdError) {
   747→          console.log("[IndividualTravelSegment] ⚠️ Strategy 1 query error:", pairIdError.message);
   748→        } else if (childByPairId) {
   749→          effectiveChildGenerationId = childByPairId.id;
   750→          console.log("[IndividualTravelSegment] ✅ Strategy 1 SUCCESS - Found child by pair_shot_generation_id column:", {
   751→            parent_generation_id: effectiveParentGenerationId?.substring(0, 8),
   752→            pair_shot_generation_id: params.pair_shot_generation_id?.substring(0, 8),
   753→            existing_child_id: effectiveChildGenerationId?.substring(0, 8),
   754→          });
   755→        } else {
   756→          console.log("[IndividualTravelSegment] ❌ Strategy 1 MISS - No child found with pair_shot_generation_id:", params.pair_shot_generation_id?.substring(0, 8));
   757→        }
   758→      } else {
   759→        console.log("[IndividualTravelSegment] ⏭️ Strategy 1 SKIPPED - No pair_shot_generation_id provided");
   760→      }
   761→
   762→      // Strategy 2: Fallback to child_order match (legacy ONLY - skip if pair_shot_generation_id was provided)
   763→      // If pair_shot_generation_id was provided but no match found, we want a NEW child for that pair,
   764→      // not a variant on some unrelated video that happens to have the same child_order
   765→      if (!effectiveChildGenerationId && params.segment_index !== undefined && !params.pair_shot_generation_id) {
   766→        console.log("[IndividualTravelSegment] 🔍 Strategy 2: Looking for child by child_order (legacy fallback)...");
   767→        const { data: childByOrder, error: orderError } = await supabase
   768→          .from('generations')
   769→          .select('id')
   770→          .eq('parent_generation_id', effectiveParentGenerationId)
   771→          .eq('child_order', params.segment_index)
   772→          .limit(1)
   773→          .maybeSingle();
   774→
   775→        if (orderError) {
   776→          console.log("[IndividualTravelSegment] ⚠️ Strategy 2 query error:", orderError.message);
   777→        } else if (childByOrder) {
   778→          effectiveChildGenerationId = childByOrder.id;
   779→          console.log("[IndividualTravelSegment] ✅ Strategy 2 SUCCESS - Found child by child_order:", {
   780→            parent_generation_id: effectiveParentGenerationId?.substring(0, 8),
   781→            segment_index: params.segment_index,
   782→            existing_child_id: effectiveChildGenerationId?.substring(0, 8),
   783→          });
   784→        } else {
   785→          console.log("[IndividualTravelSegment] ❌ Strategy 2 MISS - No child found with child_order:", params.segment_index);
   786→        }
   787→      } else if (!effectiveChildGenerationId && params.pair_shot_generation_id) {
   788→        console.log("[IndividualTravelSegment] ⏭️ Strategy 2 SKIPPED - pair_shot_generation_id was provided but no match found, will create NEW child for this pair");
   789→      }
   790→    }
   791→    
   792→    console.log("[IndividualTravelSegment] 🔍 CHILD LOOKUP RESULT:", {
   793→      finalChildGenId: effectiveChildGenerationId?.substring(0, 8) || null,
   794→      willCreateVariant: !!effectiveChildGenerationId,
   795→      willCreateNewChild: !effectiveChildGenerationId,
   796→    });
   797→
   798→    // Update params with the effective IDs
   799→    const paramsWithIds = {
   800→      ...params,
   801→      parent_generation_id: effectiveParentGenerationId,
   802→      child_generation_id: effectiveChildGenerationId,
   803→    };
   804→
   805→    // 4. Resolve project resolution (use original if available)
   806→    const origResolution = params.originalParams?.parsed_resolution_wh ||
   807→                          params.originalParams?.orchestrator_details?.parsed_resolution_wh;
   808→    const { resolution: finalResolution } = await resolveProjectResolution(
   809→      params.project_id,
   810→      origResolution
   811→    );
   812→
   813→    // 5. Build task params matching travel_segment structure
   814→    const taskParams = buildIndividualTravelSegmentParams(paramsWithIds, finalResolution);
   815→
   816→    console.log("[TrailingGen] Built task params:", {
   817→      model_name: taskParams.model_name,
   818→      segment_index: taskParams.segment_index,
   819→      num_frames: taskParams.num_frames,
   820→      seed_to_use: taskParams.seed_to_use,
   821→      parsed_resolution_wh: taskParams.parsed_resolution_wh,
   822→      child_generation_id: taskParams.child_generation_id,
   823→      is_last_segment: taskParams.is_last_segment,
   824→      origResolution,
   825→      finalResolution,
   826→      hasPhaseConfig: !!taskParams.phase_config,
   827→      hasOrchestratorDetails: !!taskParams.orchestrator_details,
   828→      additionalLorasCount: Object.keys(taskParams.additional_loras as Record<string, number> || {}).length,
   829→    });
   830→
   831→    // 6. Create task using unified create-task function
   832→    const result = await createTask({
   833→      project_id: params.project_id,
   834→      task_type: 'individual_travel_segment',
   835→      params: taskParams
   836→    });
   837→
   838→    console.log("[IndividualTravelSegment] Task created successfully:", result);
   839→    return result;
   840→
   841→  } catch (error) {
   842→    handleError(error, { context: 'IndividualTravelSegment', showToast: false });
   843→    throw error;
   844→  }
   845→}
   846→
   847→// TaskValidationError is used internally - import from taskCreation.ts if needed externally
   848→export type { TaskCreationResult } from "../taskCreation";
   849→

    88→function buildStructureGuidance(
    89→  structureVideos: StructureVideoConfigWithMetadata[] | undefined,
    90→  structureVideoConfig: StructureVideoConfig,
    91→  uni3cEndPercent: number | undefined,
    92→  totalFrames: number,
    93→): Record<string, unknown> | null {
    94→  if (structureVideos && structureVideos.length > 0) {
    95→    const firstVideo = structureVideos[0];
    96→    const isUni3c = firstVideo.structure_type === 'uni3c';
    97→

   102→      treatment: video.treatment ?? DEFAULT_VIDEO_STRUCTURE_PARAMS.structure_video_treatment,

   116→        firstVideo.uni3c_end_percent ?? (uni3cEndPercent ?? 1.0),
   117→      ];
   118→      guidance.frame_policy = 'fit';
   119→      guidance.zero_empty_frames = true;

   188→/** Shared Supabase query shape for shot_generations with joined generation data */
   189→const SHOT_GEN_QUERY = `
   190→  id,
   191→  generation_id,
   192→  timeline_frame,
   193→  metadata,
   194→  generation:generations!shot_generations_generation_id_generations_id_fk (

   196→    location,
   197→    type,
   198→    primary_variant_id
   199→  )
   200→`;
   201→
   202→type ShotGenRow = {
   203→  id: string;
   204→  generation_id: string | null;
   205→  timeline_frame: number | null;
   206→  metadata: Record<string, unknown> | null;
   207→  generation: { id: string; location: string | null; type: string | null; primary_variant_id?: string | null } | null;
   208→};
   209→
   210→/** Filter shot_generations to positioned image rows with valid locations */
   211→function filterImageShotGenerations(rows: ShotGenRow[]): ShotGenRow[] {
   212→  return rows.filter(shotGen => {
   213→    const hasValidLocation = shotGen.generation?.location && shotGen.generation.location !== '/placeholder.svg';
   214→    return shotGen.generation &&
   215→           shotGen.timeline_frame != null &&
   216→           !isVideoShotGenerations(shotGen as ShotGenerationsLike) &&
   217→           hasValidLocation;
   218→  });
   219→}

   221→/**
   222→ * Prepare and submit a video generation task.
   223→ * Handles resolution, fresh DB queries, per-pair overrides, model selection,
   224→ * structure guidance, and task creation.
   225→ */
   226→export async function generateVideo(params: GenerateVideoParams): Promise<GenerateVideoResult> {
   227→  const {
   228→    projectId,
   229→    selectedShotId,
   230→    selectedShot,
   231→    queryClient,
   232→    effectiveAspectRatio,
   233→    generationMode,
   234→    promptConfig,
   235→    motionConfig,
   236→    modelConfig,
   237→    structureVideoConfig,
   238→    batchVideoFrames,

   241→    clearAllEnhancedPrompts,
