   243→    stitchConfig,
   244→  } = params;
   245→
   246→  const {
   247→    base_prompt: batchVideoPrompt,
   248→    enhance_prompt: enhancePrompt,
   249→    text_before_prompts: textBeforePrompts,
   250→    text_after_prompts: textAfterPrompts,
   251→    default_negative_prompt: defaultNegativePrompt,
   252→  } = promptConfig;
   253→
   254→  const {
   255→    amount_of_motion: rawAmountOfMotion,
   256→    motion_mode: motionMode,
   257→    advanced_mode: advancedMode,
   258→    phase_config: phaseConfig,
   259→    selected_phase_preset_id: selectedPhasePresetId,
   260→  } = motionConfig;
   261→
   262→  const { seed, random_seed: randomSeed, turbo_mode: turboMode, debug } = modelConfig;
   263→
   264→  // Ensure amountOfMotion has a valid default (destructuring default only applies when absent, not undefined)
   265→  const amountOfMotion = rawAmountOfMotion ?? 50;
   266→
   267→  if (!projectId) {
   268→    toast.error('No project selected. Please select a project first.');
   269→    return { success: false, error: 'No project selected' };
   270→  }
   271→
   272→  // Wait for pending mutations to complete before submitting (prevents stale data race conditions)
   273→  const mutationCache = queryClient.getMutationCache();
   274→  const pendingMutations = mutationCache.getAll().filter(m => m.state.status === 'pending');
   275→  if (pendingMutations.length > 0) {
   276→    await Promise.race([
   277→      Promise.all(pendingMutations.map(m => m.state.promise?.catch(() => {}))),
   278→      new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 1000)),
   279→    ]);
   280→  }
   281→
   282→  // ── Resolution ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   283→  // Priority: shot aspect ratio > project aspect ratio > default
   284→  let resolution: string | undefined;
   285→  if (selectedShot?.aspect_ratio) {
   286→    resolution = ASPECT_RATIO_TO_RESOLUTION[selectedShot.aspect_ratio];
   287→  }
   288→  if (!resolution && effectiveAspectRatio) {
   289→    resolution = ASPECT_RATIO_TO_RESOLUTION[effectiveAspectRatio];
   290→  }
   291→  if (!resolution) {
   292→    resolution = DEFAULT_RESOLUTION;
   293→  }
   294→
   295→  // ── Fetch fresh shot generation data from DB ────────────────────────────
   296→  // Single query used for both image URL extraction and timeline override extraction.
   297→  // Query directly to avoid using stale React Query cache.
   298→  let allShotGenerations: ShotGenRow[];
   299→
   300→  try {
   301→    const { data, error } = await supabase
   302→      .from('shot_generations')
   303→      .select(SHOT_GEN_QUERY)
   304→      .eq('shot_id', selectedShotId)
   305→      .order('timeline_frame', { ascending: true });
   306→
   307→    if (error) {
   308→      handleError(error, { context: 'TaskSubmission', toastTitle: 'Failed to fetch current images' });
   309→      return { success: false, error: 'Failed to fetch shot data' };
   310→    }
   311→
   312→    allShotGenerations = (data || []) as ShotGenRow[];
   313→  } catch (err) {
   314→    handleError(err, { context: 'TaskSubmission', toastTitle: 'Failed to prepare task data' });
   315→    return { success: false, error: 'Failed to prepare task data' };
   316→  }
   317→
   318→  // ── Extract image URLs and IDs from fetched data ────────────────────────
   319→  const filteredForUrls = filterImageShotGenerations(allShotGenerations)
   320→    .sort((a, b) => (a.timeline_frame ?? 0) - (b.timeline_frame ?? 0));
   321→
   322→  const freshImagesWithIds = filteredForUrls.map(shotGen => ({
   323→    location: shotGen.generation?.location,
   324→    generationId: shotGen.generation?.id || shotGen.generation_id,
   325→    primaryVariantId: shotGen.generation?.primary_variant_id || undefined,
   326→    shotGenerationId: shotGen.id,
   327→  })).filter(item => Boolean(item.location));
   328→
   329→  const absoluteImageUrls = freshImagesWithIds
   330→    .map(item => getDisplayUrl(item.location))
   331→    .filter((url): url is string => Boolean(url) && url !== '/placeholder.svg');
   332→
   333→  const filteredImages = freshImagesWithIds.filter(item => {
   334→    const url = getDisplayUrl(item.location);
   335→    return Boolean(url) && url !== '/placeholder.svg';
   336→  });
   337→
   338→  const imageGenerationIds = filteredImages
   339→    .map(item => item.generationId)
   340→    .filter((id): id is string => Boolean(id));
   341→
   342→  // Extract primary variant IDs for future-proofing (stored in task params for Load Images feature)
   343→  const imageVariantIds = filteredImages
   344→    .map(item => item.primaryVariantId)
   345→    .filter((id): id is string => Boolean(id));
   346→
   347→  // Each pair_shot_generation_id is the shot_generations.id of the START image of that pair
   348→  const pairShotGenerationIds = filteredImages
   349→    .slice(0, -1)
   350→    .map(item => item.shotGenerationId)
   351→    .filter((id): id is string => Boolean(id));
   352→
   353→  // ── Per-pair overrides (timeline vs batch) ──────────────────────────────
   354→  let basePrompts: string[];
   355→  let segmentFrames: number[];
   356→  let frameOverlap: number[];
   357→  let negativePrompts: string[];
   358→  let enhancedPromptsArray: string[] = [];
   359→  let pairPhaseConfigsArray: (PhaseConfig | null)[] = [];
   360→  let pairLorasArray: (Array<{ path: string; strength: number }> | null)[] = [];
   361→  let pairMotionSettingsArray: (Record<string, unknown> | null)[] = [];
   362→
   363→  if (generationMode === 'timeline') {
   364→    // ── TIMELINE MODE: Extract per-pair overrides from fetched data ──────
   365→    const pairPrompts: Record<number, { prompt: string; negativePrompt: string }> = {};
   366→    const enhancedPrompts: Record<number, string> = {};
   367→    const pairPhaseConfigsOverrides: Record<number, PhaseConfig> = {};
   368→    const pairLorasOverrides: Record<number, Array<{ path: string; strength: number }>> = {};
   369→    const pairMotionSettingsOverrides: Record<number, Record<string, unknown>> = {};
   370→    const pairNumFramesOverrides: Record<number, number> = {};
   371→
   372→    // Reuse the same data already fetched — filter to match absoluteImageUrls exactly
   373→    const filteredShotGenerations = filterImageShotGenerations(allShotGenerations);
   374→
   375→    // Build sorted positions for frame gap calculation
   376→    const sortedPositions = filteredShotGenerations
   377→      .filter(shotGen => shotGen.timeline_frame != null && shotGen.timeline_frame >= 0)
   378→      .map(shotGen => ({
   379→        id: getGenerationId(shotGen),
   380→        pos: shotGen.timeline_frame!,
   381→      }))
   382→      .sort((a, b) => a.pos - b.pos);
   383→
   384→    // Extract per-pair overrides from filtered data (indexes match actual image pairs)
   385→    for (let i = 0; i < filteredShotGenerations.length - 1; i++) {
   386→      const firstItem = filteredShotGenerations[i];
   387→      const metadata = firstItem.metadata as Record<string, unknown> | null;
   388→      const { overrides, enhancedPrompt, legacyOverrides } = extractPairOverrides(metadata);
   389→
   390→      // Prompts
   391→      if (overrides.prompt || overrides.negativePrompt) {
   392→        pairPrompts[i] = {
   393→          prompt: overrides.prompt || '',
   394→          negativePrompt: overrides.negativePrompt || '',
   395→        };
   396→      }
   397→
   398→      if (enhancedPrompt) {
   399→        enhancedPrompts[i] = enhancedPrompt;
   400→      }
   401→
   402→      // Phase config (ignore stale phase_config when motion_mode is 'basic')
   403→      const pairMotionMode = overrides.motionMode || legacyOverrides?.motion_mode;
   404→      const isBasicMode = pairMotionMode === 'basic';
   405→
   406→      if (!isBasicMode) {
   407→        if (overrides.phaseConfig) {
   408→          pairPhaseConfigsOverrides[i] = overrides.phaseConfig;
   409→        } else if (legacyOverrides?.phase_config) {
   410→          pairPhaseConfigsOverrides[i] = legacyOverrides.phase_config;
   411→        }
   412→      }
   413→
   414→      // LoRAs
   415→      if (overrides.loras && overrides.loras.length > 0) {
   416→        pairLorasOverrides[i] = overrides.loras.map(lora => ({
   417→          path: lora.path,
   418→          strength: lora.strength,
   419→        }));
   420→      } else if (legacyOverrides?.additional_loras && Object.keys(legacyOverrides.additional_loras).length > 0) {
   421→        pairLorasOverrides[i] = Object.entries(legacyOverrides.additional_loras).map(([path, strength]) => ({
   422→          path,
   423→          strength: typeof strength === 'number' ? strength : 1.0,
   424→        }));
   425→      }
   426→
   427→      // Motion and structure settings
   428→      const hasMotionOverrides = overrides.motionMode !== undefined || overrides.amountOfMotion !== undefined;
   429→      const hasStructureOverrides = overrides.structureMotionStrength !== undefined ||
   430→                                    overrides.structureTreatment !== undefined ||
   431→                                    overrides.structureUni3cEndPercent !== undefined;
   432→
   433→      if (hasMotionOverrides || hasStructureOverrides) {
   434→        pairMotionSettingsOverrides[i] = {
   435→          ...(overrides.amountOfMotion !== undefined && { amount_of_motion: overrides.amountOfMotion / 100 }),
   436→          ...(overrides.motionMode !== undefined && { motion_mode: overrides.motionMode }),
   437→          ...(overrides.structureMotionStrength !== undefined && { structure_motion_strength: overrides.structureMotionStrength }),
   438→          ...(overrides.structureTreatment !== undefined && { structure_treatment: overrides.structureTreatment }),
   439→          ...(overrides.structureUni3cEndPercent !== undefined && { uni3c_end_percent: overrides.structureUni3cEndPercent }),
   440→        };
   441→      } else if (legacyOverrides?.amount_of_motion !== undefined || legacyOverrides?.motion_mode !== undefined) {
   442→        pairMotionSettingsOverrides[i] = {
   443→          amount_of_motion: legacyOverrides.amount_of_motion,
   444→          motion_mode: legacyOverrides.motion_mode,
   445→        };
   446→      }
   447→
   448→      // Num frames
   449→      if (overrides.numFrames !== undefined) {
   450→        pairNumFramesOverrides[i] = overrides.numFrames;
   451→      }
   452→    }
   453→
   454→    // Calculate frame gaps from sorted positions
   455→    const frameGaps: number[] = [];
   456→    for (let i = 0; i < sortedPositions.length - 1; i++) {
   457→      frameGaps.push(sortedPositions[i + 1].pos - sortedPositions[i].pos);
   458→    }
   459→
   460→    // Build per-pair arrays from overrides
   461→    basePrompts = frameGaps.length > 0
   462→      ? frameGaps.map((_, index) => {
   463→          // Empty string = backend uses base_prompt (singular)
   464→          const pairPrompt = pairPrompts[index]?.prompt;
   465→          return (pairPrompt && pairPrompt.trim()) ? pairPrompt.trim() : '';
   466→        })
   467→      : [''];
   468→
   469→    segmentFrames = frameGaps.length > 0
   470→      ? frameGaps.map((gap, index) => pairNumFramesOverrides[index] ?? gap)
   471→      : [batchVideoFrames];
   472→
   473→    frameOverlap = frameGaps.length > 0 ? frameGaps.map(() => 10) : [10];
   474→
   475→    negativePrompts = frameGaps.length > 0
   476→      ? frameGaps.map((_, index) => {
   477→          const pairNeg = pairPrompts[index]?.negativePrompt;
   478→          return (pairNeg && pairNeg.trim()) ? pairNeg.trim() : defaultNegativePrompt;
   479→        })
   480→      : [defaultNegativePrompt];
   481→
   482→    enhancedPromptsArray = frameGaps.length > 0
   483→      ? frameGaps.map((_, index) => enhancedPrompts[index] || '')
   484→      : [];
   485→
   486→    pairPhaseConfigsArray = frameGaps.length > 0
   487→      ? frameGaps.map((_, index) => {
   488→          const config = pairPhaseConfigsOverrides[index];
   489→          return config ? stripModeFromPhaseConfig(config) : null;
   490→        })
   491→      : [];
   492→
   493→    pairLorasArray = frameGaps.length > 0
   494→      ? frameGaps.map((_, index) => pairLorasOverrides[index] || null)
   495→      : [];
   496→
   497→    pairMotionSettingsArray = frameGaps.length > 0
   498→      ? frameGaps.map((_, index) => pairMotionSettingsOverrides[index] || null)
   499→      : [];
   500→  } else {
   501→    // ── BATCH MODE: Uniform settings for all pairs ──────────────────────
   502→    const numPairs = Math.max(0, absoluteImageUrls.length - 1);
   503→
   504→    basePrompts = numPairs > 0 ? Array(numPairs).fill('') : [''];
   505→    negativePrompts = numPairs > 0 ? Array(numPairs).fill(defaultNegativePrompt) : [defaultNegativePrompt];
   506→    segmentFrames = numPairs > 0 ? Array(numPairs).fill(batchVideoFrames) : [batchVideoFrames];
   507→    frameOverlap = numPairs > 0 ? Array(numPairs).fill(10) : [10];
   508→    pairPhaseConfigsArray = numPairs > 0 ? Array(numPairs).fill(null) : [];
   509→    pairLorasArray = numPairs > 0 ? Array(numPairs).fill(null) : [];
   510→    pairMotionSettingsArray = numPairs > 0 ? Array(numPairs).fill(null) : [];
   511→    enhancedPromptsArray = numPairs > 0 ? Array(numPairs).fill(null) : [];
   512→  }
   513→
   514→  // ── Model and phase config determination ────────────────────────────────
   515→  let actualModelName: string;
   516→  let effectivePhaseConfig: PhaseConfig;
   517→
   518→  const userLorasForPhaseConfig = (selectedLoras || []).map(l => ({
   519→    path: l.path,
   520→    strength: parseFloat(l.strength?.toString() ?? '0') || 0.0,
   521→    lowNoisePath: (l as { lowNoisePath?: string }).lowNoisePath,
   522→    isMultiStage: (l as { isMultiStage?: boolean }).isMultiStage,
   523→  }));
   524→
   525→  // Use advanced mode only when explicitly enabled, phaseConfig exists, and motionMode isn't 'basic'
   526→  const useAdvancedMode = advancedMode && phaseConfig && motionMode !== 'basic';
   527→
   528→  if (useAdvancedMode) {
   529→    actualModelName = phaseConfig.num_phases === 2
   530→      ? 'wan_2_2_i2v_lightning_baseline_3_3'
   531→      : 'wan_2_2_i2v_lightning_baseline_2_2_2';
   532→    effectivePhaseConfig = phaseConfig;
   533→  } else {
   534→    const basicConfig = buildBasicModePhaseConfig(amountOfMotion, userLorasForPhaseConfig);
   535→    actualModelName = basicConfig.model;
   536→    effectivePhaseConfig = basicConfig.phaseConfig;
   537→  }
   538→
   539→  // Validate phase config consistency before sending
   540→  {
   541→    const phasesLength = effectivePhaseConfig.phases?.length || 0;
   542→    const stepsLength = effectivePhaseConfig.steps_per_phase?.length || 0;
   543→    const numPhases = effectivePhaseConfig.num_phases;
   544→
   545→    if (numPhases !== phasesLength || numPhases !== stepsLength) {
   546→      console.error('[Generation] Inconsistent phase config:', {
   547→        num_phases: numPhases, phases_array_length: phasesLength, steps_array_length: stepsLength,
   548→      });
   549→      toast.error(`Invalid phase configuration: num_phases (${numPhases}) doesn't match arrays (phases: ${phasesLength}, steps: ${stepsLength}). Please reset to defaults.`);
   550→      return { success: false, error: 'Invalid phase configuration' };
   551→    }
   552→  }
   553→
   554→  // Only include enhanced_prompts if there are actual non-empty values
   555→  const hasValidEnhancedPrompts = enhancedPromptsArray.some(p => p && p.trim().length > 0);
   556→
   557→  // ── Build request body ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
   558→  const requestBody: Record<string, unknown> = {
   559→    project_id: projectId,
   560→    shot_id: selectedShot.id,
   561→    image_urls: absoluteImageUrls,
   562→    ...(imageGenerationIds.length > 0 && imageGenerationIds.length === absoluteImageUrls.length
   563→      ? { image_generation_ids: imageGenerationIds }
   564→      : {}),
   565→    ...(imageVariantIds.length > 0 && imageVariantIds.length === absoluteImageUrls.length
   566→      ? { image_variant_ids: imageVariantIds }
   567→      : {}),
   568→    ...(pairShotGenerationIds.length > 0 ? { pair_shot_generation_ids: pairShotGenerationIds } : {}),
   569→    ...(parentGenerationId ? { parent_generation_id: parentGenerationId } : {}),
   570→    base_prompts: basePrompts,
   571→    base_prompt: batchVideoPrompt,
   572→    segment_frames: segmentFrames,
   573→    frame_overlap: frameOverlap,
   574→    negative_prompts: negativePrompts,
   575→    ...(hasValidEnhancedPrompts ? { enhanced_prompts: enhancedPromptsArray } : {}),
   576→    ...(pairPhaseConfigsArray.some(x => x !== null) ? { pair_phase_configs: pairPhaseConfigsArray } : {}),
   577→    ...(pairLorasArray.some(x => x !== null) ? { pair_loras: pairLorasArray } : {}),
   578→    ...(pairMotionSettingsArray.some(x => x !== null) ? { pair_motion_settings: pairMotionSettingsArray } : {}),
   579→    model_name: actualModelName,
   580→    model_type: 'i2v',
   581→    seed,
   582→    debug: debug ?? DEFAULT_STEERABLE_MOTION_SETTINGS.debug,
   583→    show_input_images: DEFAULT_STEERABLE_MOTION_SETTINGS.show_input_images,
   584→    enhance_prompt: enhancePrompt,
   585→    generation_mode: generationMode,
   586→    random_seed: randomSeed,
   587→    turbo_mode: turboMode,
   588→    amount_of_motion: amountOfMotion / 100.0,
   589→    advanced_mode: useAdvancedMode,
   590→    motion_mode: motionMode,
   591→    phase_config: stripModeFromPhaseConfig(effectivePhaseConfig),
   592→    regenerate_anchors: false,
   593→    selected_phase_preset_id: selectedPhasePresetId || undefined,
   594→    generation_name: variantNameParam.trim() || undefined,
   595→    ...(textBeforePrompts ? { text_before_prompts: textBeforePrompts } : {}),
   596→    ...(textAfterPrompts ? { text_after_prompts: textAfterPrompts } : {}),
   597→    independent_segments: true,
   598→    chain_segments: false,
   599→    use_svi: false,
   600→  };
   601→
   602→  // LoRAs are in phase_config for GPU worker processing.
   603→  // Also send as separate loras array for orchestrator_details (segment regeneration).
   604→  if (selectedLoras && selectedLoras.length > 0) {
   605→    requestBody.loras = selectedLoras.map(l => ({
   606→      path: l.path,
   607→      strength: parseFloat(l.strength?.toString() ?? '1') || 1.0,
   608→    }));
   609→  }
   610→
   611→  if (resolution) {
   612→    requestBody.resolution = resolution;
   613→  }
   614→
   615→  if (stitchConfig) {
   616→    requestBody.stitch_config = stitchConfig;
   617→  }
   618→
   619→  // Structure guidance (unified format with videos nested inside)
   620→  const totalFrames = segmentFrames.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
   621→  const structureGuidance = buildStructureGuidance(
   622→    params.structureVideos,
   623→    structureVideoConfig,
   624→    params.uni3cEndPercent,
   625→    totalFrames,
   626→  );
   627→  if (structureGuidance) {
   628→    requestBody.structure_guidance = structureGuidance;
   629→  }
   630→
   631→  // ── Submit task ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   632→  try {
   633→    // Clear stale enhanced prompts when enhance_prompt is disabled
   634→    if (!enhancePrompt) {
   635→      try {
   636→        await clearAllEnhancedPrompts();
   637→      } catch (clearError) {
   638→        handleError(clearError, { context: 'generateVideoService', showToast: false });
   639→      }
   640→    }
   641→
   642→    const result = await createTravelBetweenImagesTask(requestBody as TravelBetweenImagesTaskParams);
   643→    return { success: true, parentGenerationId: result.parentGenerationId };
   644→  } catch (error) {
   645→    const appError = handleError(error, { context: 'generateVideoService', toastTitle: 'Failed to create video generation task' });
   646→    return { success: false, error: appError.message };
   647→  }
   648→}
   649→

    10→/**
    11→ * Parameters for creating an image upscale task
    12→ */
    13→export interface ImageUpscaleTaskParams {
    14→  project_id: string;
    15→  image_url: string; // The source image to upscale
    16→  generation_id?: string | null; // Optional: the generation ID to update with upscaled URL
    17→  source_variant_id?: string; // Optional: track which variant was the source for lineage
    18→  scale_factor?: number; // Default to 2x upscaling
    19→  noise_scale?: number; // Default to 0.1
    20→  output_format?: string; // Default to "jpeg"
    21→  shot_id?: string; // Optional: associate with shot for navigation from TasksPane
    22→}
    23→
    24→/**
    25→ * Validates image upscale task parameters
    26→ * @param params - Parameters to validate
    27→ * @throws TaskValidationError if validation fails
    28→ */
    29→function validateImageUpscaleParams(params: ImageUpscaleTaskParams): void {
    30→  validateRequiredFields(params, ['project_id', 'image_url']);
    31→
    32→  if (params.image_url.trim() === '') {
    33→    throw new TaskValidationError('Image URL cannot be empty', 'image_url');
    34→  }
    35→
    36→  // Validate URL format
    37→  try {
    38→    new URL(params.image_url);
    39→  } catch {
    40→    throw new TaskValidationError('Image URL must be a valid URL', 'image_url');
    41→  }
    42→
    43→  if (params.scale_factor !== undefined && (params.scale_factor < 1 || params.scale_factor > 8)) {
    44→    throw new TaskValidationError('Scale factor must be between 1 and 8', 'scale_factor');
    45→  }
    46→}
    47→
    48→/**
    49→ * Builds the task params for an image-upscale task
    50→ */
    51→function buildImageUpscaleTaskParams(
    52→  params: ImageUpscaleTaskParams
    53→): Record<string, unknown> {
    54→  const taskParams: Record<string, unknown> = {
    55→    image: params.image_url,
    56→    scale_factor: params.scale_factor ?? 2,
    57→    noise_scale: params.noise_scale ?? 0.1,
    58→    output_format: params.output_format ?? "jpeg",
    59→  };
    60→
    61→  // Add generation_id to task params so the worker knows which generation to update
    62→  // Also add based_on for lineage tracking (same as inpaint and other edit tasks)
    63→  // is_primary=true makes the upscaled version the main display variant
    64→  if (params.generation_id) {
    65→    taskParams.generation_id = params.generation_id;
    66→    taskParams.based_on = params.generation_id;
    67→    taskParams.is_primary = true;
    68→  }
    69→
    70→  // Track source variant if enhancing from a specific variant
    71→  if (params.source_variant_id) {
    72→    taskParams.source_variant_id = params.source_variant_id;
    73→  }
    74→
    75→  if (params.shot_id) {
    76→    taskParams.shot_id = params.shot_id;
    77→  }
    78→
    79→  return taskParams;
    80→}
    81→
    82→/**
    83→ * Creates an image upscale task
    84→ * 
    85→ * @param params - Image upscale task parameters
    86→ * @returns Promise resolving to the created task
    87→ */
    88→export async function createImageUpscaleTask(params: ImageUpscaleTaskParams): Promise<TaskCreationResult> {
    89→  console.log("[createImageUpscaleTask] Creating task with params:", params);
    90→
    91→  try {
    92→    // 1. Validate parameters
    93→    validateImageUpscaleParams(params);
    94→
    95→    // 2. Build task params in the image-upscale format
    96→    const taskParams = buildImageUpscaleTaskParams(params);
    97→
    98→    // 3. Create task using unified create-task function
    99→    const result = await createTask({
   100→      project_id: params.project_id,
   101→      task_type: "image-upscale",
   102→      params: taskParams,
   103→    });
   104→
   105→    console.log("[createImageUpscaleTask] Task created successfully:", result);
   106→    return result;
   107→
   108→  } catch (error) {
   109→    handleError(error, { context: 'ImageUpscale', showToast: false });
   110→    throw error;
   111→  }
   112→}

    42→async function fetchTaskContext(
    43→  supabase: any,
    44→  taskId: string
    45→): Promise<TaskContext | null> {
    46→  // Fetch task first
    47→  const { data: task, error } = await supabase

    49→    .select(`id, task_type, project_id, params`)
    50→    .eq("id", taskId)

    52→

    62→      .from("task_types")
    63→      .select("tool_type, category, content_type, variant_type")

   103→ */
   104→export interface CompleteTaskDeps {
   105→  /**
   106→   * Override Supabase client factory for tests.
   107→   */
   108→  createClient?: (supabaseUrl: string, serviceKey: string) => any;
   109→  /**
   110→   * Provide a pre-constructed Supabase client (bypasses createClient).
   111→   */
   112→  supabaseAdmin?: any;
   113→  /**
   114→   * Override auth + ownership utilities for tests.
   115→   */
   116→  authenticateRequest?: typeof authenticateRequest;
   117→  verifyTaskOwnership?: typeof verifyTaskOwnership;
   118→  getTaskUserId?: typeof getTaskUserId;
   119→  /**
   120→   * Override logger implementation for tests.
   121→   */
   122→  LoggerClass?: typeof SystemLogger;
   123→  /**
   124→   * Override env var access for tests.
   125→   */
   126→  env?: { get: (key: string) => string | undefined };
   127→}
   128→
   129→export async function completeTaskHandler(req: Request, deps: CompleteTaskDeps = {}): Promise<Response> {
   130→  if (req.method !== "POST") {
   131→    return new Response("Method not allowed", { status: 405 });
   132→  }
   133→
   134→  // 1) Parse and validate request
   135→  const parseResult = await parseCompleteTaskRequest(req);
   136→  if (!parseResult.success) {
   137→    return parseResult.response;
   138→  }
   139→  const parsedRequest = parseResult.data;
   140→  const taskIdString = parsedRequest.taskId;
   141→
   142→  console.log(`[COMPLETE-TASK] Processing task ${taskIdString} (mode: ${parsedRequest.mode})`);
   143→
   144→  // 2) Get environment variables and create Supabase client
   145→  const env = deps.env ?? Deno.env;
   146→  const serviceKey = env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY");
   147→  const supabaseUrl = env.get("SUPABASE_URL");
   148→  if (!serviceKey || !supabaseUrl) {
   149→    console.error("Missing required environment variables");
   150→    return new Response("Server configuration error", { status: 500 });
   151→  }
   152→  const createClientFn = deps.createClient ?? createClient;
   153→  const supabaseAdmin = deps.supabaseAdmin ?? createClientFn(supabaseUrl, serviceKey);
   154→  
   155→  // Create logger
   156→  const LoggerClass = deps.LoggerClass ?? SystemLogger;
   157→  const logger = new LoggerClass(supabaseAdmin, 'complete-task', taskIdString);
   158→  logger.info("Processing task", { 
   159→    task_id: taskIdString, 
   160→    mode: parsedRequest.mode,
   161→    filename: parsedRequest.filename
   162→  });
   163→
   164→  // 3) Security check: Validate storage path for orchestrator references
   165→  if (parsedRequest.requiresOrchestratorCheck && parsedRequest.storagePath) {
   166→    const securityResult = await validateStoragePathSecurity(
   167→      supabaseAdmin,
   168→      taskIdString,
   169→      parsedRequest.storagePath,
   170→      parsedRequest.storagePathTaskId
   171→    );
   172→    if (!securityResult.allowed) {
   173→      logger.error("Storage path security check failed", { error: securityResult.error });
   174→      await logger.flush();
   175→      return new Response(securityResult.error || "Access denied", { status: 403 });
   176→    }
   177→  }
   178→
   179→  // 4) Authenticate request
   180→  const authenticateFn = deps.authenticateRequest ?? authenticateRequest;
   181→  const auth = await authenticateFn(req, supabaseAdmin, "[COMPLETE-TASK]");
   182→  if (!auth.success) {
   183→    logger.error("Authentication failed", { error: auth.error });
   184→    await logger.flush();
   185→    return new Response(auth.error || "Authentication failed", { status: auth.statusCode || 403 });
   186→  }
   187→
   188→  const isServiceRole = auth.isServiceRole;
   189→  const callerId = auth.userId;
   190→
   191→  try {
   192→    // 5) Verify task ownership if user token
   193→    if (!isServiceRole && callerId) {
   194→      const verifyOwnershipFn = deps.verifyTaskOwnership ?? verifyTaskOwnership;
   195→      const ownershipResult = await verifyOwnershipFn(supabaseAdmin, taskIdString, callerId, "[COMPLETE-TASK]");
   196→      if (!ownershipResult.success) {
   197→        return new Response(ownershipResult.error || "Forbidden", { status: ownershipResult.statusCode || 403 });
   198→      }
   199→    }
   200→
   201→    // 6) MODE 4: Verify referenced file exists
   202→    if (parsedRequest.storagePath) {
   203→      const pathParts = parsedRequest.storagePath.split('/');
   204→      const isMode3Format = pathParts.length >= 4 && pathParts[1] === 'tasks';
   205→
   206→      if (!isMode3Format) {
   207→        const fileCheck = await verifyFileExists(supabaseAdmin, parsedRequest.storagePath);
   208→        if (!fileCheck.exists) {
   209→          return new Response("Referenced file does not exist or is not accessible in storage", { status: 404 });
   210→        }
   211→      }
   212→    }
   213→
   214→    // 7) Determine user ID for storage path
   215→    let userId: string;
   216→    if (isServiceRole) {
   217→      const getTaskUserIdFn = deps.getTaskUserId ?? getTaskUserId;
   218→      const taskUserResult = await getTaskUserIdFn(supabaseAdmin, taskIdString, "[COMPLETE-TASK]");
   219→      if (taskUserResult.error) {
   220→        return new Response(taskUserResult.error, { status: taskUserResult.statusCode || 404 });
   221→      }
   222→      userId = taskUserResult.userId!;
   223→    } else {
   224→      userId = callerId!;
   225→    }
   226→
   227→    // 8) Fetch task context once (used by validation, generation creation, orchestrator check)
   228→    const taskContext = await fetchTaskContext(supabaseAdmin, taskIdString);
   229→    if (!taskContext) {
   230→      logger.error("Failed to fetch task context", { task_id: taskIdString });
   231→      await logger.flush();
   232→      return new Response("Task not found", { status: 404 });
   233→    }
   234→    console.log(`[COMPLETE-TASK] Task type: ${taskContext.task_type}, tool_type: ${taskContext.tool_type}`);
   235→
   236→    // 9) Handle storage operations
   237→    const storageResult = await handleStorageOperations(supabaseAdmin, parsedRequest, userId, isServiceRole);
   238→    const { publicUrl, objectPath, thumbnailUrl } = storageResult;
   239→
   240→    // 10) Validate shot references and update params if needed
   241→    console.log(`[COMPLETE-TASK] Validating shot references for task ${taskIdString}`);
   242→    try {
   243→      let updatedParams = { ...taskContext.params };
   244→      let needsParamsUpdate = false;
   245→
   246→      // Validate and cleanup invalid shot_id references
   247→      const shotValidation = await validateAndCleanupShotId(supabaseAdmin, updatedParams, taskContext.tool_type);
   248→      if (shotValidation.needsUpdate) {
   249→        needsParamsUpdate = true;
   250→        updatedParams = shotValidation.updatedParams;
   251→      }
   252→
   253→      // Add thumbnail URL if available
   254→      if (thumbnailUrl) {
   255→        needsParamsUpdate = true;
   256→        updatedParams = setThumbnailInParams(updatedParams, taskContext.task_type, thumbnailUrl);
   257→      }
   258→
   259→      if (needsParamsUpdate) {
   260→        console.log(`[COMPLETE-TASK] Updating task parameters${thumbnailUrl ? ' with thumbnail_url' : ''}${shotValidation.needsUpdate ? ' with cleaned shot references' : ''}`);
   261→        await supabaseAdmin.from("tasks").update({ params: updatedParams }).eq("id", taskIdString);
   262→        
   263→        // Keep in-memory context in sync so downstream steps (generation creation) use updated params
   264→        taskContext.params = updatedParams;
   265→      }
   266→    } catch (validationError) {
   267→      console.error(`[COMPLETE-TASK] Error during validation:`, validationError);
   268→      // Continue anyway - don't fail task completion due to validation errors
   269→    }
   270→
   271→    // 11) Create generation (if applicable)
   272→    const CREATE_GENERATION_IN_EDGE = Deno.env.get("CREATE_GENERATION_IN_EDGE") !== "false";
   273→    if (CREATE_GENERATION_IN_EDGE) {
   274→      try {
   275→        await createGenerationFromTask(
   276→          supabaseAdmin,
   277→          taskContext.id,
   278→          {
   279→            id: taskContext.id,
   280→            task_type: taskContext.task_type,
   281→            project_id: taskContext.project_id,
   282→            params: taskContext.params,
   283→            tool_type: taskContext.tool_type,
   284→            content_type: taskContext.content_type,
   285→            variant_type: taskContext.variant_type,
   286→            category: taskContext.category,
   287→          },
   288→          publicUrl,
   289→          thumbnailUrl,
   290→          logger
   291→        );
   292→      } catch (genErr: any) {
   293→        const msg = genErr?.message || String(genErr);
   294→        logger.error("Generation creation failed", { error: msg });
   295→        await logger.flush();
   296→        // Preserve atomic semantics: do NOT mark the task Complete if generation creation failed.
   297→        return new Response(`Generation creation failed: ${msg}`, { status: 500 });
   298→      }
   299→    }
   300→
   301→    // 12) Update task to Complete
   302→    console.log(`[COMPLETE-TASK] Updating task ${taskIdString} to Complete status`);
   303→    const { error: dbError } = await supabaseAdmin.from("tasks").update({
   304→      status: "Complete",
   305→      output_location: publicUrl,
   306→      generation_processed_at: new Date().toISOString()
   307→    }).eq("id", taskIdString).eq("status", "In Progress");
   308→
   309→    if (dbError) {
   310→      console.error("[COMPLETE-TASK] Database update error:", dbError);
   311→      await cleanupFile(supabaseAdmin, objectPath);
   312→      return new Response(`Database update failed: ${dbError.message}`, { status: 500 });
   313→    }
   314→
   315→    // 13) Check orchestrator completion (for segment tasks) - uses task context
   316→    try {
   317→      await checkOrchestratorCompletion(
   318→        supabaseAdmin,
   319→        taskIdString,
   320→        taskContext, // Pass context instead of fetching again
   321→        publicUrl,
   322→        thumbnailUrl,
   323→        supabaseUrl,
   324→        serviceKey
   325→      );
   326→    } catch (orchErr) {
   327→      console.error("[COMPLETE-TASK] Error checking orchestrator completion:", orchErr);
   328→    }
   329→
   330→    // 14) Calculate cost (service role only)
   331→    if (isServiceRole) {
   332→      await triggerCostCalculationIfNotSubTask(supabaseAdmin, supabaseUrl, serviceKey, taskIdString);
   333→    }
   334→
   335→    // 15) Return success
   336→    console.log(`[COMPLETE-TASK] Successfully completed task ${taskIdString}`);
   337→    const responseData = {
   338→      success: true,
   339→      public_url: publicUrl,
   340→      thumbnail_url: thumbnailUrl,
   341→      message: "Task completed and file uploaded successfully"
   342→    };
   343→
   344→    logger.info("Task completed successfully", { 
   345→      task_id: taskIdString,
   346→      output_location: publicUrl,
   347→      has_thumbnail: !!thumbnailUrl
   348→    });
   349→    await logger.flush();
   350→
   351→    return new Response(JSON.stringify(responseData), {
   352→      status: 200,
   353→      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" }
   354→    });
   355→
   356→  } catch (error: any) {
   357→    logger.critical("Unexpected error", { 
   358→      task_id: taskIdString, 
   359→      error: error?.message,
   360→      stack: error?.stack?.substring(0, 500)
   361→    });
   362→    console.error("[COMPLETE-TASK] Edge function error:", error);
   363→    await logger.flush();
   364→    return new Response(`Internal error: ${error?.message}`, { status: 500 });
   365→  }
   366→}
   367→
   368→// Some TS envs don't know about import.meta.main; Deno does.
   369→if ((import.meta as any).main) {
   370→  serve((req) => completeTaskHandler(req));
   371→}
   372→
   373→

    19→
    20→/**
    21→ * Reference-related API parameters for image generation tasks.

    31→  reference_mode: ReferenceMode;
    32→}
    33→
    34→/** Default reference params (internal use only - not exported) */

    37→  subject_strength: 0.0,
    38→  subject_description: '',
    39→  in_this_scene: false,
    40→  in_this_scene_strength: 0.0,
    41→  reference_mode: 'style',
    42→};
    43→
    44→/**
    45→ * Hires fix API parameters for image generation tasks.
    46→ * Uses snake_case to match API directly.
    47→ */
    48→export interface HiresFixApiParams {
    49→  /** Number of inference steps (used for single-pass or base pass in two-pass mode) */
    50→  num_inference_steps?: number;
    51→  hires_scale?: number;
    52→  hires_steps?: number;
    53→  hires_denoise?: number;
    54→  /** Lightning LoRA strength for phase 1 (initial generation) */
    55→  lightning_lora_strength_phase_1?: number;
    56→  /** Lightning LoRA strength for phase 2 (hires/refinement pass) */
    57→  lightning_lora_strength_phase_2?: number;
    58→  additional_loras?: Record<string, string>;
    59→}
    60→
    61→/**
    62→ * Filter reference settings based on the selected reference mode.
    63→ * This ensures only relevant settings are passed to the backend based on what mode is active.
    64→ */
    65→function filterReferenceSettingsByMode(
    66→  referenceMode: 'style' | 'subject' | 'style-character' | 'scene' | 'custom' | undefined,
    67→  settings: {
    68→    style_reference_strength?: number;
    69→    subject_strength?: number;
    70→    subject_description?: string;
    71→    in_this_scene?: boolean;

    74→): Partial<typeof settings> {

    60→function validateZImageTurboI2IParams(params: ZImageTurboI2ITaskParams): void {

    81→
    82→  if (params.seed !== undefined && (params.seed < 0 || params.seed > 0x7fffffff)) {
    83→    throw new TaskValidationError('Seed must be a 32-bit positive integer', 'seed');
    84→  }
    85→
    86→  if (params.loras && params.loras.length > 0) {
    87→    params.loras.forEach((lora, index) => {
    88→      if (!lora.path || lora.path.trim() === '') {

    91→      if (lora.scale !== undefined && (lora.scale < 0 || lora.scale > 2)) {
    92→        throw new TaskValidationError(`LoRA ${index + 1}: scale must be between 0 and 2`, `loras[${index}].scale`);
    93→      }
    94→    });
    95→  }
    96→}

Let me chronologically analyze the conversation:

1. **Initial Request**: User asked to implement a plan for adding optimistic task placeholders to all MediaLightbox edit flows. The plan was detailed, covering 6 files.

2. **First Implementation Round**: I read the IncomingTasksContext to understand the API, then read all 6 target files. I added imports and then deployed 6 parallel agents to make the edits to each file:
   - useTaskGeneration.ts (Inpaint + Annotated Edit)
   - useMagicEditMode.ts (Magic Edit)
   - useRepositionTaskCreation.ts (Fill Edges)
   - useImg2ImgMode.ts (Img2Img)
   - useUpscale.ts (Upscale)
   - useVideoEnhance.ts (Video Enhance)

3. **User feedback on naming**: User asked about confusing naming. I flagged `image-upscale` uses hyphen while others use underscores, but confirmed it matches the actual DB task type.

4. **User asked for more coverage**: User wanted all task creation flows covered, specifically mentioning ImageGenerationForm, batch generation, and join clips.

5. **Transition quality issue**: User reported that video regenerate has a clean transition from placeholder to real task, but image ones have a gap (placeholder disappears, then task appears). I investigated and found the key difference: SegmentRegenerateForm does `await queryClient.refetchQueries()` BEFORE `removeIncomingTask()`, while our new code just called `removeIncomingTask()` immediately. I fixed all 6 files to add the refetch-before-remove pattern.

6. **Expanded to more files**: After fixing the transition, I found and added placeholders to:
   - useVideoEditing.ts (edit_video_orchestrator, useMutation pattern)
   - InlineEditVideoView.tsx (edit_video_orchestrator, useMutation pattern)
   - useVideoItemJoinClips.ts (join_clips, try/finally pattern)
   - useJoinClipsGenerate.ts (join_clips, useMutation pattern)
   - ImageGenerationModal.tsx (image_generation, try/finally)
   - ImageGenerationToolPage.tsx (image_generation, try/finally)
   - CharacterAnimatePage.tsx (character_animate, useMutation pattern)

7. **Pre-existing file fix**: Found useJoinSegmentsHandler.ts had `removeIncomingTask` WITHOUT refetching first (in both success and catch paths, not in finally). Fixed it to use the standard finally block with refetch-before-remove.

8. **Current work**: User asked "Anything we can delete or standardised?" - I was investigating:
   - `completeIncomingTask` vs `removeIncomingTask` usage
   - `useTaskStatusCounts` / `baselineCount` / `expectedCount` usage
   - Potential for extracting the repeated refetch-before-remove pattern into a helper

The investigation was cut short by the summary request.

Key patterns established:
- For hooks with try/finally: `addIncomingTask` before try, `removeIncomingTask` in finally after refetch
- For useMutation: `addIncomingTask` in `onMutate`, `removeIncomingTask` in `onSettled` after refetch, using a ref for the ID
- Always: `await refetchQueries(paginatedAll)` + `await refetchQueries(statusCountsAll)` before `removeIncomingTask`

Files that were NOT modified (pre-existing, already had the pattern):
- SegmentRegenerateForm.tsx
- SegmentSlotFormView.tsx
- useFormSubmission.ts
- useGenerateBatch.ts
- useJoinSegmentsHandler.ts (was modified to fix the missing refetch)

The last user message was "Anything we can delete or standardised?" and I had just started investigating three areas of inconsistency:
1. `completeIncomingTask` (used only in useFormSubmission.ts) vs `removeIncomingTask` (everywhere else)
2. `useTaskStatusCounts` imported in 9 files but only used for `baselineCount` in a few
3. The refetch-before-remove pattern duplicated ~18 times could be extracted into a helper

<summary>
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user asked to implement optimistic task placeholders across ALL task creation flows in the app. The initial plan covered 6 MediaLightbox edit hooks, but the user expanded scope to include image generation forms, batch generation, join clips, video editing, and character animation — essentially every place a task is created. The user also wanted clean transitions (no gap between placeholder disappearing and real task appearing) and consistency across all implementations. The final request was to identify anything that could be deleted or standardized.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **IncomingTasksContext**: React context providing `addIncomingTask()`, `removeIncomingTask()`, and `completeIncomingTask()` for optimistic UI placeholders in the TasksPane
   - **Refetch-before-remove pattern**: `await queryClient.refetchQueries()` for both `paginatedAll` and `statusCountsAll` BEFORE calling `removeIncomingTask()` — ensures the real task is in the list before placeholder disappears
   - **Two implementation patterns**: (a) try/finally for regular async handlers, (b) useMutation `onMutate`/`onSettled` with a ref for the task ID
   - **`completeIncomingTask` vs `removeIncomingTask`**: `completeIncomingTask` updates remaining placeholders' baselines (used for multi-prompt batch flows). `removeIncomingTask` is simpler removal (used for single-task flows)
   - **`baselineCount`/`expectedCount`**: Optional fields on IncomingTask for accurate count display when multiple overlapping placeholders exist
   - **queryKeys.tasks.paginatedAll / statusCountsAll**: React Query keys for task list data that must be refetched before removing placeholders

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`src/shared/contexts/IncomingTasksContext.tsx`** (read only)
     - Defines the IncomingTasksContext API: `addIncomingTask`, `removeIncomingTask`, `completeIncomingTask`
     - `addIncomingTask` takes `{ taskType: string, label: string, expectedCount?: number, baselineCount?: number }`, returns string ID
     - Auto-cleanup after 60 seconds for stale tasks

   - **`src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/inpainting/useTaskGeneration.ts`** (modified)
     - Covers Inpaint + Annotated Edit flows

4. Errors and Fixes:
   - **Placeholder-to-task gap**: User reported image edit placeholders disappear then real task appears (not a clean transition). Root cause: our code called `removeIncomingTask` immediately in finally without waiting for task list data to refresh. Fix: added `await queryClient.refetchQueries()` for both `paginatedAll` and `statusCountsAll` BEFORE `removeIncomingTask()` in all 6 initial files plus all subsequent files.
   - **useJoinSegmentsHandler.ts missing refetch**: Pre-existing file had `removeIncomingTask` in both success and catch paths (not in finally) without any refetch. Fixed to use the standard finally block with refetch-before-remove.
   - **No TypeScript errors**: Every round of changes compiled cleanly (`npx tsc --noEmit`).

5. Problem Solving:
   - Identified the clean transition pattern by examining how SegmentRegenerateForm (which worked) differed from our new implementations (which had the gap)
   - Used two different patterns for the two code structures: try/finally for regular async handlers, and onMutate/onSettled with a ref for useMutation-based handlers
   - Comprehensive audit found CharacterAnimatePage.tsx as the last missing task creation site
   - All 18 consumer files now have matching `addIncomingTask`/`removeIncomingTask` pairs
